# 职业与工作

**employer**
英/美 /ɪmˈplɔɪə(r)/
n. 雇主；用人单位
[义] A person or organization that pays people to work for them. 付钱雇人干活的个人或机构（the largest employer in town 城里最大雇主，employer of choice 优选雇主）。与 employee 配对；劳动合同、福利、职场政策语境的核心词。
[例] ① The employer must provide a safe workplace. 雇主须提供安全的工作场所。 ② She changed employers twice in five years. 五年里她换了两次雇主。
[搭] employer of record 名义雇主, largest employer 最大雇主, former employer 前雇主, employer brand 雇主品牌
[联] employee 雇员, employment 就业, hire 雇用, boss 老板, company 公司, salary 薪水, workplace 职场, contract 合同, HR 人力资源, labor 劳动
[记] 可数：an employer。employ 动词（雇用）。employer-employee 关系为劳动法基础

**employee**
英/美 /ɪmˈplɔɪiː/
n. 雇员；员工
[义] A person who is paid to work for someone else. 受雇为他人工作并领取报酬的人（full-time employee 全职员工，government employee 公务员）。与 employer 相对；HR、福利、绩效语境极高频。
[例] ① The company has over two thousand employees worldwide. 该公司全球有两千多名员工。 ② Employees are entitled to paid leave. 雇员享有带薪假期。
[搭] full-time employee 全职员工, employee benefits 员工福利, employee handbook 员工手册, former employee 前员工
[联] employer 雇主, employment 就业, staff 员工, colleague 同事, salary 薪水, workplace 职场, boss 老板, hire 雇用, workforce 劳动力, union 工会
[记] 可数：an employee。employer 与 employee 配对记忆

**employment**
英/美 /ɪmˈplɔɪmənt/
n. 就业；雇用
[义] The state of having paid work; the act of employing people. 有薪工作的状态或雇用行为（find employment 找到工作，employment rate 就业率）。比 job 更正式、偏宏观统计。
[例] ① She found employment in the tech sector after graduation. 毕业后她在科技行业找到了工作。 ② The factory provides employment for hundreds of locals. 这家工厂为数百当地人提供就业。
[搭] find employment 就业, employment contract 雇佣合同, employment rate 就业率, terms of employment 雇用条件, full employment 充分就业
[联] unemployment 失业, employee 雇员, employer 雇主, job 工作, career 职业生涯, hire 雇用, work 工作, labor 劳动, sector 行业
[记] 不可数为主。unemployment 反义。employment 亦指「雇用行为」可数

**workplace**
英/美 /ˈwɜːkpleɪs/
n. 工作场所；职场
[义] A place where people work; the environment and culture of working life. 上班的地方（safe workplace 安全的工作场所），亦泛指职场文化与环境（workplace harassment 职场骚扰，toxic workplace 有毒职场）。
[例] ① The workplace has become more flexible since remote work spread. 远程办公普及后职场更灵活了。 ② Bullying has no place in a healthy workplace. 健康的工作场所不容霸凌。
[搭] safe workplace 安全的工作场所, workplace culture 职场文化, remote workplace 远程办公, return to the workplace 重返办公室
[联] office 办公室, employer 雇主, employee 员工, colleague 同事, harassment 骚扰, safety 安全, remote 远程的, team 团队, policy 政策
[记] 可数：a workplace。work + place 复合词

**office**
英/美 /ˈɒfɪs/
n. 办公室；办事处
[义] A room or building where people work at desks; a local branch of an organization. 室内办公地点（open-plan office 开放式办公室），亦指政府部门/官职（Office of the President 总统办公室）——多义。
[例] ① She commutes to the office three days a week. 她每周三天通勤去办公室。 ② Please submit the form to our regional office. 请将表格交到我们的地区办事处。
[搭] head office 总部, home office 家庭办公室, office hours 办公时间, open-plan office 开放式办公室, back to the office 重返办公室
[联] workplace 职场, desk 办公桌, colleague 同事, meeting 会议, remote 远程, boss 老板, commute 通勤, building 大楼, staff 员工
[记] 可数：an office。office 作「官职」时不可数（hold office 任职）

**boss**
英/美 /bɒs/
n. 老板；上司
v. 对……指手画脚
[义] The person who is in charge of you at work. 工作中的上级（ask the boss 请示老板）。口语极常用；正式场合可用 manager、supervisor。动词 boss around 指「颐指气使」。
[例] ① My boss approved my vacation request. 老板批准了我的休假申请。 ② Stop bossing everyone around. 别对每个人指手画脚。
[搭] boss around 颐指气使, new boss 新上司, ask the boss 请示老板, like a boss 干得漂亮（口语）
[联] manager 经理, supervisor 主管, employer 雇主, employee 员工, colleague 同事, subordinate 下属, leadership 领导力, hire 雇用, promotion 晋升
[记] 可数：a boss。口语；正式写作可用 supervisor/manager。the boss 常指直接上级

**manager**
英/美 /ˈmænɪdʒə(r)/
n. 经理；管理者
[义] A person responsible for controlling or organizing a business, team, or department. 负责团队、部门或业务运营的人（project manager 项目经理，line manager 直线经理）。比 boss 更正式、强调管理职能。
[例] ① The manager scheduled a team meeting for Monday. 经理把团队会议安排在周一。 ② She was promoted to manager last year. 她去年晋升为经理。
[搭] general manager 总经理, project manager 项目经理, line manager 直线经理, middle manager 中层管理者, report to a manager 向经理汇报
[联] boss 老板, supervisor 主管, executive 高管, manage 管理, team 团队, employee 员工, leadership 领导力, department 部门, promotion 晋升
[记] 可数：a manager。manage 动词；management 名词（管理；管理层）

**supervisor**
英/美 /ˈsuːpəvaɪzə(r)/
n. 主管；监督者
[义] A person who watches and directs the work of others. 监督、指导一线员工工作的人（direct supervisor 直属主管）。比 manager 层级可能更低，更强调现场督导；学术场景指论文导师（thesis supervisor）。
[例] ① Talk to your supervisor if the deadline is unrealistic. 若截止日期不现实，请与主管沟通。 ② Her PhD supervisor encouraged her to publish early. 她的博士导师鼓励她尽早发表。
[搭] direct supervisor 直属主管, shift supervisor 轮班主管, thesis supervisor 论文导师, report to a supervisor 向主管汇报
[联] manager 经理, boss 老板, supervise 监督, employee 员工, oversee 监管, mentor 导师, team 团队, work 工作
[记] 可数：a supervisor。supervise 动词。supervisor 可指工厂领班或学业导师

**executive**
英/美 /ɪɡˈzekjətɪv/
n. 高管；执行者
adj. 行政的；高层的
[义] n. A senior manager in a company（chief executive 首席执行官）。adj. 高层决策相关的（executive decision 高管决策，executive suite 高管层）。与 entry-level employee 相对。
[例] ① Executives met to discuss the merger. 高管开会讨论并购。 ② She joined the company as a senior executive. 她以高级管理人员身份加入公司。
[搭] chief executive 首席执行官, executive team 高管团队, executive decision 高管决策, senior executive 资深高管
[联] manager 经理, CEO 首席执行官, leadership 领导力, board 董事会, company 公司, decision 决策, corporate 企业的, promotion 晋升
[记] 可数：an executive。CEO = Chief Executive Officer。executive 亦指政府「行政部门」

**staff**
英/美 /stɑːf/
n. 全体员工；职员
v. 为……配备人员
[义] n. All the people employed by a particular organization. 某机构的全体雇员（medical staff 医护人员，staff meeting 全体员工会）。集合名词，英式谓语可用复数。v. 配备人手（staff a project 为项目配人）。
[例] ① The staff are preparing for the annual review. 全体员工在准备年度考核。 ② The hotel is staffed twenty-four hours a day. 酒店全天有人值班。
[搭] staff meeting 员工会议, staff member 员工, short-staffed 人手不足, medical staff 医护人员
[联] employee 雇员, workforce 劳动力, personnel 人事, colleague 同事, hire 雇用, team 团队, manager 经理, office 办公室
[记] 集合名词；英式 the staff are / 美式 the staff is 均可。staff 不可数表整体；a staff member 指个人

**workforce**
英/美 /ˈwɜːkfɔːs/
n. 劳动力；全体员工
[义] All the people who work in a company, industry, or country. 某公司、行业或国家的劳动人口总和（aging workforce 老龄化劳动力，remote workforce 远程劳动力）。比 staff 更宏观、常涉政策与统计。
[例] ① The workforce has become more diverse in the last decade. 十年来劳动力构成更多元了。 ② Training helps upskill the workforce. 培训有助于提升劳动力技能。
[搭] aging workforce 老龄化劳动力, remote workforce 远程劳动力, workforce development 劳动力发展, join the workforce 进入劳动力市场
[联] staff 员工, employee 雇员, labor 劳动, employment 就业, industry 行业, skill 技能, training 培训, unemployment 失业
[记] 可数/不可数：the workforce。work + force。enter the workforce 指开始工作生涯

**personnel**
英/美 /ˌpɜːsəˈnel/
n. 人事；全体人员
[义] The people who work for an organization; the department that manages employees. 公司全体职员或人事部门（personnel department 人事部，personnel file 人事档案）。正式、行政色彩强。
[例] ① All personnel must complete safety training. 全体人员须完成安全培训。 ② Contact personnel for benefits questions. 福利问题请联系人事。
[搭] personnel department 人事部, personnel file 人事档案, military personnel 军事人员, key personnel 关键人员
[联] HR 人力资源, staff 员工, employee 雇员, manager 经理, hire 雇用, record 档案, department 部门
[记] 不可数表「人事」部门或人员总称；复数 personnel 指「人员」。HR = Human Resources 更现代

**salary**
英/美 /ˈsæləri/
n. 薪水；月薪/年薪
[义] A fixed regular payment, typically paid monthly or annually, for professional or office work. 按月或按年发放的固定薪酬（annual salary 年薪，salary increase 加薪）。与 wage（常按小时/周计）对照。
[例] ① Her salary was negotiated before she accepted the offer. 接受录用前谈好了薪水。 ② The salary is competitive for this role. 这个职位的薪资有竞争力。
[搭] annual salary 年薪, salary increase 加薪, base salary 基本工资, salary negotiation 薪资谈判, starting salary 起薪
[联] wage 工资, paycheck 薪水支票, bonus 奖金, compensation 薪酬, employee 雇员, employer 雇主, raise 加薪, benefit 福利, income 收入
[记] 可数/不可数：a salary / salary。通常按月/年；wage 多按小时/周

**wage**
英/美 /weɪdʒ/
n. 工资；工钱
[义] A fixed regular payment for labor, especially paid weekly or by the hour. 按小时或周支付的劳动报酬（minimum wage 最低工资，living wage 生活工资）。常指蓝领、小时工；salary 多指白领固定薪。
[例] ① Many workers rely on hourly wages to pay rent. 许多工人靠小时工资付房租。 ② The union fought for higher wages. 工会为更高工资而斗争。
[搭] minimum wage 最低工资, hourly wage 时薪, living wage 生活工资, wage increase 涨工资, wage gap 工资差距
[联] salary 薪水, paycheck 工资支票, labor 劳动, worker 工人, union 工会, employee 雇员, earn 挣, income 收入
[记] 常用复数 wages。earn wages 挣工资。minimum wage 为政策高频词

**paycheck**
英/美 /ˈpeɪtʃek/
n. 薪水支票；工资
[义] A check or payment that an employee receives as salary. 员工收到的薪资金额或支票（live paycheck to paycheck 月光族——靠每份工资勉强过到下月发薪）。美式常用；英式 pay cheque。
[例] ① Her paycheck arrives on the last Friday of the month. 她每月最后一个周五发工资。 ② Many families live paycheck to paycheck. 许多家庭月光，靠工资勉强周转。
[搭] live paycheck to paycheck 月光族, direct deposit paycheck 工资直存, paycheck stub 工资明细
[联] salary 薪水, wage 工资, payroll 工资单, bonus 奖金, income 收入, bank 银行, employee 雇员, monthly 每月的
[记] 可数：a paycheck。美式拼写；英式 pay cheque

**bonus**
英/美 /ˈbəʊnəs/
n. 奖金；红利
[义] An extra amount of money given to an employee in addition to their usual pay. 除常规薪酬外的额外奖金（year-end bonus 年终奖，signing bonus 签约奖金）。亦指任何「额外好处」。
[例] ① Employees received a bonus for exceeding sales targets. 员工因超额完成销售目标获得奖金。 ② The job comes with a generous bonus package. 这份工作附带丰厚的奖金方案。
[搭] year-end bonus 年终奖, signing bonus 签约奖金, performance bonus 绩效奖金, bonus pool 奖金池
[联] salary 薪水, compensation 薪酬, incentive 激励, reward 奖励, employee 雇员, promotion 晋升, profit 利润, commission 佣金
[记] 可数：a bonus。bonus 亦可指「意外好处」（a bonus feature 额外功能）

**benefit**
英/美 /ˈbenɪfɪt/
n. 福利；益处
v. 有益于
[义] n. ① 员工福利（health benefits 健康福利，employee benefits 员工福利包）；② 益处。职场语境常指保险、假期、退休金等非现金报酬。
[例] ① The benefits package includes dental and vision care. 福利方案含牙科与眼科。 ② Remote work benefits both employees and employers. 远程办公对雇员和雇主都有好处。
[搭] employee benefits 员工福利, health benefits 健康福利, benefit package 福利方案, fringe benefits 额外福利
[联] compensation 薪酬, salary 薪水, insurance 保险, pension 养老金, employer 雇主, perk 额外待遇, vacation 假期
[记] 可数/不可数。benefits 复数常专指「员工福利」。动词 benefit from

**compensation**
英/美 /ˌkɒmpənˈseɪʃn/
n. 薪酬；补偿
[义] Money and other benefits given to employees; payment for loss or injury. 员工所得报酬的总称（total compensation 总薪酬），亦指赔偿金（workers' compensation 工伤补偿）——多义。
[例] ① The offer includes a competitive compensation package. 录用方案含具竞争力的薪酬包。 ② She received compensation after the workplace injury. 职场受伤后她获得了赔偿。
[搭] compensation package 薪酬方案, total compensation 总薪酬, workers' compensation 工伤赔偿, executive compensation 高管薪酬
[联] salary 薪水, bonus 奖金, benefit 福利, wage 工资, pay 支付, injury 受伤, employee 雇员, negotiate 谈判
[记] 不可数/可数。compensate 动词。total compensation 含薪+奖+福利

**overtime**
英/美 /ˈəʊvətaɪm/
n. 加班；加班时间
adv. 加班地
[义] Time worked in addition to one's normal hours; extra pay for such work. 正常工时之外的劳动（work overtime 加班，overtime pay 加班费）。劳动法与劳资谈判高频词。
[例] ① Nurses often work overtime during flu season. 流感季护士常加班。 ② Overtime must be approved by a supervisor. 加班须主管批准。
[搭] work overtime 加班, overtime pay 加班费, overtime hours 加班时数, approve overtime 批准加班
[联] shift 轮班, wage 工资, salary 薪水, workload 工作量, employee 雇员, labor 劳动, deadline 截止日期, schedule 排班
[记] 不可数。overtime pay 加班费。work overtime 固定搭配

**freelance**
英/美 /ˈfriːlɑːns/
adj. 自由职业的
adv. 以自由职业者身份
v. 做自由职业
n. 自由职业者
[义] Working for different companies on particular assignments rather than as a permanent employee. 不隶属于单一雇主、按项目接活（freelance writer 自由撰稿人，go freelance 转为自由职业）。零工经济核心词之一。
[例] ① She went freelance after ten years in corporate marketing. 在企业营销干了十年后她转自由职业。 ② He freelances for several magazines. 他为多家杂志自由撰稿。
[搭] go freelance 做自由职业, freelance work 自由职业工作, freelance rate 自由职业报价, freelance contract 自由职业合同
[联] contractor 承包商, gig economy 零工经济, self-employed 自雇的, client 客户, project 项目, employee 雇员, remote 远程, flexible 灵活的
[记] 形容词/副词/动词/名词兼用。freelancer 名词（自由职业者）

**contractor**
英/美 /ˈkɒntræktə(r)/
n. 承包商；合同工
[义] A person or company hired to do specific work under a contract, not as a permanent employee. 按合同完成特定工作的个人或公司（independent contractor 独立承包商，government contractor 政府承包商）。与 employee 法律身份不同。
[例] ① The firm hired a contractor to renovate the office. 公司雇承包商翻新办公室。 ② As an independent contractor, she invoices clients monthly. 作为独立承包商，她按月向客户开票。
[搭] independent contractor 独立承包商, government contractor 政府承包商, general contractor 总承包商, contractor agreement 承包协议
[联] freelance 自由职业, contract 合同, employee 雇员, hire 雇用, project 项目, vendor 供应商, self-employed 自雇的
[记] 可数：a contractor。contract 合同；contractor 签合同做事的人/公司

**intern**
英/美 /ɪnˈtɜːn/
n. 实习生
v. 实习
[义] A student or recent graduate who works for a period to gain experience, often with low or no pay. 为积累经验而在公司短期工作的学生或毕业生（summer intern 暑期实习生）。动词 intern at a company 实习。
[例] ① She was an intern at a law firm last summer. 去年夏天她在一家律所实习。 ② The intern shadowed the manager for a month. 实习生跟了经理一个月。
[搭] summer intern 暑期实习生, intern at 在……实习, unpaid intern 无薪实习生, intern program 实习项目
[联] internship 实习期, trainee 培训生, apprentice 学徒, student 学生, experience 经验, mentor 导师, hire 雇用, entry-level 入门级的
[记] 可数：an intern。internship 名词。美式 intern；英式亦用 intern

**internship**
英/美 /ˈɪntɜːnʃɪp/
n. 实习；实习期
[义] A period of work experience for students or graduates in a company or organization. 在公司积累实践经验的阶段性安排（apply for an internship 申请实习，paid internship 带薪实习）。求职简历关键条目。
[例] ① The internship led to a full-time job offer. 实习带来了全职工作录用。 ② She completed a six-month internship in finance. 她完成了为期六个月的金融实习。
[搭] apply for an internship 申请实习, paid internship 带薪实习, summer internship 暑期实习, internship program 实习项目
[联] intern 实习生, trainee 培训生, experience 经验, resume 简历, career 职业生涯, mentor 导师, employer 雇主, graduate 毕业生
[记] 可数：an internship。实习经历用 do/complete an internship

**apprentice**
英/美 /əˈprentɪs/
n. 学徒
v. 当学徒
[义] A person who learns a trade from a skilled employer, usually for a fixed period. 跟师傅学手艺的学徒（apprentice plumber 管道学徒）。偏技工、传统行业；比 intern 更绑「手艺传承」。
[例] ① He started as an apprentice carpenter at sixteen. 他十六岁起当木工学徒。 ② The program pairs apprentices with master craftspeople. 项目把学徒与资深工匠配对。
[搭] apprentice plumber 管道学徒, apprentice program 学徒项目, serve an apprenticeship 当学徒, master and apprentice 师傅与学徒
[联] trainee 培训生, intern 实习生, craft 手艺, trade 行业, skill 技能, mentor 师傅, learn 学习, vocational 职业的
[记] 可数：an apprentice。apprenticeship 名词（学徒期）

**trainee**
英/美 /ˌtreɪˈniː/
n. 培训生；受训者
[义] A person who is being trained for a job. 正在接受岗位培训的人（management trainee 管理培训生，trainee teacher 实习教师）。比 apprentice 更宽，不限手工行业。
[例] ① Trainees attend workshops during their first month. 培训生首月须参加工作坊。 ② She joined the bank as a graduate trainee. 她以毕业生培训生身份入行。
[搭] management trainee 管理培训生, graduate trainee 毕业生培训生, trainee program 培训项目, trainee position 培训岗位
[联] intern 实习生, apprentice 学徒, training 培训, employee 雇员, mentor 导师, skill 技能, onboarding 入职培训
[记] 可数：a trainee。train 动词；training 名词

**interview**
英/美 /ˈɪntəvjuː/
n. 面试；采访
v. 面试；采访
[义] A formal meeting at which someone is asked questions to see if they are suitable for a job. 求职时接受用人单位提问的正式会面（job interview 求职面试，phone interview 电话面试）。媒体义「采访」靠语境区分。
[形] interview / interviewed / interviewed / interviewing
[例] ① She has three interviews next week. 她下周有三场面试。 ② The panel interviewed twelve candidates. 评审小组面试了十二名候选人。
[搭] job interview 求职面试, phone interview 电话面试, interview process 面试流程, interview questions 面试问题
[联] applicant 申请人, candidate 候选人, resume 简历, hire 雇用, employer 雇主, offer 录用通知, question 问题, career 职业生涯
[记] 可数：an interview。interviewer 面试官；interviewee 被面试者

**applicant**
英/美 /ˈæplɪkənt/
n. 申请人
[义] A person who formally applies for a job, place, or benefit. 正式提交求职申请的人（job applicant 求职者，qualified applicant 合格申请人）。招聘流程中的身份标签。
[例] ① The company received over two hundred applicants. 公司收到两百多份申请。 ② Applicants must submit a cover letter. 申请人须提交求职信。
[搭] job applicant 求职者, qualified applicant 合格申请人, applicant pool 申请人池, successful applicant 成功录用者
[联] application 申请, candidate 候选人, interview 面试, resume 简历, hire 雇用, employer 雇主, apply 申请, recruit 招聘
[记] 可数：an applicant。apply 动词；application 名词

**candidate**
英/美 /ˈkændɪdət/
n. 候选人；求职者
[义] A person who is being considered for a job or position. 进入筛选、面试阶段的人选（shortlisted candidate 入围候选人）。亦指选举候选人——语境区分。
[例] ① We interviewed five candidates for the role. 我们为该职位面试了五名候选人。 ② The best candidate had strong teamwork skills. 最佳人选团队合作能力强。
[搭] job candidate 求职候选人, shortlisted candidate 入围者, ideal candidate 理想人选, candidate for 某职位的候选人
[联] applicant 申请人, interview 面试, hire 雇用, recruit 招聘, employee 雇员, qualify 具备资格, resume 简历, select 选拔
[记] 可数：a candidate。candidacy 名词（候选资格）。选举与求职语境均高频

**recruit**
英/美 /rɪˈkruːt/
v. 招聘；招募
n. 新员工
[义] v. To find new people to join a company or organization. 为岗位寻找并雇用新人（recruit talent 招募人才）。n. 新招进来的人（new recruits 新员工）。
[形] recruit / recruited / recruited / recruiting
[例] ① The HR team is recruiting for three positions. 人事团队正在为三个职位招聘。 ② New recruits attend onboarding next Monday. 新员工下周一参加入职培训。
[搭] recruit talent 招募人才, new recruit 新员工, recruit for 为……招聘, recruitment drive 招聘活动
[联] recruitment 招聘, recruiter 招聘人员, hire 雇用, employer 雇主, candidate 候选人, HR 人力资源, talent 人才, interview 面试
[记] 及物动词 recruit sb.。recruitment 名词；recruiter 招聘官

**recruitment**
英/美 /rɪˈkruːtmənt/
n. 招聘；招募
[义] The process of finding and hiring new employees. 寻找、筛选并雇用员工的整个过程（recruitment process 招聘流程，campus recruitment 校园招聘）。
[例] ① Recruitment slowed during the economic downturn. 经济下行期招聘放缓。 ② She works in campus recruitment for a tech company. 她在一家科技公司做校园招聘。
[搭] recruitment process 招聘流程, campus recruitment 校园招聘, recruitment agency 招聘机构, online recruitment 网络招聘
[联] recruit 招聘, recruiter 招聘人员, hire 雇用, HR 人力资源, candidate 候选人, interview 面试, talent 人才
[记] 不可数为主。recruit 动词/名词

**recruiter**
英/美 /rɪˈkruːtə(r)/
n. 招聘人员；猎头
[义] A person whose job is to find and attract candidates for employment. 专职物色、联系求职者的人（in-house recruiter 内部招聘，external recruiter 外部猎头）。求职者常通过 LinkedIn 与 recruiter 联系。
[例] ① A recruiter reached out about a manager role. 一位招聘顾问联系她谈经理岗位。 ② Recruiters screen resumes before interviews. 招聘人员在面试前筛选简历。
[搭] in-house recruiter 内部招聘, external recruiter 外部猎头, recruiter call 猎头电话, tech recruiter 科技行业招聘
[联] recruitment 招聘, recruit 招募, headhunter 猎头, HR 人力资源, candidate 候选人, interview 面试, LinkedIn 领英
[记] 可数：a recruiter。与 headhunter 近义，headhunter 更偏高管猎聘

**vacancy**
英/美 /ˈveɪkənsi/
n. 空缺；职位空缺
[义] A job that is available for someone to do; an empty position. 可供应聘的职位空额（job vacancy 职位空缺，fill a vacancy 填补空缺）。英式常见 Vacancies 橱窗招聘告示。
[例] ① There is a vacancy for a part-time assistant. 有一个兼职助理空缺。 ② The company posted three new vacancies online. 公司在网上发布了三个新职位。
[搭] job vacancy 职位空缺, fill a vacancy 填补空缺, vacancy rate 空缺率, post a vacancy 发布招聘
[联] opening 空额, position 职位, hire 雇用, recruit 招聘, apply 申请, employer 雇主, job 工作, staff 员工
[记] 可数：a vacancy。vacant 形容词（空缺的）。No vacancies 常见于招聘窗

**onboarding**
英/美 /ˈɒnbɔːdɪŋ/
n. 入职培训；入职引导
[义] The process of integrating a new employee into an organization. 帮助新员工熟悉公司、岗位与文化的流程（onboarding process 入职流程，onboarding buddy 入职伙伴）。HR 现代术语。
[例] ① Onboarding includes IT setup and policy training. 入职培训含 IT 配置与制度学习。 ② A good onboarding experience reduces early turnover. 良好的入职体验能降低早期离职率。
[搭] onboarding process 入职流程, onboarding buddy 入职伙伴, employee onboarding 员工入职, onboarding checklist 入职清单
[联] orientation 入职介绍, training 培训, new hire 新员工, HR 人力资源, employee 雇员, mentor 导师, probation 试用期
[记] 不可数为主。onboard 动词（使入职）。与 orientation 近义，onboarding 更系统、更长

**probation**
英/美 /prəˈbeɪʃn/
n. 试用期；缓刑
[义] A period at the start of a new job when someone is watched to see if they are suitable. 新员工上岗后的考察期（on probation 试用中，pass probation 通过试用）。法律义「缓刑」——另一义。
[例] ① She is on probation for the first three months. 她头三个月处于试用期。 ② He passed probation and became a permanent employee. 他通过试用，成为正式雇员。
[搭] on probation 试用中, pass probation 通过试用, probation period 试用期, probationary period 试用期
[联] onboarding 入职, hire 雇用, employee 雇员, terminate 解雇, performance 绩效, review 考核, contract 合同
[记] 不可数/可数。probationary 形容词（probationary period 试用期）。法律缓刑义靠语境区分

**qualification**
英/美 /ˌkwɒlɪfɪˈkeɪʃn/
n. 资格；学历；技能条件
[义] An exam pass, certificate, or skill that makes you suitable for a job. 胜任岗位所需的学历、证书或能力（academic qualifications 学历，minimum qualifications 最低资格要求）。
[例] ① The role requires professional qualifications in accounting. 该职位要求具备会计专业资格。 ② What qualifications do you need to apply? 申请需要什么资历？
[搭] academic qualifications 学历, professional qualification 专业资格, minimum qualifications 最低要求, meet the qualifications 符合资格
[联] credential 资历, certification 认证, degree 学位, skill 技能, resume 简历, candidate 候选人, requirement 要求, training 培训
[记] 可数：a qualification。qualify 动词（使具备资格）

**credential**
英/美 /krəˈdenʃl/
n. 资历；资格证书（常用复数）
[义] The abilities and experience that make someone suitable for a job; official documents proving identity or status. 证明胜任力的资历与证书（credentials 履历资历，verify credentials 核实资格）。网络安全语境 login credentials 指「登录凭据」——另一义。
[例] ① The candidate's credentials impressed the hiring panel. 候选人资历给招聘小组留下深刻印象。 ② Please bring your professional credentials to the interview. 请带专业证书参加面试。
[搭] professional credentials 专业资历, academic credentials 学历, verify credentials 核实资格, credentials committee 资格审查委员会
[联] qualification 资格, certification 认证, resume 简历, experience 经验, degree 学位, license 执照, interview 面试
[记] 常用复数 credentials。credential 单数较少。login credentials 为 IT 义

**certification**
英/美 /ˌsɜːtɪfɪˈkeɪʃn/
n. 认证；证书
[义] An official document or process that proves someone has the skills or knowledge for a job. 证明具备某技能的官方认证（professional certification 职业认证，get certification 取得证书）。比 qualification 更强调考试或机构背书。
[例] ① The IT role requires cloud certification. 该 IT 岗位要求云认证。 ② She earned her teaching certification last year. 她去年取得了教师资格证。
[搭] professional certification 职业认证, certification exam 认证考试, obtain certification 获得认证, industry certification 行业认证
[联] qualification 资格, credential 资历, license 执照, training 培训, exam 考试, skill 技能, certificate 证书
[记] 可数/不可数。certify 动词。certified 形容词（certified accountant 注册会计师）

**layoff**
英/美 /ˈleɪɒf/
n. 裁员；解雇
[义] An act of making employees leave their jobs because there is no more work for them. 因业务收缩等原因让员工离职（mass layoffs 大规模裁员，face layoffs 面临裁员）。美式常用；英式 redundancy 亦常见。
[例] ① The company announced layoffs affecting two hundred staff. 公司宣布裁员，波及两百名员工。 ② She survived three rounds of layoffs. 她躲过了三轮裁员。
[搭] mass layoff 大规模裁员, face layoffs 面临裁员, layoff notice 裁员通知, temporary layoff 临时解雇
[联] redundancy 冗余裁员, unemployment 失业, fire 解雇, terminate 终止雇佣, employer 雇主, workforce 劳动力, severance 遣散费
[记] 可数：a layoff。动词词组 lay off sb.（裁员；停止做某事——另一义）

**furlough**
英/美 /ˈfɜːləʊ/
n. 临时休假；停职留薪/无薪假
v. 使临时休假
[义] A temporary leave from work, often without pay, especially when a company cannot afford to keep staff. 公司因故让员工暂时离岗（during the pandemic many were furloughed 疫情期间许多人被休假）。疫情后进入日常新闻词汇。
[例] ① Hundreds of employees were furloughed when flights dropped. 航班锐减时数百员工被停职休假。 ② The furlough lasted three months before recalls began. 休假持续三个月后才开始召回。
[搭] be furloughed 被停职休假, furlough scheme 休假补贴计划, return from furlough 休假返岗
[联] layoff 裁员, unemployment 失业, temporary 临时的, employee 雇员, employer 雇主, pandemic 疫情, salary 薪水
[记] 可数/不可数。英式/美式新闻高频。与 layoff 不同：furlough 常暗示可能返岗

**redundancy**
英 /rɪˈdʌndənsi/
n. 裁员；冗余
[义] British: the situation when someone loses their job because they are no longer needed. 英式：因岗位不再需要而被裁（made redundant 被裁，redundancy pay 遣散费）。美式更常说 layoff。
[例] ① He was made redundant when the branch closed. 分行关闭时他被裁了。 ② Redundancy consultations must follow legal rules. 裁员磋商须依法进行。
[搭] made redundant 被裁, redundancy pay 遣散费, face redundancy 面临裁员, redundancy package 裁员补偿包
[联] layoff 裁员, severance 遣散费, unemployment 失业, employer 雇主, employee 雇员, workforce 劳动力
[记] 英式职场核心词。redundant 形容词（多余的；被裁的）。美式用 layoff

**severance**
英/美 /ˈsevərəns/
n. 遣散费；切断
[义] Money paid by an employer to a worker when their employment is ended. 解雇时雇主支付的补偿金（severance package 遣散方案，severance pay 遣散工资）。正式、法律色彩。
[例] ① She negotiated a generous severance package. 她谈妥了丰厚的遣散方案。 ② Severance terms were outlined in the contract. 遣散条款在合同中列明。
[搭] severance pay 遣散费, severance package 遣散方案, severance agreement 遣散协议
[联] layoff 裁员, redundancy 裁员, terminate 解雇, compensation 补偿, contract 合同, employer 雇主, negotiate 谈判
[记] 不可数/可数。severance pay 固定搭配。sever 动词（切断）

**termination**
英/美 /ˌtɜːmɪˈneɪʃn/
n. 终止；解雇
[义] The act of ending someone's employment; the end of a contract. 雇佣关系或合同的结束（termination of employment 解雇，wrongful termination 不当解雇）。HR 与法律正式用语。
[例] ① Termination must follow company policy and local law. 解雇须符合公司制度与当地法律。 ② He received notice of termination effective Friday. 他收到通知，雇佣关系周五终止。
[搭] termination of employment 终止雇佣, wrongful termination 不当解雇, immediate termination 立即解雇, termination notice 解雇通知
[联] fire 解雇, layoff 裁员, resign 辞职, contract 合同, severance 遣散费, employer 雇主, HR 人力资源
[记] 可数/不可数。terminate 动词。termination 亦指合同终止

**demotion**
英/美 /diːˈməʊʃn/
n. 降职
[义] A move to a lower rank or less important position in a company. 职级或岗位被下调（suffer a demotion 遭降职）。与 promotion 相对。
[例] ① After the error, he faced demotion to a junior role. 失误后他面临降职到初级岗位。 ② Demotion is rare without a formal review. 未经正式考核很少降职。
[搭] suffer a demotion 遭降职, demotion to 降职为, face demotion 面临降职
[联] promotion 晋升, transfer 调动, manager 经理, employee 雇员, performance 绩效, role 职位, boss 老板
[记] 可数：a demotion。demote 动词。与 promotion 成对

**transfer**
英/美 /trænsˈfɜː(r)/
n. 调动；转岗
v. 调动；转移
[义] n. A move to a different job, department, or location within the same organization. 在同一组织内换岗、换部门或换地点（internal transfer 内部调动）。v. 调动、转移。
[形] transfer / transferred / transferred / transferring
[例] ① She requested a transfer to the Shanghai office. 她申请调动到上海办事处。 ② The company transferred him to sales. 公司把他调到了销售部。
[搭] internal transfer 内部调动, job transfer 工作调动, transfer to 调往, transfer request 调动申请
[联] promotion 晋升, demotion 降职, relocate 搬迁, department 部门, employee 雇员, position 职位, manager 经理
[记] 可数：a transfer。transfer to 调往某地/某岗

**deadline**
英/美 /ˈdedlaɪn/
n. 截止日期；最后期限
[义] A date or time by which something must be finished. 必须完成某事的最后时限（meet a deadline 赶上截止日，miss a deadline 错过期限）。职场压力与项目管理核心词。
[例] ① The project deadline is next Friday. 项目截止日期是下周五。 ② She worked late to meet the deadline. 她加班以赶上期限。
[搭] meet a deadline 赶上截止日, miss a deadline 错过期限, tight deadline 紧迫的期限, deadline extension 延期
[联] project 项目, task 任务, schedule 日程, pressure 压力, overtime 加班, boss 老板, work 工作, deliver 交付
[记] 可数：a deadline。meet/miss a deadline 固定搭配

**meeting**
英/美 /ˈmiːtɪŋ/
n. 会议
[义] An occasion when people come together to discuss work or make decisions. 为讨论工作而召开的会面（staff meeting 员工会，zoom meeting 线上会）。职场日程最常见条目之一。
[例] ① The manager called a meeting at nine. 经理九点召集会议。 ② Too many meetings reduce deep work time. 太多会议挤压深度工作时间。
[搭] staff meeting 员工会, team meeting 团队会, schedule a meeting 安排会议, meeting room 会议室, zoom meeting 线上会议
[联] agenda 议程, colleague 同事, boss 老板, project 项目, discuss 讨论, schedule 日程, presentation 演示, office 办公室
[记] 可数：a meeting。have/hold a meeting。meeting 亦指「会面、偶遇」——另一义

**project**
英/美 /ˈprɒdʒekt/
n. 项目
v. 规划；投射
[义] n. A planned piece of work with specific goals and a timeline. 有目标、有期限的工作任务（lead a project 负责项目，project manager 项目经理）。v. 投射、预测——多义。
[例] ① She was assigned to a six-month project. 她被派到一个为期六个月的项目。 ② The team delivered the project on time. 团队按时交付了项目。
[搭] project manager 项目经理, project deadline 项目截止日, work on a project 做项目, group project 小组项目
[联] task 任务, deadline 截止日期, team 团队, client 客户, budget 预算, deliver 交付, assignment 任务, manager 经理
[记] 可数：a project。project manager 常缩写 PM

**task**
英/美 /tɑːsk/
n. 任务
v. 派给任务
[义] A piece of work that must be done. 必须完成的一项工作（daily tasks 日常任务，assign a task 分配任务）。比 project 更小、更具体。
[例] ① Her first task was to update the database. 她的第一项任务是更新数据库。 ② Managers should not task interns with unpaid overtime. 经理不应让实习生做无薪加班活。
[搭] daily tasks 日常任务, assign a task 分配任务, complete a task 完成任务, task list 任务清单
[联] project 项目, assignment 作业, duty 职责, work 工作, deadline 截止日期, complete 完成, priority 优先级
[记] 可数：a task。take on a task 承担任务

**shift**
英/美 /ʃɪft/
n. 轮班；班次
v. 转移；换班
[义] n. A period of work hours（night shift 夜班，work shifts 轮班）。v. 转移、改变。服务业、制造业、医护极高频。
[例] ① She works the night shift at the hospital. 她在医院上夜班。 ② Employees shift between roles during peak season. 旺季时员工在岗位间轮换。
[搭] night shift 夜班, day shift 日班, work shifts 轮班, shift schedule 排班表, shift worker 轮班工人
[联] schedule 排班, overtime 加班, worker 工人, employee 雇员, hours 工时, rotation 轮换, wage 工资
[记] 可数：a shift。on the night shift 上夜班

**commute**
英/美 /kəˈmjuːt/
n. 通勤
v. 通勤
[义] The journey between home and work; to make that journey regularly. 家与工作地点之间的往返（long commute 通勤时间长，commute by train 坐火车通勤）。
[例] ① His daily commute takes ninety minutes. 他每日通勤要九十分钟。 ② She commutes from the suburbs to downtown. 她从郊区通勤到市中心。
[搭] daily commute 每日通勤, long commute 长通勤, commute by train 坐火车通勤, commute time 通勤时间
[联] remote 远程办公, office 办公室, traffic 交通, work 工作, distance 距离, hybrid 混合办公, transport 交通
[记] 可数/不可数。commuter 名词（通勤者）。remote work 减少 commute

**remote**
英/美 /rɪˈməʊt/
adj. 远程的；偏远的
[义] Done from home or another location outside the traditional office. 不在办公室、异地完成工作（remote work 远程办公，remote employee 远程员工）。疫情后日常职场词汇。
[例] ① The company allows remote work two days a week. 公司允许每周两天远程办公。 ② Remote employees need clear deadlines. 远程员工需要明确的截止日期。
[搭] remote work 远程办公, remote employee 远程员工, fully remote 全远程, remote meeting 远程会议
[联] hybrid 混合办公, commute 通勤, office 办公室, workplace 职场, flexible 灵活的, home 家, zoom 线上会议
[记] 形容词。work remotely 副词短语。remote 亦指「遥远的」——语境区分

**hybrid**
英/美 /ˈhaɪbrɪd/
adj. 混合的；混班的
n. 混合体
[义] Combining remote and in-office work. 远程与到岗相结合的办公模式（hybrid schedule 混合排班，hybrid meeting 线上线下混合会议）。后疫情职场标配词。
[例] ① They adopted a hybrid model: three days office, two days home. 他们采用混合模式：三天办公室、两天居家。 ② Hybrid meetings need good audio for remote colleagues. 混合会议需给远程同事良好音响。
[搭] hybrid work 混合办公, hybrid schedule 混合排班, hybrid model 混合模式, hybrid meeting 混合会议
[联] remote 远程, office 办公室, commute 通勤, flexible 灵活的, workplace 职场, schedule 日程, colleague 同事
[记] 形容词。hybrid work 固定搭配。hybrid 原义「杂交」

**workload**
英/美 /ˈwɜːkləʊd/
n. 工作量
[义] The amount of work that a person or organization has to do. 个人或团队须承担的工作总量（heavy workload 工作量大，manage workload 管理工作量）。与 burnout 议题相关。
[例] ① Her workload doubled after a colleague left. 同事离职后她的工作量翻倍。 ② Managers should monitor team workload fairly. 经理应公平关注团队工作量。
[搭] heavy workload 工作量大, manage workload 管理工作量, workload balance 工作量平衡, reduce workload 减轻工作量
[联] overtime 加班, burnout 倦怠, stress 压力, deadline 截止日期, task 任务, employee 雇员, boss 老板
[记] 可数/不可数：a heavy workload。work + load

**productivity**
英/美 /ˌprɒdʌkˈtɪvəti/
n. 生产力；效率
[义] The rate at which work is done or goods are produced. 单位时间内完成工作的效率（boost productivity 提高效率，productivity tools 效率工具）。管理与经济学高频词。
[例] ① Flexible hours improved productivity in the pilot team. 弹性工时提升了试点团队的效率。 ② Too many emails hurt productivity. 过多邮件损害生产力。
[搭] boost productivity 提高效率, labor productivity 劳动生产率, productivity tools 效率工具, productivity gap 生产率差距
[联] efficient 高效的, output 产出, work 工作, performance 绩效, employee 雇员, manager 经理, tool 工具
[记] 不可数。productive 形容词；productively 副词

**feedback**
英/美 /ˈfiːdbæk/
n. 反馈
[义] Information about how well you are doing at work, intended to help you improve. 关于工作表现的评价与建议（give feedback 给反馈，360-degree feedback 360度评估）。绩效与发展核心环节。
[例] ① She asked her manager for honest feedback. 她请经理给予坦诚反馈。 ② Constructive feedback should be specific and timely. 建设性反馈应具体、及时。
[搭] give/receive feedback 给予/接收反馈, constructive feedback 建设性反馈, feedback session 反馈会谈, customer feedback 客户反馈
[联] performance review 绩效评估, manager 经理, improve 改进, criticism 批评, praise 表扬, colleague 同事, mentor 导师
[记] 不可数。feedback on 关于……的反馈。feed back 动词短语少用

**industry**
英/美 /ˈɪndəstri/
n. 行业；工业
[义] A particular form of business or commercial activity; the companies that produce a particular type of goods or services. 某一商业领域或同类企业总和（tech industry 科技业，film industry 电影业）。求职谈 industry experience 行业经验。
[例] ① She has ten years of experience in the industry. 她在这行业有十年经验。 ② The industry faces new regulations next year. 行业明年面临新监管。
[搭] tech industry 科技业, film industry 电影业, industry experience 行业经验, industry leader 行业龙头
[联] sector 部门, company 公司, career 职业生涯, market 市场, trade 贸易, business 商业, employer 雇主
[记] 可数/不可数：the tech industry。industrial 形容词

**sector**
英/美 /ˈsektə(r)/
n. 部门；行业板块
[义] A part of an economy or society, especially one involved in a particular type of business. 经济或社会中的某一板块（public sector 公共部门，private sector 私营部门）。比 industry 更宏观、政策文本常见。
[例] ① She moved from the public sector to a startup. 她从公共部门转到一家初创公司。 ② The service sector employs most urban workers. 服务业吸纳了大部分城市劳动者。
[搭] public sector 公共部门, private sector 私营部门, service sector 服务业, financial sector 金融部门
[联] industry 行业, economy 经济, government 政府, company 公司, employment 就业, market 市场
[记] 可数：a sector。sector 亦指「扇形」——几何义

**company**
英/美 /ˈkʌmpəni/
n. 公司
[义] A business organization that makes or sells goods or services. 从事生产或服务的商业组织（work for a company 在公司上班，startup company 创业公司）。最通用的「公司」说法之一。
[例] ① She joined the company as a trainee. 她以培训生身份加入公司。 ② The company offers remote work options. 公司提供远程办公选项。
[搭] tech company 科技公司, company culture 公司文化, work for a company 在公司工作, company policy 公司政策
[联] corporation 大公司, firm 律所/公司, employer 雇主, startup 初创, employee 员工, business 企业, office 办公室
[记] 可数：a company。company 亦指「陪伴」——另一义

**corporation**
英/美 /ˌkɔːpəˈreɪʃn/
n. 大公司；法人企业
[义] A large company or group of companies authorized to act as a single entity. 依法作为单一实体运营的大型企业（multinational corporation 跨国公司）。比 company 更正式、规模大。
[例] ① She works for a multinational corporation with offices worldwide. 她在一家跨国公司工作，办公室遍布全球。 ② The corporation reported record profits. 集团报告了创纪录利润。
[搭] multinational corporation 跨国公司, corporation tax 公司税, public corporation 公共企业, corporation law 公司法
[联] company 公司, business 企业, executive 高管, shareholder 股东, multinational 跨国的, employer 雇主
[记] 可数：a corporation。Corp. 缩写。incorporate 动词（注册成立公司）

**firm**
英/美 /fɜːm/
n. 公司；商行
[义] A business company, especially one providing professional services. 企业，尤指提供专业服务的商行（law firm 律所，consulting firm 咨询公司）。；此处为名词义。
[例] ① He made partner at a firm specializing in tax law. 他在一家税务律所成为合伙人。 ② The firm hired twenty graduates this year. 这家公司今年招了二十名毕业生。
[搭] law firm 律所, consulting firm 咨询公司, accounting firm 会计师事务所, firm partner 事务所合伙人
[联] company 公司, corporation 大公司, employer 雇主, partner 合伙人, client 客户, professional 专业的, office 办公室
[记] 可数：a firm。law firm / consulting firm 为固定搭配。

**startup**
英/美 /ˈstɑːtʌp/
n. 初创公司
[义] A newly established business, typically one that is fast-growing and technology-oriented. 新成立、常指科技导向、增长快的企业（join a startup 加入初创，startup culture 初创文化）。
[例] ① She left corporate employment for a startup. 她离开企业雇佣去了一家初创公司。 ② Startups often offer equity instead of high salaries. 初创常给股权而非高薪。
[搭] tech startup 科技初创, join a startup 加入初创, startup founder 初创创始人, startup funding 初创融资
[联] entrepreneur 创业者, founder 创始人, company 公司, venture 风险投资, employee 员工, equity 股权, innovation 创新
[记] 可数：a startup。亦拼 start-up。founder 常指 startup 创始人

**entrepreneur**
英/美 /ˌɒntrəprəˈnɜː(r)/
n. 创业者；企业家
[义] A person who starts and runs businesses, taking on financial risks. 创办并经营企业、承担风险的人（serial entrepreneur 连续创业者）。与 employee 身份相对。
[例] ① The entrepreneur launched three startups in ten years. 这位创业者十年里创办了三家初创公司。 ② Entrepreneurs often work long hours without guaranteed pay. 企业家常长时间工作且无保障收入。
[搭] serial entrepreneur 连续创业者, social entrepreneur 社会企业家, aspiring entrepreneur 有志创业者, entrepreneur mindset 企业家思维
[联] startup 初创, founder 创始人, business 生意, risk 风险, innovate 创新, employer 雇主, CEO 首席执行官
[记] 可数：an entrepreneur。entrepreneurship 名词（创业精神）

**founder**
英/美 /ˈfaʊndə(r)/
n. 创始人
v. 失败；沉没
[义] n. A person who establishes a company or organization. 创立公司或组织的人（co-founder 联合创始人）。v. 失败、沉没（The project foundered.）——另一义。
[例] ① She is a founder and CEO of the startup. 她是这家初创的创始人兼 CEO。 ② The founders still own a majority stake. 创始人仍持多数股权。
[搭] co-founder 联合创始人, company founder 公司创始人, founder equity 创始人股权, founder-led 创始人领导
[联] entrepreneur 创业者, startup 初创, CEO 首席执行官, establish 创立, employee 员工, investor 投资者
[记] 可数：a founder。found 动词（创立）。co-founder 极常见

**CEO**
英/美 /ˌsiː iː ˈəʊ/
n. 首席执行官
[义] Chief Executive Officer: the highest-ranking person in a company, responsible for overall management. 公司最高管理者（CEO of a tech company 科技公司 CEO）。董事会任命，对业绩负总责。
[例] ① The CEO announced a new remote work policy. CEO 宣布了新的远程办公政策。 ② She became CEO at the age of thirty-five. 她三十五岁成为 CEO。
[搭] CEO of 某公司的 CEO, acting CEO 代理 CEO, CEO succession CEO 继任, meet the CEO
[联] executive 高管, founder 创始人, board 董事会, manager 经理, company 公司, leadership 领导力, president 总裁
[记] 可数：a CEO。Chief Executive Officer 全称。C-suite 指高管层（CEO, CFO, COO 等）

**client**
英/美 /ˈklaɪənt/
n. 客户；委托人
[义] A person or organization that pays for professional services or products. 购买专业服务或产品的客户（client meeting 客户会议，internal vs. external clients 内外部客户）。B2B、律所、咨询、自由职业核心词。
[例] ① The consultant met with clients all afternoon. 顾问整个下午都在见客户。 ② Protect client data according to policy. 须按政策保护客户数据。
[搭] client meeting 客户会议, client base 客户群, key client 重要客户, internal client 内部客户
[联] customer 顾客, vendor 供应商, service 服务, contract 合同, project 项目, freelance 自由职业, sales 销售
[记] 可数：a client。client 偏专业服务关系；customer 偏购买产品

**customer**
英/美 /ˈkʌstəmə(r)/
n. 顾客；客户
[义] A person who buys goods or services from a business. 从商家购买商品或服务的人（customer service 客户服务，customer satisfaction 客户满意度）。零售、服务业极高频。
[例] ① Employees should treat every customer with respect. 员工应尊重每一位顾客。 ② The company surveyed customer feedback. 公司调查了客户反馈。
[搭] customer service 客户服务, customer satisfaction 客户满意度, loyal customer 忠实客户, customer base 客户群
[联] client 客户, service 服务, sales 销售, business 生意, feedback 反馈, support 支持, product 产品
[记] 可数：a customer。client 与 customer 在 B2B 中常互换，细辨见 client 条

**stakeholder**
英/美 /ˈsteɪkhəʊldə(r)/
n. 利益相关方
[义] A person or group with an interest in the success of a company or project. 对公司或项目结果有利害关系的个人或群体（internal stakeholders 内部利益相关者，stakeholder meeting 利益相关方会议）。管理、ESG 语境高频。
[例] ① The CEO updated stakeholders on the merger. CEO 向利益相关方通报并购进展。 ② Employees are key stakeholders in culture change. 员工是文化变革的关键利益相关方。
[搭] key stakeholder 关键利益相关方, stakeholder meeting 利益相关方会议, internal stakeholder 内部利益相关者, stakeholder management 利益相关方管理
[联] shareholder 股东, client 客户, employee 雇员, investor 投资者, board 董事会, company 公司, community 社区
[记] 可数：a stakeholder。stake（利害关系）+ holder

**vendor**
英/美 /ˈvendə(r)/
n. 供应商；卖方
[义] A person or company that sells goods or services, especially to other businesses. 向企业供货或提供服务的卖方（approved vendor 核准供应商，vendor contract 供应商合同）。采购与供应链语境。
[例] ① IT approved a new software vendor. IT 部门核准了一家新软件供应商。 ② Vendors must comply with security standards. 供应商须符合安全标准。
[搭] approved vendor 核准供应商, vendor contract 供应商合同, third-party vendor 第三方供应商, vendor management 供应商管理

[记] 可数：a vendor。vendor 亦指街头「小贩」——另一义

**union**
英/美 /ˈjuːniən/
n. 工会；联合
[义] An organization of workers formed to protect their rights and interests. 为维护劳工权益而组成的工人组织（join a union 加入工会，union member 工会会员）。亦指「联合、联盟」——多义。
[例] ① Employees voted to form a union. 员工投票决定成立工会。 ② The union negotiated higher wages. 工会谈判提高了工资。
[搭] labor union 工会（美式）, trade union 工会（英式）, union member 工会会员, union contract 工会合同
[联] strike 罢工, wage 工资, worker 工人, labor 劳动, negotiate 谈判, employee 雇员, employer 雇主
[记] 可数：a union。join a union。trade union 英式常用

**strike**
英/美 /straɪk/
n. 罢工
v. 罢工；打击
[义] n. A period when workers refuse to work to protest pay or conditions. 工人为抗议薪酬或条件而停止工作（go on strike 罢工）。v. 罢工、打击——多义。
[例] ① Transit workers went on strike for better pay. 公交工人罢工要求更高薪酬。 ② The strike lasted two weeks before a deal was reached. 罢工持续两周后达成协议。
[搭] go on strike 罢工, general strike 总罢工, strike action 罢工行动, call a strike 发动罢工
[联] union 工会, labor 劳动, wage 工资, protest 抗议, negotiate 谈判, worker 工人, employer 雇主
[记] 可数：a strike。go on strike 固定搭配。strike 亦指「打击」——多义

**payroll**
英/美 /ˈpeɪrəʊl/
n. 工资单；在册员工
[义] A list of employees and their salaries; the total amount a company pays in wages. 公司发放薪酬的名单与总额（on the payroll 在工资名册上，payroll tax 工资税）。HR 与财务用语。
[例] ① The company has five hundred people on the payroll. 公司在册五百人。 ② Payroll is processed on the last day of the month. 工资在每月最后一天发放。
[搭] on the payroll 在册, payroll tax 工资税, payroll department 薪资部门, process payroll 处理工资发放
[联] salary 薪水, wage 工资, paycheck 工资支票, employee 雇员, HR 人力资源, tax 税, benefit 福利
[记] 不可数/可数。run payroll 发工资。payroll 亦指「薪资部门」

**commission**
英/美 /kəˈmɪʃn/
n. 佣金；委员会
[义] An amount of money paid to a salesperson based on sales. 按销售额支付给销售人员的提成（earn commission 赚佣金，sales commission 销售佣金）。亦指「委员会」——多义。
[例] ① She earns a base salary plus commission. 她拿底薪加提成。 ② Commission rates vary by product line. 佣金比例因产品线而异。

[联] bonus 奖金, salary 薪水, sales 销售, incentive 激励, pay 报酬, percentage 百分比, employee 雇员
[记] 可数/不可数。on commission 靠提成。commission 亦指「委员会」

**raise**
英/美 /reɪz/
n. 加薪（美式）
v. 提高；举起
[义] n. American: an increase in salary（ask for a raise 要求加薪）。v. 提高、举起——多义。英式加薪常说 pay rise。
[例] ① She got a five percent raise after her review. 考核后她涨了百分之五的工资。 ② He asked his boss for a raise. 他向老板要求加薪。

[记] 可数：a raise。美式；英式 pay rise。raise 动词义极多，靠语境区分

**temp**
英/美 /temp/
n. 临时工
[义] informal A temporary worker, especially one supplied by an agency. 短期雇佣、常由中介派遣的临时工（work as a temp 做临时活，temp agency 临时工中介）。形容词 temporary 的口语缩写。
[例] ① She worked as a temp between permanent jobs. 她在找正式工作间隙做临时工。 ② The office hired a temp for the busy season. 办公室旺季雇了一名临时工。
[搭] work as a temp 做临时工, temp agency 临时工中介, temp job 临时工作, temp worker 临时工
[联] temporary 临时的, contract 合同, employee 雇员, agency 中介, hire 雇用, part-time 兼职的, intern 实习生

**position**
英/美 /pəˈzɪʃn/
n. 职位；岗位
[义] A job in a company; a role with specific duties. 公司中的工作岗位（open position 空缺职位，apply for a position 申请职位）。比 job 略正式。
[例] ① She applied for a position in marketing. 她申请了营销岗位。 ② The position requires three years of experience. 该职位要求三年经验。
[搭] open position 空缺职位, apply for a position 申请职位, senior position 高级职位, position description 职位描述
[联] job 工作, role 角色, vacancy 空缺, hire 雇用, employee 雇员, apply 申请, title 头衔, career 职业生涯
[记] 可数：a position。position 亦指「位置、立场」——多义

**role**
英/美 /rəʊl/
n. 角色；职责
[义] The function or position that someone has in a team or organization. 在团队或组织中的职能与定位（job role 岗位职责，play a key role 起关键作用）。比 position 更强调做什么而非职级名称。
[例] ① Her role expanded after the reorganization. 重组后她的职责扩大了。 ② Clarify each team member's role before the project starts. 项目启动前明确各成员角色。
[搭] job role 岗位职责, key role 关键角色, role clarity 职责清晰, expand one's role 扩大职责
[联] position 职位, duty 职责, task 任务, team 团队, responsibility 责任, job 工作, manager 经理
[记] 可数：a role。play a role in 在……中发挥作用

**duty**
英/美 /ˈdjuːti/
n. 职责；值班
[义] Something you must do because of your job. 因工作而必须履行的责任（job duties 工作职责，on duty 值班中）。复数 duties 常指具体职责清单。
[例] ① Reporting safety issues is part of every employee's duties. 报告安全隐患是每个员工职责的一部分。 ② Nurses rotate night duty. 护士轮换夜班。
[搭] job duties 工作职责, on duty 值班, off duty 下班, duty of care 照护责任
[联] responsibility 责任, role 角色, task 任务, obligation 义务, work 工作, shift 轮班, employee 雇员
[记] 可数/不可数：a duty / duties。on duty / off duty 固定搭配

**assignment**
英/美 /əˈsaɪnmənt/
n. 任务；委派
[义] A task or piece of work assigned to someone. 派给某人的具体任务（work assignment 工作任务，overseas assignment 海外派驻）。亦指学生作业——语境区分。
[例] ① Her first overseas assignment lasted two years. 她第一次海外派驻持续了两年。 ② The manager gave the team a tight assignment. 经理给了团队一项紧迫的任务。
[搭] work assignment 工作任务, overseas assignment 海外派驻, temporary assignment 临时任务, complete an assignment 完成任务
[联] task 任务, project 项目, duty 职责, delegate 委派, employee 雇员, deadline 截止日期
[记] 可数：an assignment。assign 动词

**schedule**
英/美 /ˈʃedjuːl/ 美 /ˈskedʒuːl/
n. 日程；排班表
v. 安排
[义] A plan of activities or work times. 工作或活动的时间安排（work schedule 工作时间表，on schedule 按时）。shift schedule 指轮班表。
[例] ① Her schedule is packed with meetings this week. 她本周日程排满会议。 ② Managers schedule shifts one month ahead. 经理提前一个月排班。
[搭] work schedule 工作时间表, on schedule 按时, tight schedule 紧凑日程, schedule a meeting 安排会议
[联] shift 轮班, meeting 会议, deadline 截止日期, calendar 日历, plan 计划, overtime 加班, flexible 灵活的
[记] 可数：a schedule。behind/ahead of schedule 延迟/提前

**department**
英/美 /dɪˈpɑːtmənt/
n. 部门；系
[义] A section of a company or organization with a specific function. 公司内按职能划分的部门（HR department 人事部，sales department 销售部）。
[例] ① She transferred to the marketing department. 她调到了市场部。 ② Each department has its own budget. 每个部门有独立预算。
[搭] HR department 人事部, sales department 销售部, department head 部门负责人, cross-department 跨部门
[联] division 事业部, team 团队, manager 经理, company 公司, office 办公室, employee 员工, organization 组织
[记] 可数：a department。dept. 缩写

**headquarters**
英/美 /ˌhedˈkwɔːtəz/
n. 总部
[义] The main offices of a company or organization. 公司或机构的主要行政中心（corporate headquarters 公司总部，relocate headquarters 搬迁总部）。通常作复数形式。
[例] ① The company headquarters is in Shanghai. 公司总部在上海。 ② She works at regional offices, not headquarters. 她在区域办事处工作，不在总部。
[搭] corporate headquarters 公司总部, headquarters building 总部大楼, relocate headquarters 搬迁总部
[联] office 办公室, branch 分公司, company 公司, CEO 首席执行官, corporate 企业的, location 地点
[记] 通常复数 headquarters；单数 headquarter 少用。HQ 口语缩写

**teamwork**
英/美 /ˈtiːmwɜːk/
n. 团队合作
[义] The combined effort of a group working together effectively. 团队协同完成任务的能力与行为（good teamwork 团队合作好，teamwork skills 协作技能）。面试高频软技能词。
[例] ① The project succeeded thanks to strong teamwork. 项目因出色团队合作而成功。 ② Interviewers often ask about teamwork examples. 面试官常问团队合作事例。
[搭] good teamwork 良好协作, teamwork skills 团队合作技能, teamwork and communication 协作与沟通
[联] team 团队, collaborate 合作, colleague 同事, cooperate 合作, project 项目, skill 技能, communication 沟通
[记] 不可数。team + work 复合词

**headhunter**
英/美 /ˈhedhʌntə(r)/
n. 猎头
[义] informal A recruiter who finds senior executives or specialists for companies. 为高管或专业人才猎聘的招聘顾问（executive headhunter 高管猎头）。比 recruiter 更偏高端岗位。
[例] ① A headhunter contacted her about a CEO role. 一位猎头联系她谈 CEO 职位。 ② Headhunters often search passive candidates. 猎头常搜寻未主动求职的候选人。
[搭] executive headhunter 高管猎头, headhunter call 猎头电话, contact a headhunter 联系猎头
[联] recruiter 招聘人员, executive 高管, candidate 候选人, hire 雇用, career 职业生涯, LinkedIn 领英
[记] 可数：a headhunter。headhunt 动词。略带非正式色彩

**application**
英/美 /ˌæplɪˈkeɪʃn/
n. 申请；申请书
[义] A formal request for a job, usually with documents. 求职的正式提交（job application 求职申请，submit an application 提交申请）。亦指「应用、涂抹」——多义。
[例] ① She submitted five applications last week. 她上周投了五份申请。 ② Incomplete applications will not be reviewed. 不完整的申请不予审核。
[搭] job application 求职申请, submit an application 提交申请, application form 申请表, online application 在线申请
[联] applicant 申请人, apply 申请, resume 简历, interview 面试, hire 雇用, cover letter 求职信
[记] 可数：an application。apply 动词。application 亦指手机 app——另一义

**cover letter**
英/美 /ˈkʌvə ˌletə(r)/
n. 求职信；附信
[义] A letter sent with a resume explaining why you want a job and why you are suitable. 随简历附上、说明求职动机与匹配度的信（write a cover letter 写求职信）。英美求职标配。
[例] ① Tailor your cover letter to each position. 针对每个职位定制求职信。 ② The recruiter read her cover letter before the resume. 招聘官先看求职信再看简历。
[搭] write a cover letter 写求职信, cover letter and resume 求职信与简历, tailored cover letter 定制求职信
[联] resume 简历, application 申请, applicant 申请人, interview 面试, job 工作, hire 雇用
[记] 可数：a cover letter。英式 covering letter 同义

**performance review**
英/美 /pəˈfɔːməns rɪˈvjuː/
n. 绩效评估
[义] A formal meeting at which an employee discusses their work with a manager, often annually. 员工与上级定期评估工作表现的会谈（annual performance review 年度考核）。亦简称 review。
[例] ① Her performance review is scheduled for December. 她的绩效评估定在十二月。 ② Feedback from the performance review shaped her goals. 绩效面谈的反馈影响了她新目标。
[搭] annual performance review 年度考核, performance review meeting 绩效面谈, mid-year review 年中考核
[联] feedback 反馈, manager 经理, employee 雇员, promotion 晋升, bonus 奖金, goal 目标, evaluate 评估
[记] 可数：a performance review。亦说 performance appraisal（更正式）

**white-collar**
英/美 /ˌwaɪt ˈkɒlə(r)/
adj. 白领的
[义] Relating to office work, especially professional or clerical jobs. 在办公室从事脑力劳动的职业（white-collar worker 白领工人，white-collar job 白领工作）。与 blue-collar 对照。
[例] ① She sought a white-collar career after university. 大学毕业后她追求白领事业。 ② White-collar roles often require a degree. 白领岗位常要求学历。
[搭] white-collar worker 白领, white-collar job 白领工作, white-collar crime 白领犯罪（另一义）
[联] blue-collar 蓝领的, office 办公室, professional 专业的, employee 雇员, career 职业生涯, desk 办公桌
[记] 形容词。源自「穿白衬衫的办公室职员」。blue-collar 反义对照

**blue-collar**
英/美 /ˌbluː ˈkɒlə(r)/
adj. 蓝领的
[义] Relating to manual or industrial work. 从事体力或现场劳动的职业（blue-collar worker 蓝领工人，blue-collar trade 蓝领手艺）。与 white-collar 对照。
[例] ① Blue-collar wages rose in the manufacturing sector. 制造业蓝领工资上涨了。 ② He comes from a blue-collar family. 他出身蓝领家庭。
[搭] blue-collar worker 蓝领工人, blue-collar job 蓝领工作, blue-collar trade 蓝领手艺
[联] white-collar 白领的, worker 工人, wage 工资, union 工会, factory 工厂, skilled 熟练的, labor 劳动
[记] 形容词。源自「穿耐脏蓝工装的一线工人」

**craft**
英/美 /krɑːft/
n. 手艺；工艺
v. 精心制作
[义] A job or activity needing skill, especially making things by hand. 需要熟练技能的职业，尤指手工技艺（learn a craft 学手艺，by craft 干这一行的）。动词 craft 指「精心打造」。
[例] ① He learned the craft from his father, a carpenter. 他从当木匠的父亲那里学会了手艺。 ② Skilled crafts still command good wages. 熟练手艺仍能挣可观工资。
[搭] learn a craft 学手艺, by craft 干这一行, craft skills 手工技能, master a craft 精通手艺
[联] trade 行业, apprentice 学徒, skill 技能, vocational 职业的, blue-collar 蓝领的, artisan 工匠
[记] 可数/不可数。craftsman/craftsperson 工匠。crafted 形容词（精心制作的）

**gig economy**
英/美 /ˈɡɪɡ iˈkɒnəmi/
n. 零工经济
[义] A labor market of short-term contracts and freelance work rather than permanent jobs. 以短期合同与自由职业为主、而非长期雇佣的劳动市场（work in the gig economy 在零工经济中讨生活）。
[例] ① Many drivers work in the gig economy without benefits. 许多司机在零工经济中工作却没有福利。 ② The gig economy grew with app-based platforms. 基于 App 的平台推动了零工经济发展。
[搭] gig economy worker 零工经济从业者, participate in the gig economy 参与零工经济, gig economy platform 零工平台
[联] freelance 自由职业, contractor 承包商, temp 临时工, flexible 灵活的, benefit 福利, platform 平台, side hustle 副业
[记] 不可数。gig 原指音乐人「单场演出」→ 泛指短期活计

**side hustle**
英/美 /ˈsaɪd ˌhʌsl/
n. 副业
[义] informal A job or business you do in addition to your main employment to earn extra money. 主业之外为增收而做的兼职或生意（start a side hustle 搞副业）。现代职场与创作者经济热词。
[例] ① Her side hustle selling prints became a full-time business. 她卖版画的副业做成了全职生意。 ② Many employees keep a side hustle for extra income. 许多员工靠副业补贴收入。
[搭] start a side hustle 开始副业, side hustle income 副业收入, full-time job and side hustle 主业与副业
[联] freelance 自由职业, gig economy 零工经济, income 收入, entrepreneur 创业者, part-time 兼职, main job 主业
[记] 可数：a side hustle。hustle 口语指「忙活、拼搏」

**workaholic**
英/美 /ˌwɜːkəˈhɒlɪk/
n. 工作狂
adj. 工作狂式的
[义] A person who works excessively hard and long hours, often neglecting rest and personal life. 过度投入工作、常忽视休息与生活的人（self-confessed workaholic 自认工作狂）。含轻微警示色彩。
[例] ① He's a workaholic who checks email on vacation. 他是个工作狂，度假还在查邮件。 ② Workaholic culture can lead to burnout. 工作狂文化会导致倦怠。
[搭] self-confessed workaholic 自认工作狂, workaholic tendencies 工作狂倾向, recover from being a workaholic 摆脱工作狂状态
[联] burnout 倦怠, overtime 加班, workload 工作量, work-life balance 工作与生活平衡, stress 压力, boss 老板, career 事业
[记] 可数：a workaholic。work + alcoholic 仿造词。含轻微贬义或自嘲

**minimum wage**
英/美 /ˈmɪnɪməm weɪdʒ/
n. 最低工资
[义] The lowest hourly pay that employers may legally pay workers. 法律规定的最低小时工资标准（raise the minimum wage 提高最低工资，minimum wage worker 最低工资劳动者）。劳动政策核心议题。
[例] ① The state raised the minimum wage to fifteen dollars an hour. 该州把最低工资提高到每小时十五美元。 ② Many workers rely on minimum wage jobs. 许多工人靠最低工资岗位谋生。
[搭] raise the minimum wage 提高最低工资, minimum wage worker 最低工资劳动者, federal minimum wage 联邦最低工资, living wage 生活工资（高于最低工资的运动）
[联] wage 工资, labor 劳动, union 工会, employee 雇员, policy 政策, poverty 贫困, fair 公平的
[记] 不可数。the minimum wage 常作单数。living wage 为相关但更高目标

**job description**
英/美 /ˈdʒɒb dɪˌskrɪpʃn/
n. 职位描述
[义] A written explanation of the duties, requirements, and conditions of a job. 列明岗位职责、要求与条件的书面说明（read the job description 阅读职位描述）。招聘与 HR 标准文件。
[例] ① The job description lists required qualifications. 职位描述列出了所需资格。 ② Make sure your resume matches the job description. 确保简历与职位描述匹配。
[搭] read the job description 阅读职位描述, update a job description 更新职位描述, job description and requirements 职位描述与要求
[联] position 职位, role 角色, vacancy 空缺, apply 申请, qualification 资格, HR 人力资源, hire 雇用
[记] 可数：a job description。缩写 JD（口语/HR）

**offer**
英/美 /ˈɒfə(r)/
n. 录用通知；报价
v. 提供
[义] n. A formal proposal to give someone a job（job offer 录用通知，accept an offer 接受录用）。v. 提供。职场语境中 offer 常为「录用函」。
[例] ① She received a job offer with a higher salary. 她收到一份录用通知，薪水更高。 ② He accepted the offer and starts Monday. 他接受了录用，周一开始上班。
[搭] job offer 录用通知, accept/decline an offer 接受/拒绝录用, offer letter 录用函, counter-offer 还价/反向要约
[联] hire 雇用, salary 薪水, interview 面试, applicant 申请人, negotiate 谈判, employer 雇主, start date 入职日
[记] 可数：an offer。accept the offer 固定说法。counter-offer 谈判薪资时用

**work-life balance**
英/美 /ˌwɜːk laɪf ˈbæləns/
n. 工作与生活平衡
[义] The ability to divide time and energy between work and personal life in a healthy way. 在职业与个人生活之间保持健康分配（poor work-life balance 失衡，promote work-life balance 促进平衡）。现代职场福利议题。
[例] ① Remote work improved her work-life balance. 远程办公改善了她的工作与生活平衡。 ② Companies advertise work-life balance to attract talent. 公司以工作生活平衡招揽人才。
[搭] achieve work-life balance 实现工作生活平衡, poor work-life balance 平衡不佳, work-life balance policy 平衡政策
[联] burnout 倦怠, overtime 加班, flexible 灵活的, remote 远程, family 家庭, stress 压力, benefit 福利, workload 工作量
[记] 不可数。亦写 work/life balance。与 workaholic 对照讨论

**part-time**
英/美 /ˌpɑːt ˈtaɪm/
adj. 兼职的；adv. 兼职地
[义] For or during only part of the normal working day or week. 非全日、非全周的工作安排（part-time job 兼职工作，work part-time 兼职上班）。与 full-time 对照。
[例] ① She took a part-time position while studying. 读书期间她找了份兼职岗位。 ② He works part-time at a café. 他在一家咖啡馆兼职。
[搭] part-time job 兼职工作, work part-time 兼职上班, part-time employee 兼职员工, part-time hours 兼职工时
[联] full-time 全职的, employee 雇员, job 工作, schedule 日程, wage 工资, student 学生, side hustle 副业, flexible 灵活的
[记] 形容词/副词。part-time 无连字符变体 part time 亦见。与 full-time 成对记忆

**full-time**
英/美 /ˌfʊl ˈtaɪm/
adj. 全职的；adv. 全职地
[义] For the whole of the normal working week. 按标准工时全职投入的工作（full-time employee 全职员工，work full-time 全职上班）。福利与合同常与 part-time 不同。
[例] ① She became a full-time employee after probation. 试用期后她转为全职员工。 ② He works full-time and studies at night. 他全职工作，晚上上课。
[搭] full-time employee 全职员工, work full-time 全职上班, full-time position 全职岗位, full-time hours 全职工时
[联] part-time 兼职的, employment 就业, salary 薪水, benefit 福利, career 职业生涯, job 工作, overtime 加班
[记] 形容词/副词。full-time job 常含稳定雇佣意味

**branch**
英/美 /brɑːntʃ/
n. 分公司；分支机构
[义] A local office or shop belonging to a larger company or organization. 大公司下属的地方办事处或门店（branch office 分公司，open a new branch 开设新分店）。与 headquarters 相对。
[例] ① The bank has a branch on every high street. 该银行每条商业街都有分行。 ② She manages the regional branch of the company. 她管理公司的区域分公司。
[搭] branch office 分公司, branch manager 分公司经理, open a branch 开设分店, local branch 当地分支机构
[联] headquarters 总部, office 办公室, company 公司, manager 经理, regional 区域的, employee 员工, location 地点
[记] 可数：a branch。branch 亦指「树枝、分支」——多义靠语境

**HR**
英/美 /ˌeɪtʃ ˈɑː(r)/
n. 人力资源（部）
[义] Human Resources; the department in a company that deals with hiring, training, pay, and employee relations. 负责招聘、培训、薪酬与员工关系的人事部门（HR department 人事部，contact HR 联系人事）。口语极常用缩写。
[例] ① HR will schedule your onboarding session. 人事部会安排你的入职培训。 ② She works in HR at a tech startup. 她在一家科技初创公司的人事部工作。
[搭] HR department 人事部, contact HR 联系人事, HR policy 人事政策, HR manager 人事经理
[联] employee 雇员, employer 雇主, hire 雇用, recruitment 招聘, payroll 工资单, benefit 福利, interview 面试, personnel 人事
[记] 不可数/集合名词。全称 Human Resources。英式亦说 personnel department

**worker**
英/美 /ˈwɜːkə(r)/
n. 工人；劳动者
[义] A person who does a particular job to earn money, especially manual or industrial work. 为挣钱从事某种工作、尤指体力或产业劳动的人（factory worker 工厂工人，skilled worker 熟练工）。比 employee 更宽，可指非正式雇佣或自雇劳动者。
[例] ① Factory workers organized through the union. 工厂工人通过工会组织起来。 ② The site employs over five hundred workers. 工地雇用了五百多名工人。
[搭] factory worker 工厂工人, skilled worker 熟练工, manual worker 体力劳动者, migrant worker 移民劳工, shift worker 轮班工人
[联] employee 雇员, laborer 劳工, blue-collar 蓝领的, wage 工资, union 工会, workplace 职场, employer 雇主, workforce 劳动力
[记] 可数：a worker。worker 亦泛指任何「工作者」（office worker 办公室职员）

**laborer**
英/美 /ˈleɪbərə(r)/
n. 劳工；体力劳动者
[义] A person who does hard physical work, especially on building sites or farms. 从事繁重体力劳动的人，尤指建筑或农业现场（day laborer 日结劳工，construction laborer 建筑小工）。美式 laborer；英式 labourer。
[例] ① Laborers loaded trucks from dawn to dusk. 劳工从早到晚装车。 ② The company hired seasonal laborers for the harvest. 公司为收割雇了季节性劳工。
[搭] day laborer 日结劳工, construction laborer 建筑小工, manual laborer 体力劳工, farm laborer 农场劳工
[联] worker 工人, labor 劳动, wage 工资, construction 建筑, blue-collar 蓝领的, minimum wage 最低工资, employer 雇主
[记] 可数：a laborer。labor 美式；labour 英式

**carpenter**
英/美 /ˈkɑːpəntə(r)/
n. 木匠；木工
[义] A person whose job is making and repairing wooden objects and structures. 制作、修理木制物件与结构的工匠（master carpenter 老木工，carpenter's tools 木工工具）。建筑与装修常见蓝领职业。
[例] ① The carpenter built custom shelves for the office. 木匠为办公室做了定制架子。 ② She apprenticed as a carpenter after high school. 高中毕业后她当了木工学徒。
[搭] master carpenter 老木工, carpenter's shop 木工坊, hire a carpenter 请木匠, apprentice carpenter 木工学徒
[联] craft 手艺, apprentice 学徒, builder 建筑工, electrician 电工, plumber 水管工, blue-collar 蓝领的, construction 建筑
[记] 可数：a carpenter。carpentry 名词（木工手艺）

**electrician**
英/美 /ɪˌlekˈtrɪʃn/
n. 电工
[义] A person whose job is to install and repair electrical equipment. 安装、维修电气设备的技术工人（ licensed electrician 持证电工，call an electrician 叫电工）。建筑、工厂、居家维修高频职业。
[例] ① An electrician rewired the old office building. 电工给旧办公楼重新布线。 ② Electricians must follow strict safety codes. 电工须遵守严格安全规范。
[搭] licensed electrician 持证电工, call an electrician 叫电工, electrician's license 电工执照, master electrician 高级电工
[联] plumber 水管工, mechanic 机修工, contractor 承包商, craft 手艺, blue-collar 蓝领的, wiring 布线, safety 安全
[记] 可数：an electrician。electrical 形容词。需执照方可独立执业（各地法规不同）

**plumber**
英/美 /ˈplʌmə(r)/
n. 水管工；管道工
[义] A person whose job is to install and repair water pipes, toilets, and heating systems. 安装、维修水管与卫生、供暖系统的人（ emergency plumber 紧急管道工，plumber's snake 管道疏通器）。居家与建筑维修极高频。
[例] ① We called a plumber when the kitchen sink leaked. 厨房水槽漏水我们叫了水管工。 ② The plumber replaced the old pipes in the factory. 管道工更换了工厂旧管道。
[搭] call a plumber 叫水管工, emergency plumber 紧急管道工, licensed plumber 持证水管工, plumber's tools 管道工具
[联] electrician 电工, carpenter 木匠, mechanic 机修工, contractor 承包商, repair 维修, pipe 管道, blue-collar 蓝领的
[记] 可数：a plumber。plumbing 名词（管道系统/管道业）

**mechanic**
英/美 /məˈkænɪk/
n. 机修工；技工
[义] A person whose job is repairing machines, especially car engines. 修理机器、尤指汽车发动机的技术工人（auto mechanic 汽车机修工，aircraft mechanic 航空机械师）。
[例] ① The mechanic fixed the truck's brakes in an hour. 机修工一小时修好了卡车刹车。 ② She trained as an aircraft mechanic in the air force. 她在空军受训成为航空机械师。
[搭] auto mechanic 汽车机修工, aircraft mechanic 航空机械师, skilled mechanic 熟练技工, mechanic shop 修理厂
[联] engineer 工程师, technician 技术员, repair 维修, factory 工厂, blue-collar 蓝领的, vehicle 车辆, skill 技能
[记] 可数：a mechanic。mechanical 形容词。mechanic 亦曾指「力学」——旧义

**welder**
英/美 /ˈweldə(r)/
n. 焊工
[义] A person whose job is joining metal parts by welding. 以焊接方式连接金属部件的工人（certified welder 持证焊工，welder's mask 焊工面罩）。制造业、建筑业、船舶行业常见。
[例] ① Welders wear protective gear against sparks. 焊工穿戴防护装备防火花。 ② The factory needs more certified welders. 工厂需要更多持证焊工。
[搭] certified welder 持证焊工, welder's mask 焊工面罩, pipe welder 管道焊工, welder certification 焊工认证
[联] miner 矿工, construction worker 建筑工人, factory worker 工厂工人, blue-collar 蓝领的, metal 金属, safety 安全, skill 技能
[记] 可数：a welder。weld 动词；welding 名词

**construction worker**
英/美 /kənˈstrʌkʃn ˌwɜːkə(r)/
n. 建筑工人
[义] A person employed to build or repair buildings, roads, and other structures. 从事盖房、修路等营造与修缮的工人（construction site worker 工地工人）。施工现场统称性职业词。
[例] ① Construction workers must wear hard hats on site. 建筑工人在工地须戴安全帽。 ② The project employs two hundred construction workers. 项目雇了两百名建筑工人。
[搭] construction site worker 工地工人, skilled construction worker 熟练建筑工, construction worker safety 建筑工人安全
[联] builder 建筑工, laborer 劳工, carpenter 木匠, electrician 电工, contractor 承包商, site 工地, blue-collar 蓝领的
[记] 可数：a construction worker。亦说 construction laborer

**miner**
英/美 /ˈmaɪnə(r)/
n. 矿工
[义] A person who works in a mine extracting coal, metal, or minerals. 在矿井开采煤、金属或矿物的工人（coal miner 煤矿工人，miner’s strike 矿工罢工）。高危职业；新闻与历史语境常见。
[例] ① Coal miners face long shifts underground. 煤矿工人面临长时间井下作业。 ② The explosion trapped several miners. 爆炸困住了几名矿工。
[搭] coal miner 煤矿工人, gold miner 金矿工人, miner strike 矿工罢工, former miner 前矿工
[联] worker 工人, laborer 劳工, union 工会, dangerous occupation 高危职业, wage 工资, underground 地下的, factory 工厂
[记] 可数：a miner。mine 名词/动词（矿井；采矿）

**factory worker**
英/美 /ˈfæktri ˌwɜːkə(r)/
n. 工厂工人
[义] A person employed in a factory, often on a production line. 在工厂、常在流水线上劳作的工人（factory floor worker 车间工人）。制造业核心职业词。
[例] ① Factory workers clock in at six every morning. 工厂工人每天早上六点打卡。 ② Automation changed roles for many factory workers. 自动化改变了许多工厂工人的岗位。
[搭] factory floor worker 车间工人, factory worker union 工厂工人工会, skilled factory worker 熟练工厂工
[联] worker 工人, manufacturing 制造业, assembly line 流水线, shift 轮班, wage 工资, union 工会, blue-collar 蓝领的
[记] 可数：a factory worker。factory 工厂；manufacturing plant 同义偏正式

**midwife**
英/美 /ˈmɪdwaɪf/
n. 助产士
[义] A person, usually a woman, who is trained to help women give birth. 受训协助产妇分娩的专业人员（registered midwife 注册助产士，community midwife 社区助产士）。与 obstetrician（产科医生）分工不同——见 physician 条。
[例] ① The midwife visited the mother after the birth. 助产士产后上门探望产妇。 ② She trained as a midwife for five years. 她接受了五年助产士培训。
[搭] registered midwife 注册助产士, community midwife 社区助产士, midwife-led care 助产士主导照护, call a midwife 请助产士
[联] nurse 护士, physician 医生, caregiver 照护者, hospital 医院, birth 分娩, healthcare worker 医护人员, patient 患者
[记] 可数：a midwife。复数 midwives /ˈmɪdwaɪvz/。mid + wife 古义「与产妇同在者」

**paramedic**
英/美 /ˌpærəˈmedɪk/
n. 急救员；护理 paramedic
[义] A person trained to give emergency medical care outside hospitals, often in ambulances. 在院外、多在救护车上提供紧急医疗的人员（paramedic team 急救组）。比 EMT 培训通常更高级。
[例] ① Paramedics arrived within eight minutes of the call. 报警后八分钟内急救员赶到。 ② She works as a paramedic on the night shift. 她夜班当急救员。
[搭] paramedic team 急救组, paramedic training 急救培训, call paramedics 叫急救人员, flight paramedic 航空急救员
[联] EMT 急救医士, nurse 护士, ambulance 救护车, emergency 紧急, hospital 医院, first aid 急救, physician 医生
[记] 可数：a paramedic。para- + medic。与 EMT 并读：各地级别划分不同

**EMT**
英/美 /ˌiː em ˈtiː/
n. 急救医士
[义] Emergency Medical Technician; a person trained to provide basic emergency care and transport patients. 提供基础紧急救护并转运患者的急救技术人员（EMT certification EMT 认证）。美式常用缩写。
[例] ① The EMT stabilized the patient before transport. 急救医士转运前稳定了患者状况。 ② She completed EMT training last summer. 她去年夏天完成了 EMT 培训。
[搭] EMT certification EMT 认证, EMT training EMT 培训, certified EMT 持证 EMT, EMT and paramedic EMT 与 paramedic
[联] paramedic 急救员, ambulance 救护车, emergency 紧急, first aid 急救, nurse 护士, hospital 医院
[记] 可数：an EMT。全称 Emergency Medical Technician。级别因地区而异

**nursing assistant**
英/美 /ˈnɜːsɪŋ əˈsɪstənt/
n. 护理助理
[义] A worker who helps nurses care for patients with basic tasks like bathing and feeding. 协助护士完成洗浴、喂食等基础照护的工作人员（certified nursing assistant 认证护理助理，缩写 CNA）。养老与医院常见岗位。
[例] ① Nursing assistants often work long shifts on wards. 护理助理常在病房上长班。 ② She became a nursing assistant before nurse training. 读护理前她先做了护理助理。
[搭] certified nursing assistant 认证护理助理（CNA）, nursing assistant training 护理助理培训, hospital nursing assistant 医院护理助理
[联] nurse 护士, caregiver 照护者, hospital 医院, patient 患者, elder care 养老照护, shift 轮班, healthcare worker 医护人员
[记] 可数：a nursing assistant。美式常称 CNA。与 nurse 分工：助理做基础照护，护士执行医嘱

**care worker**
英/美 /ˈkeə ˌwɜːkə(r)/
n. 护工；照护员
[义] A person paid to help elderly, disabled, or ill people with daily living at home or in care facilities. 有偿协助老人、残障或病患日常生活的人（home care worker 居家护工，care home worker 养老院护工）。；此处强调职业身份。
[例] ① Care workers assist residents with meals and medication. 护工帮住户用餐、服药。 ② The agency trains new care workers for two weeks. 中介对新护工培训两周。
[搭] home care worker 居家护工, care home worker 养老院护工, care worker training 护工培训, support care workers 支持护工
[联] caregiver 照护者, nurse 护士, nursing assistant 护理助理, elder 老人, patient 患者, shift 轮班, wage 工资
[记] 可数：a care worker。caregiver 偏角色；care worker 偏职业称谓。英式 carer 亦常见

**healthcare worker**
英/美 /ˈhelθkeə ˌwɜːkə(r)/
n. 医护人员
[义] Anyone who works in a job that involves caring for people's health. 从事健康照护相关工作的总称（frontline healthcare workers 一线医护人员）。
[例] ① Healthcare workers faced burnout during the pandemic. 疫情期间医护人员普遍倦怠。 ② The hospital thanked its healthcare workers publicly. 医院公开感谢医护人员。
[搭] frontline healthcare workers 一线医护人员, healthcare worker shortage 医护短缺, support healthcare workers 支持医护
[联] nurse 护士, physician 医生, paramedic 急救员, hospital 医院, patient 患者, shift 轮班, pandemic 疫情
[记] 可数：a healthcare worker。healthcare 亦写 health care。不替代 nurse/physician 等专条

**dentist**
英/美 /ˈdentɪst/
n. 牙医
[义] A person whose job is to care for people's teeth and gums. 诊治牙齿与牙龈的专业人员（family dentist 家庭牙医，dentist appointment 看牙预约）。独立执业或诊所就业均常见。
[例] ① She visits the dentist twice a year for checkups. 她每年看两次牙医做检查。 ② The dentist recommended a deep cleaning. 牙医建议做深度洁牙。
[搭] family dentist 家庭牙医, dentist appointment 看牙预约, dentist office 牙科诊所, pediatric dentist 儿童牙医
[联] physician 医生, nurse 护士, clinic 诊所, patient 患者, profession 专业职业, healthcare worker 医护人员
[记] 可数：a dentist。dental 形容词；dentistry 名词（牙科学）

**veterinarian**
英/美 /ˌvetərɪˈneəriən/
n. 兽医
[义] A person trained to treat the injuries and illnesses of animals. 诊治动物伤病的人（small-animal veterinarian 小动物兽医）。口语常简称 vet。
[例] ① The veterinarian examined the farm dog's injured paw. 兽医检查了农场犬受伤的爪子。 ② She decided to become a veterinarian at age ten. 她十岁起就决定当兽医。
[搭] small-animal veterinarian 小动物兽医, farm veterinarian 农场兽医, veterinarian clinic 动物诊所, call a veterinarian 请兽医
[联] doctor 医生（喻）, physician 医生, animal 动物, farmer 农民, clinic 诊所, profession 专业职业, patient 患者
[记] 可数：a veterinarian。口语 vet /vet/。veterinary 形容词

**police officer**
英/美 /pəˈliːs ˌɒfɪsə(r)/
n. 警察；警员
[义] A member of the police force. 警察队伍成员（on-duty police officer 执勤警员，police officer training 警校训练）。比 cop 正式；比 policeman 性别中性。
[例] ① A police officer directed traffic after the accident. 事故后一名警察指挥交通。 ② She joined the force as a police officer in 2018. 她 2018 年入警当警员。
[搭] on-duty police officer 执勤警员, police officer training 警校训练, local police officer 当地警员, police officer uniform 警服
[联] detective 侦探, sheriff 警长, law 法律, patrol 巡逻, officer 警官, security guard 保安, crime 犯罪, duty 值班
[记] 可数：a police officer。口语 cop；旧式 policeman/policewoman。

**firefighter**
英/美 /ˈfaɪəfaɪtə(r)/
n. 消防员
[义] A person whose job is to put out fires and rescue people in emergencies. 灭火并在紧急情况下救人的职业人员（ volunteer firefighter 志愿消防员，firefighter training 消防训练）。
[例] ① Firefighters reached the building within minutes. 消防员数分钟内赶到火场。 ② She works as a firefighter and EMT on the same crew. 她在同一班组当消防员兼 EMT。
[搭] volunteer firefighter 志愿消防员, firefighter training 消防训练, firefighter gear 消防装备, career firefighter 职业消防员
[联] EMT 急救医士, paramedic 急救员, emergency 紧急, rescue 救援, shift 轮班, dangerous occupation 高危职业, hero 英雄
[记] 可数：a firefighter。亦说 fireman（旧式，偏男性）。firefighting 名词

**detective**
英/美 /dɪˈtektɪv/
n. 侦探；刑警
[义] A police officer whose job is to investigate crimes. 负责调查罪案的警务人员（ homicide detective 凶案侦探，private detective 私家侦探）。刑侦与悬疑语境高频。
[例] ① The detective interviewed witnesses all afternoon. 侦探整个下午询问证人。 ② She was promoted from patrol officer to detective. 她从巡逻警员晋升为刑警。
[搭] homicide detective 凶案侦探, private detective 私家侦探, detective work 侦查工作, detective story 侦探小说
[联] police officer 警察, sheriff 警长, crime 犯罪, investigate 调查, law 法律, suspect 嫌疑人, case 案件
[记] 可数：a detective。缩写 det.（口语）。private eye 私家侦探俚语

**security guard**
英/美 /sɪˈkjʊərəti ɡɑːd/
n. 保安；警卫
[义] A person employed to protect a building, property, or people. 受雇保护建筑物、财产或人员的人（night security guard 夜班保安，armed security guard 武装保安）。；此处为固定职业称谓。
[例] ① The security guard checked IDs at the entrance. 保安在入口查证件。 ② Security guards patrol the warehouse at night. 保安夜间巡逻仓库。
[搭] night security guard 夜班保安, armed security guard 武装保安, security guard job 保安工作, hire security guards 雇保安
[联] guard 守卫, police officer 警察, patrol 巡逻, building 大楼, safety 安全, shift 轮班, employee 雇员
[记] 可数：a security guard。亦说 security officer（略正式）

**soldier**
英/美 /ˈsəʊldʒə(r)/
n. 士兵；军人
[义] A person who serves in an army. 在军队服役的人（active-duty soldier 现役军人，soldier training 军事训练）。职业身份词。
[例] ① The soldier returned home after a year overseas. 士兵海外服役一年后回家。 ② Soldiers must follow strict discipline. 军人须遵守严格纪律。
[搭] active-duty soldier 现役军人, soldier training 军事训练, career soldier 职业军人, former soldier 退伍军人
[联] army 军队, officer 军官, veteran 退伍军人, duty 职责, war 战争, serve 服役, discipline 纪律
[记] 可数：a soldier。soldier 作动词旧义「硬撑」——罕用

**librarian**
英/美 /laɪˈbreəriən/
n. 图书管理员
[义] A person who works in a library and helps people find information and books. 在图书馆工作、协助查书与资料的人（school librarian 学校图书管理员）。
[例] ① The librarian helped students with research sources. 图书管理员帮学生找研究资料。 ② She has worked as a librarian for twenty years. 她当图书管理员二十年了。
[搭] school librarian 学校图书管理员, public librarian 公共图书馆馆员, librarian assistance 馆员协助
[联] library 图书馆, book 书, student 学生, information 信息, school 学校, education 教育, archive 档案
[记] 可数：a librarian。librarian 与 library 同根

**chef**
英/美 /ʃef/
n. 厨师；主厨
[义] A professional cook, especially the head cook in a restaurant. 专业厨师，尤指餐厅主厨（executive chef 行政总厨，pastry chef 甜点师）。比 cook 更强调专业训练与职级。
[例] ① The chef designed a new seasonal menu. 主厨设计了新季节菜单。 ② She trained in Paris to become a chef. 她在巴黎受训成为厨师。
[搭] executive chef 行政总厨, head chef 主厨, pastry chef 甜点师, chef's special 主厨推荐
[联] cook 厨师, kitchen 厨房, restaurant 餐厅, cuisine 菜系, hospitality 餐饮服务业, server 服务员, food 食物
[记] 可数：a chef。chef 源自法语「首领」。cook 可指普通炊事员

**cook**
英/美 /kʊk/
n. 厨师；炊事员
v. 烹饪
[义] n. A person who prepares food for eating, especially as a job. 以做饭为职业的人（line cook 流水线厨师，home cook 家庭烹饪者非职业）。v. 烹饪。比 chef 层级通常更低或更泛。
[例] ① The cook prepared meals for two hundred workers. 炊事员为两百名工人备餐。 ② She works as a cook in a school cafeteria. 她在学校食堂当厨师。
[搭] line cook 流水线厨师, short-order cook 快餐厨师, cook meals 做饭, professional cook 职业厨师
[联] chef 主厨, kitchen 厨房, restaurant 餐厅, food 食物, server 服务员, wage 工资, shift 轮班
[记] 可数：a cook。cook 动词极常用。chef 偏高级/主厨

**server**
英/美 /ˈsɜːvə(r)/
n. 服务员（餐馆）
[义] A person who serves food and drinks to customers in a restaurant. 在餐厅为顾客上菜、上 drinks 的人（restaurant server 餐厅服务员）。性别中性；旧式 waiter/waitress 仍见。
[例] ① The server took their order promptly. 服务员很快来点餐。 ② Servers rely on tips for much of their income. 服务员收入很大程度靠小费。
[搭] restaurant server 餐厅服务员, food server 餐饮服务员, server station 服务员工作区, tip your server 给服务员小费
[联] waiter 服务员（男）, waitress 女服务员, chef 厨师, restaurant 餐厅, customer 顾客, tip 小费, shift 轮班

**bartender**
英/美 /ˈbɑːtendə(r)/
n. 调酒师；酒吧服务员
[义] A person who serves drinks, especially alcoholic ones, in a bar. 在酒吧供应饮品、尤指含酒精饮料的人（head bartender 首席调酒师）。餐饮服务业常见职业。
[例] ① The bartender mixed cocktails until closing time. 调酒师营业到打烊。 ② She worked as a bartender while in college. 大学期间她做酒吧服务员。
[搭] head bartender 首席调酒师, bartender job 调酒师工作, bartender training 调酒培训, work as a bartender 当调酒师

**barista**
英/美 /bəˈriːstə/
n. 咖啡师
[义] A person who makes and serves coffee in a coffee shop. 在咖啡店制作、供应咖啡的人（skilled barista 熟练咖啡师）。源自意大利语；全球咖啡馆文化通用词。
[例] ① The barista spelled her name wrong on the cup. 咖啡师在杯子上拼错了她的名字。 ② He trained as a barista before opening his own café. 开咖啡馆前他受过咖啡师培训。

英/美 /kæˈʃɪə(r)/

[义] A person whose job is to receive and pay out money in a shop, bank, or restaurant. 在商店、银行或餐厅收款、找零的人（checkout cashier 收银台收银员）。零售与服务业基础岗位。
[例] ① The cashier scanned each item quickly. 收银员快速扫了每件商品。 ② She worked as a cashier during summer break. 暑假她当收银员。

[例] ① The receptionist directed her to the manager's office. 前台把她引到经理办公室。 ② Receptionists are often the first face clients see. 前台常是客户见到的第一张面孔。

[例] ① The secretary managed the CEO's calendar. 秘书管理 CEO 日程。 ② She worked as a secretary before moving into HR. 进人事前她当过秘书。

[例] ① The clerk filed documents and answered phones. 职员归档文件、接电话。 ② She started as a clerk and rose to supervisor. 她从办事员做到主管。

[例] ① The programmer fixed the bug before release. 程序员发布前修好了漏洞。 ② Programmers often work remotely on agile teams. 程序员常在敏捷团队远程协作。

[例] ① The writer signed a two-book contract. 作家签了两本书的合同。 ② She works as a technical writer for a software company. 她在软件公司当技术写作者。

n. 农民；农场主
[义] A person who owns or manages a farm. 拥有或经营农场的人（ dairy farmer 奶农，family farmer 家庭农场主）。
[例] ① Farmers struggled with drought this season. 本季农民受旱情所困。 ② The farmer sells produce at the local market. 农场主在本地市场卖农产品。

[例] ① The truck driver delivered goods overnight. 卡车司机连夜送货。 ② Drivers in the gig economy set their own hours. 零工经济里的司机自定工时。

v. 驾驶；试点

[例] ① The pilot announced a delay due to weather. 飞行员因天气通知延误。 ② Pilots undergo rigorous training and medical checks. 飞行员须受严格训练与体检。

[例] ① The flight attendant demonstrated safety procedures. 空乘演示安全程序。 ② Flight attendants work irregular shifts across time zones. 空乘跨时区轮班不规律。

[例] ① The courier delivered the contract before noon. 快递员中午前送到了合同。 ② She worked as a courier while studying. 读书期间她做快递。

[例] ① The janitor unlocked the office at six a.m. 校工早上六点打开办公室。 ② Janitors keep workplaces safe and tidy. 清洁工让职场安全整洁。

[例] ① Office cleaners work after employees leave. 办公室清洁工在员工下班后干活。 ② She hires a cleaner twice a week. 她每周请清洁工来两次。

[例] ① The school counselor helped her choose college courses. 学校辅导员帮她选大学课程。 ② Counselors offer confidential support to employees. 咨询师为员工提供保密支持。

[例] ① She joined the firm as an associate consultant. 她以专员顾问身份加入公司。 ② Associates often work long hours before promotion. 专员在晋升前常长时间工作。

[例] ① Educators debated new testing policies. 教育工作者辩论新测评政策。 ② She is a respected educator with thirty years of experience. 她是有三十年经验的教育工作者。

[例] ① Her job title is Senior Product Manager. 她的职位名称是高级产品经理。 ② Titles vary by company even for similar roles. 相似职责在不同公司职称可能不同。

n. 职级；等级
v. 排名；位列
[义] n. A level in an organization or career structure. 组织或职业结构中的层级（ senior rank 高职级， rank and file 普通员工）。v. 排名——多义。
[例] ① She rose through the ranks to vice president. 她从基层逐级升到副总裁。 ② Rank often determines salary bands in the military and large companies. 职级在军队与大公司常决定薪资档。

[义] Chief Financial Officer; the executive responsible for a company's finances. 负责公司财务战略与报告的最高财务高管（report to the CFO 向 CFO 汇报）。C-suite 常见成员。
[例] ① The CFO presented the quarterly earnings call. CFO 主持了季度业绩电话会。 ② She was promoted from controller to CFO. 她从财务总监晋升为 CFO。

[例] ① A roofer inspected the leak after the storm. 暴风雨后屋顶工检查了漏水。 ② Roofers work at heights with safety harnesses. 屋顶工系安全绳在高处作业。

[例] ① The mason rebuilt the chimney brick by brick. 泥瓦匠一块砖一块砖重建了烟囱。 ② Skilled masons are in demand for historic restorations. 历史建筑修复急需熟练石匠。

[例] ① The glazier replaced the shattered storefront window. 玻璃工更换了破碎的橱窗。 ② Glaziers measure openings before ordering panes. 玻璃工订玻璃前先量尺寸。

[例] ① The handyman fixed the door, patched the wall, and changed locks. 杂工修门、补墙、换锁一次搞定。 ② She keeps a handyman on call for the rental units. 她租屋有问题就找一个万能工。

n. 女裁缝；缝纫工
[义] A woman whose job is sewing clothes or doing alterations. 以缝纫、改衣为职业的女性（ seamstress shop 缝纫店）。现代亦用 sewer / tailor 统称。
[例] ① The seamstress hemmed six dresses before the wedding. 女裁缝婚礼前改好了六条裙边。 ② Seamstresses in the factory sewed uniform pockets all day. 工厂缝纫工整天缝制服口袋。
[搭] seamstress shop 缝纫店, skilled seamstress 熟练缝纫工, seamstress alterations 改衣服务
[联] tailor 裁缝, fashion 时尚, craft 手艺, worker 工人, textile 纺织, clothing 服装
[记] 可数：a seamstress。seam 名词（缝线）；sew 动词

**florist**
英/美 /ˈflɒrɪst/
n. 花艺师；花店老板
[义] A person who sells and arranges flowers. 销售并插花的人（ wedding florist 婚礼花艺师， local florist 本地花店）。
[例] ① The florist delivered bouquets to the office lobby. 花艺师把花束送到办公室大堂。 ② She trained as a florist and opened her own shop. 她学花艺后开了自己的店。
[搭] wedding florist 婚礼花艺师, local florist 本地花店, florist delivery 花店配送, florist arrangement 插花作品
[联] gardener 园丁, wedding 婚礼, retail 零售, small business 小生意, creative 创意的, customer 顾客
[记] 可数：a florist。floral 形容词；florist's 花店

**sommelier**
英/美 /sʌməlˈjeɪ/
n. 侍酒师
[义] A trained wine expert who advises customers in restaurants. 在餐厅为客人选酒、侍酒的专业人员（ head sommelier 首席侍酒师）。高端餐饮职业词，源自法语。
[例] ① The sommelier recommended a wine for the tasting menu. 侍酒师为品鉴菜单推荐了一款酒。 ② She became a certified sommelier after years in hospitality. 在餐饮业多年后她成为 certified 侍酒师。
[搭] head sommelier 首席侍酒师, sommelier service 侍酒服务, certified sommelier 认证侍酒师, sommelier pairing 餐酒搭配
[联] chef 厨师, server 服务员, restaurant 餐厅, wine 葡萄酒, hospitality 餐饮服务业, customer 顾客
[记] 可数：a sommelier。法语借词。wine steward 同义较旧

**concierge**
英/美 /ˈkɒnsieəʒ/
n. 礼宾员；门房
[义] A hotel employee who helps guests with bookings, tickets, and local advice. 帮客人订票、安排行程、提供本地信息的酒店员工（ hotel concierge 酒店礼宾）。
[例] ① The concierge booked theater tickets for the guests. 礼宾员为客人订了戏票。 ② Ask the concierge for restaurant recommendations. 向礼宾要餐厅推荐。
[搭] hotel concierge 酒店礼宾, concierge desk 礼宾台, concierge service 礼宾服务, building concierge 公寓门房
[联] receptionist 前台, hotel 酒店, hospitality 餐饮服务业, customer 顾客, travel 旅行, server 服务员
[记] 可数：a concierge。法语借词。亦指高端公寓「门房服务」

**travel agent**
英/美 /ˈtrævl ˌeɪdʒənt/
n. 旅行代理；旅行社职员
[义] A person whose job is arranging travel, flights, and hotels for clients. 为客户安排机票、酒店、行程的人（corporate travel agent 企业差旅代理）。
[例] ① The travel agent rebooked their flights after the delay. 旅行代理在延误后帮他们改签了机票。 ② Travel agents earn commission from airlines and hotels. 旅行社职员从航司和酒店拿佣金。
[搭] corporate travel agent 企业差旅代理, online travel agent 在线旅行社, travel agent booking 代理订票
[联] tour guide 导游, flight attendant 空乘, pilot 飞行员, customer 客户, commission 佣金, hospitality 服务业
[记] 可数：a travel agent。OTA 兴起后实体代理减少，词仍常用

**tour guide**
英/美 /ˈtʊə ˌɡaɪd/
n. 导游
[义] A person who shows tourists around places of interest and explains their history. 带游客参观并讲解的人（ licensed tour guide 持证导游， freelance tour guide 自由导游）。
[例] ① Our tour guide spoke three languages fluently. 我们的导游流利说三种语言。 ② Tour guides rely on tips during peak season. 导游旺季靠小费。
[搭] licensed tour guide 持证导游, freelance tour guide 自由导游, tour guide training 导游培训, city tour guide 城市导游
[联] travel agent 旅行代理, tourist 游客, hospitality 服务业, history 历史, language 语言, tip 小费
[记] 可数：a tour guide。guide 动词/名词

**photographer**
英/美 /fəˈtɒɡrəfə(r)/
n. 摄影师
[义] A person who takes photographs professionally. 以摄影为职业的人（ wedding photographer 婚礼摄影师， freelance photographer 自由摄影师）。
[例] ① The photographer shot product images for the company website. 摄影师为公司网站拍产品图。 ② Photographers often work as freelancers on gig jobs. 摄影师常以自由职业接零活。
[搭] wedding photographer 婚礼摄影师, freelance photographer 自由摄影师, staff photographer 专职摄影师, photographer portfolio 摄影作品集
[联] journalist 记者, artist 艺术家, creative 创意的, freelance 自由职业, media 媒体, client 客户
[记] 可数：a photographer。photography 名词；photographic 形容词

**editor**
英/美 /ˈedɪtə(r)/
n. 编辑
[义] A person who prepares written material for publication by correcting and improving it. 审校、改进文稿以供发表的人（ copy editor 文字编辑， managing editor 执行编辑）。
[例] ① The editor cut two paragraphs from the article. 编辑从文章里删了两段。 ② Editors work with writers on structure and clarity. 编辑与作者一起改结构和清晰度。
[搭] copy editor 文字编辑, managing editor 执行编辑, editor in chief 总编辑, film editor 剪辑师（另一义）
[联] writer 作家, journalist 记者, publisher 出版人, publish 出版, media 媒体, profession 专业职业
[记] 可数：an editor。edit 动词；editorial 社论/形容词

**publisher**
英/美 /ˈpʌblɪʃə(r)/
n. 出版人；出版社
[义] A person or company that prepares and issues books, magazines, or digital content for sale. 出版书刊或数字内容的人或机构（ book publisher 图书出版社）。
[例] ① The publisher acquired rights to her second novel. 出版社买下了她第二部小说的版权。 ② She signed with a major publisher last year. 她去年与大型出版社签约。
[搭] book publisher 图书出版社, digital publisher 数字出版, independent publisher 独立出版社, publisher contract 出版合同
[联] editor 编辑, writer 作家, author 作者, book 书, media 媒体, contract 合同
[记] 可数：a publisher。publish 动词；publishing 名词（出版业）

**copywriter**
英/美 /ˈkɒpiraɪtə(r)/
n. 文案；广告撰稿人
[义] A person who writes advertising or marketing text. 撰写广告与营销文案的人（ freelance copywriter 自由文案， senior copywriter 资深文案）。
[例] ① The copywriter drafted slogans for the new campaign. 文案为新 campaign 写了标语。 ② Copywriters collaborate with designers on ads. 撰稿人与设计师合作做广告。
[搭] freelance copywriter 自由文案, senior copywriter 资深文案, advertising copywriter 广告文案, copywriter portfolio 文案作品集
[联] marketer 营销人员, designer 设计师, writer 作家, advertising 广告, creative 创意的, client 客户

**translator**
英/美 /trænsˈleɪtə(r)/
n. 笔译员；译者
[义] A person who translates written text from one language to another. 将书面文字从一种语言译为另一种的人（ freelance translator 自由译者， sworn translator 宣誓译者）。
[例] ① The translator localized the company manual into Spanish. 译者把公司手册本地化成了西班牙语。 ② Translators often specialize in legal or medical texts. 笔译常专精法律或医学文本。
[搭] freelance translator 自由译者, sworn translator 宣誓译者, literary translator 文学译者, certified translator 认证译者
[联] interpreter 口译员, linguist 语言学家, language 语言, writer 作家, freelance 自由职业, document 文件
