**interpreter**
英/美 /ɪnˈtɜːprətə(r)/
n. 口译员；译员
[义] A person who translates spoken language in real time. 实时翻译口语的人（ conference interpreter 会议口译， sign language interpreter 手语翻译）。
[例] ① An interpreter joined the negotiation by video. 一名口译通过视频加入谈判。 ② Interpreters work in courts, hospitals, and summits. 口译员在法庭、医院、峰会工作。
[搭] conference interpreter 会议口译, sign language interpreter 手语翻译, court interpreter 法庭口译, simultaneous interpreter 同声传译
[联] translator 笔译员, language 语言, meeting 会议, diplomat 外交官, freelance 自由职业, communication 沟通
[记] 可数：an interpreter。interpret 动词。simultaneous interpreting 同声传译

**publicist**
英/美 /ˈpʌblɪsɪst/
n. 公关；宣传专员
[义] A person whose job is generating publicity for a client or company. 为客户或公司制造媒体曝光的人（celebrity publicist 名人公关，PR publicist 公关专员）。
[例] ① The publicist arranged interviews after the product launch. 公关在产品发布后安排了采访。 ② Publicists manage crises when bad press hits. 负面报道来袭时宣传专员处理危机。
[搭] celebrity publicist 名人公关, PR publicist 公关专员, music publicist 音乐公关, publicist firm 公关公司
[联] marketer 营销人员, journalist 记者, media 媒体, client 客户, company 公司, communication 沟通

**event planner**
英/美 /ɪˈvent ˌplænə(r)/
n. 活动策划师
[义] A person who organizes weddings, conferences, and other events. 组织婚礼、会议等活动的人（corporate event planner 企业活动策划，wedding planner 婚礼策划）。
[例] ① The event planner coordinated vendors, schedules, and budgets. 活动策划协调供应商、日程与预算。 ② Event planners work nights and weekends during season. 旺季活动策划师常夜间周末上班。
[搭] wedding planner 婚礼策划, corporate event planner 企业活动策划, event planner job 活动策划岗位

**talent agent**
英/美 /ˈtælənt ˌeɪdʒənt/
n. 艺人经纪人
[义] A person who finds work for actors, musicians, or other performers. 为演员、音乐人等表演者接洽工作的人（ Hollywood talent agent 好莱坞经纪人）。；此处为固定职业称谓。
[例] ① Her talent agent negotiated the film contract. 她的经纪人谈下了电影合同。 ② Talent agents take a percentage of client earnings. 艺人经纪从客户收入中抽成。
[搭] Hollywood talent agent 好莱坞经纪人, talent agent commission 经纪佣金, sign with a talent agent 签约经纪人
[联] actor 演员, musician 音乐家, client 客户, contract 合同, commission 佣金, entertainment 娱乐
[记] 可数：a talent agent。亦说 booking agent（演出接洽）

**makeup artist**
英/美 /ˈmeɪkʌp ˌɑːtɪst/

[义] A person who applies makeup professionally for film, TV, fashion, or weddings. 为影视、时尚、婚礼等专业化妆的人（ bridal makeup artist 新娘化妆师）。
[例] ① The makeup artist prepared the cast before filming. 化妆师拍摄前给演员化妆。 ② Makeup artists often work freelance on gig jobs. 化妆师常零工接活。

[联] hairdresser 美发师, stylist 造型师, actor 演员, artist 艺术家, fashion 时尚, freelance 自由职业

**stylist**
英/美 /ˈstaɪlɪst/
n. 造型师
[义] A person who advises on or creates clothing, hair, or overall appearance for clients. 为客户设计服装、发型或整体形象的人（ personal stylist 私人造型师， fashion stylist 时尚造型师）。
[例] ① The stylist chose outfits for the magazine shoot. 造型师为杂志拍摄选造型。 ② Personal stylists shop with executive clients. 私人造型师陪高管客户购物。

英/美 /ˈmɒdl/
n. 模特
v. 做模特；建模
[义] n. A person who wears clothes to display them for photos, shows, or advertising. 展示服装、为广告或秀场走台的人（ fashion model 时装模特， role model 榜样——另一义）。
[例] ① The model walked the runway in the designer's new line. 模特穿着设计师新系列走秀。 ② Models often work through agencies on short contracts. 模特常通过经纪公司签短期合同。

**product manager**
英/美 /ˈprɒdʌkt ˌmænɪdʒə(r)/

[义] A person responsible for guiding a product's development and strategy. 负责产品方向、功能与上市策略的人（ senior product manager 高级产品经理， PM 缩写）。
[例] ① The product manager prioritized features for the next release. 产品经理排了下一版功能优先级。 ② Product managers bridge engineers and customers. 产品经理在工程师与客户间搭桥。

[联] project manager 项目经理, developer 开发者, designer 设计师, startup 初创, tech 科技, customer 客户

**project manager**
英/美 /ˈprɒdʒekt ˌmænɪdʒə(r)/
n. 项目经理
[义] A person who plans and oversees projects to meet goals on time and budget. 规划并监督项目按时按预算完成的人（ construction project manager 工程项目经理）。
[例] ① The project manager updated the deadline after scope changes. 范围变更后项目经理更新了截止日期。 ② Project managers run stand-ups and track risks. 项目经理开站会、跟踪风险。
[搭] construction project manager 工程项目经理, IT project manager IT 项目经理, senior project manager 高级项目经理
[联] product manager 产品经理, coordinator 协调员, deadline 截止日期, team 团队, budget 预算, client 客户

**data scientist**
英/美 /ˈdeɪtə ˌsaɪəntɪst/
n. 数据科学家
[义] A person who analyzes complex data to help organizations make decisions. 分析复杂数据、辅助组织决策的人（ senior data scientist 高级数据科学家）。
[例] ① Data scientists built a churn prediction model for the company. 数据科学家为公司建了流失预测模型。 ② The team hired two data scientists last quarter. 上季度团队招了两名数据科学家。
[搭] senior data scientist 高级数据科学家, data scientist team 数据科学团队, hire a data scientist 招聘数据科学家
[联] analyst 分析师, scientist 科学家, data 数据, engineer 工程师, tech 科技, machine learning 机器学习
[记] 可数：a data scientist。与 data analyst 比更偏建模与算法

**bookkeeper**
英/美 /ˈbʊkkiːpə(r)/
n. 记账员
[义] A person whose job is keeping records of a business's financial transactions. 记录企业收支账目的人（ freelance bookkeeper 自由记账， small business bookkeeper 小企业记账）。
[例] ① The bookkeeper reconciled accounts at month-end. 记账员月底对账。 ② Small firms often outsource to a bookkeeper. 小公司常外包给记账员。
[搭] freelance bookkeeper 自由记账, small business bookkeeper 小企业记账, hire a bookkeeper 雇记账员
[联] accountant 会计, finance 财务, payroll 工资单, clerk 职员, small business 小生意, records 记录
[记] 可数：a bookkeeper。bookkeeping 名词。；bookkeeper 层级通常更低

**auditor**
英/美 /ˈɔːdɪtə(r)/
n. 审计师
[义] A person who officially examines an organization's accounts. 正式审查机构账目的人（ internal auditor 内审， external auditor 外审， IRS auditor 国税审计）。
[例] ① External auditors issued a clean opinion on the financials. 外审对财报出具了无保留意见。 ② The auditor sampled expense reports for compliance. 审计师抽样检查费用报告合规性。
[搭] internal auditor 内审, external auditor 外审, financial auditor 财务审计, auditor report 审计报告
[联] accountant 会计, finance 财务, compliance 合规, company 公司, records 记录, tax 税
[记] 可数：an auditor。audit 名词/动词

**actuary**
英/美 /ˈæktʃuəri/
n. 精算师
[义] A person who calculates insurance risks and premiums using mathematics and statistics. 用数学统计计算保险风险与保费的人（ certified actuary 持证精算师）。
[例] ① Actuaries model life expectancy for pension plans. 精算师为养老金计划建模预期寿命。 ② She passed exams to become a fellow actuary. 她通过考试成为 fellow 精算师。
[搭] certified actuary 持证精算师, insurance actuary 保险精算师, actuary exam 精算考试, fellow actuary 精算师 fellow
[联] accountant 会计, analyst 分析师, insurance 保险, finance 金融, statistician 统计师, risk 风险
[记] 可数：an actuary。actuarial 形容词。精算考试周期长、难度大

**economist**
英/美 /ɪˈkɒnəmɪst/
n. 经济学家
[义] A person who studies the economy and advises on economic policy or markets. 研究经济、为政策或市场提供分析的人（ chief economist 首席经济学家， development economist 发展经济学家）。
[例] ① The bank's economist forecast slower growth next year. 银行经济学家预测明年增长放缓。 ② Economists debated minimum wage effects in the panel. 小组讨论中经济学家辩论最低工资影响。
[搭] chief economist 首席经济学家, development economist 发展经济学家, labor economist 劳动经济学家, economist forecast 经济预测
[联] analyst 分析师, finance 金融, policy 政策, research 研究, university 大学, government 政府
[记] 可数：an economist。economics 名词；economic 形容词

**statistician**
英/美 /ˌstætɪˈstɪʃn/
n. 统计师
[义] A person who collects and analyzes numerical data. 收集、分析数值数据的人（ biostatistician 生物统计师， government statistician 政府统计员）。
[例] ① The statistician designed the survey sampling method. 统计师设计了调查抽样方法。 ② Statisticians verified the clinical trial results. 统计师核实了临床试验结果。
[搭] biostatistician 生物统计师, chief statistician 首席统计师, government statistician 政府统计员
[联] analyst 分析师, data scientist 数据科学家, research 研究, scientist 科学家, math 数学, survey 调查
[记] 可数：a statistician。statistics 名词；statistical 形容词

**trader**
英/美 /ˈtreɪdə(r)/
n. 交易员；商人
[义] A person who buys and sells stocks, currencies, or commodities for profit. 为盈利买卖股票、外汇或商品的人（floor trader 场内交易员，day trader 日内交易员）。亦指「商人」——另一义。
[例] ① Traders reacted quickly to the earnings surprise. 交易员对盈利意外迅速反应。 ② He worked as an energy trader in Houston. 他在休斯顿做能源交易员。
[搭] day trader 日内交易员, floor trader 场内交易员, energy trader 能源交易员, stock trader 股票交易员
[联] broker 经纪人, finance 金融, market 市场, investor 投资者, analyst 分析师, commission 佣金
[记] 可数：a trader。trade 动词/名词

**insurance agent**
英/美 /ɪnˈʃʊərəns ˌeɪdʒənt/
n. 保险代理人
[义] A person who sells insurance policies to individuals or businesses. 向个人或企业销售保单的人（ licensed insurance agent 持证保险代理）。
[例] ① The insurance agent explained coverage options for the startup. 保险代理向初创解释保障选项。 ② Insurance agents earn commission on new policies. 保险代理靠新单拿佣金。

**loan officer**

n. 信贷员
[义] A person who evaluates and approves loan applications at a bank. 在银行评估、审批贷款申请的人（ mortgage loan officer 房贷信贷员）。
[例] ① The loan officer reviewed their credit history. 信贷员审查了他们的信用记录。 ② Loan officers explain rates and repayment terms. 信贷员解释利率与还款条件。

**property manager**
英/美 /ˈprɒpəti ˌmænɪdʒə(r)/
n. 物业经理
[义] A person who manages rental properties for owners. 替业主管理出租物业的人（ residential property manager 住宅物业经理）。
[例] ① The property manager handled repairs and tenant complaints. 物业经理处理维修与租客投诉。 ② Property managers collect rent and schedule inspections. 物业经理收租、安排检查。

英/美 /ˈstɔː ˌmænɪdʒə(r)/
n. 店长
[义] A person in charge of a retail store's daily operations and staff. 负责零售店日常运营与员工的人（ assistant store manager 副店长）。
[例] ① The store manager scheduled shifts for the holiday rush. 店长为假日高峰排班。 ② Store managers report sales targets to district managers. 店长向区域经理汇报销售目标。

英/美 /ˌɒpəˈreɪʃnz ˌmænɪdʒə(r)/
n. 运营经理
[义] A person who oversees daily business operations to improve efficiency. 监督日常业务运营、提升效率的人（ regional operations manager 区域运营经理）。
[例] ① The operations manager streamlined the warehouse workflow. 运营经理优化了仓库流程。 ② Operations managers track KPIs across departments. 运营经理跨部门跟踪 KPI。

[例] ① The general manager approved the new staffing plan. 总经理批准了新人员计划。 ② She was promoted from store manager to general manager. 她从店长升为总经理。

[义] A person who leads a small team without necessarily being a senior manager. 带领小团队的人，未必是高级经理（ team leader promotion 组长晋升）。
[例] ① The team leader assigned tasks at the daily stand-up. 组长在每日站会派活。 ② She became team leader after two years as an engineer. 当工程师两年后她成了团队负责人。

[义] Chief Technology Officer; the executive responsible for technology strategy. 负责公司技术战略的最高技术高管（ startup CTO 初创 CTO）。

英/美 /ˌfɪzɪkl ˈθerəpɪst/
n. 物理治疗师
[义] American: a person who helps patients recover movement and manage pain after injury. 帮助伤病患者恢复运动、管理疼痛的治疗师（ licensed physical therapist 持证 PT）。英式 physiotherapist。
[例] ① The physical therapist guided knee exercises after surgery. 物理治疗师术后指导膝关节练习。 ② Physical therapists work in hospitals and clinics. 物理治疗师在医院与诊所工作。
[搭] licensed physical therapist 持证 PT, physical therapist clinic 物理治疗诊所, pediatric physical therapist 儿童 PT
[联] nurse 护士, physician 医生, rehabilitation 康复, patient 患者, healthcare worker 医护人员, injury 受伤
[记] 可数：a physical therapist。缩写 PT。physiotherapist 英式同义

**occupational therapist**
英/美 /ˌɒkjuˈpeɪʃənl ˈθerəpɪst/
n. 作业治疗师
[义] A person who helps people regain skills for daily living and work after illness or injury. 帮助患者在疾病或受伤后恢复日常生活与工作能力的治疗师（ pediatric OT 儿童作业治疗）。
[例] ① The occupational therapist taught adaptive cooking techniques. 作业治疗师教了适应性的烹饪方法。 ② Occupational therapists recommend workplace ergonomics. 作业治疗师建议职场人体工学调整。
[搭] licensed occupational therapist 持证 OT, pediatric occupational therapist 儿童 OT, occupational therapy clinic 作业治疗诊所
[联] physical therapist 物理治疗师, rehabilitation 康复, patient 患者, workplace 职场, healthcare worker 医护人员, skill 技能
[记] 可数：an occupational therapist。缩写 OT。occupational 此处指「日常生活活动」

**speech therapist**
英/美 /ˈspiːtʃ ˌθerəpɪst/
n. 言语治疗师
[义] A person who treats speech and language disorders. 治疗言语与语言障碍的治疗师（ school speech therapist 学校言语治疗师）。亦说 speech-language pathologist。
[例] ① The speech therapist worked on the child's articulation. 言语治疗师练孩子的发音。 ② Speech therapists help stroke patients regain speech. 言语治疗师帮中风患者恢复说话。
[搭] school speech therapist 学校言语治疗师, pediatric speech therapist 儿童言语治疗, speech therapy session 言语治疗课
[联] teacher 教师, patient 患者, healthcare worker 医护人员, child 儿童, communication 沟通, school 学校
[记] 可数：a speech therapist。speech-language pathologist 更正式全称

**optometrist**
英/美 /ɒpˈtɒmɪtrɪst/
n. 验光师
[义] A person who examines eyes and prescribes glasses or contact lenses. 检查视力、配镜的人（ visit an optometrist 看验光师）。与 ophthalmologist（眼科医生，可手术）不同。
[例] ① The optometrist updated her prescription for new glasses. 验光师更新了配镜度数。 ② Optometrists screen for glaucoma during exams. 验光师检查时筛查青光眼。
[搭] visit an optometrist 看验光师, optometrist exam 验光检查, licensed optometrist 持证验光师
[联] physician 医生, healthcare worker 医护人员, patient 患者, clinic 诊所, eye 眼睛, glasses 眼镜
[记] 可数：an optometrist。optometry 名词。ophthalmologist 为眼科医师（医学章/身体章）

**psychiatrist**
英/美 /saɪˈkaɪətrɪst/
n. 精神科医生
[义] A medical doctor who diagnoses and treats mental illness and can prescribe medication. 诊断、治疗精神疾病并可开药的全科医生（ child psychiatrist 儿童精神科）。psychologist 不可开药——职能不同。
[例] ① The psychiatrist adjusted her medication after the review. 精神科医生复查后调整了用药。 ② Psychiatrists often work with therapists on care plans. 精神科常与治疗师协作制定方案。
[搭] child psychiatrist 儿童精神科, consulting psychiatrist 会诊精神科, psychiatrist appointment 精神科预约
[联] psychologist 心理学家, therapist 治疗师, physician 医生, mental health 心理健康, patient 患者, healthcare worker 医护人员
[记] 可数：a psychiatrist。psychiatry 名词。psychiatrist = MD；psychologist 多为 PhD/硕士

**dental hygienist**
英/美 /ˈdentl haɪdʒiːnɪst/
n. 牙科洁牙师
[义] A person who cleans teeth and teaches oral hygiene under a dentist's supervision. 在牙医监督下洁牙并指导口腔卫生的人（ registered dental hygienist 注册洁牙师， RDH）。
[例] ① The dental hygienist scaled tartar during the cleaning. 洁牙师洗牙时刮了牙石。 ② Dental hygienists take X-rays in many offices. 许多诊所洁牙师可拍 X 光。
[搭] registered dental hygienist 注册洁牙师, dental hygienist cleaning 洁牙, dental hygienist license 洁牙师执照
[联] dentist 牙医, patient 患者, clinic 诊所, healthcare worker 医护人员, teeth 牙齿, hygiene 卫生
[记] 可数：a dental hygienist。hygienist 源自 hygiene（卫生）

**medical assistant**
英/美 /ˈmedɪkl əˈsɪstənt/
n. 医疗助理
[义] A person who performs clinical and administrative tasks in clinics and doctors' offices. 在诊所与医生办公室做临床与行政辅助的人（ certified medical assistant 认证医疗助理， CMA）。
[例] ① The medical assistant took vitals before the physician entered. 医疗助理在医生进门前量了生命体征。 ② Medical assistants schedule appointments and draw blood. 医疗助理预约、抽血。
[搭] certified medical assistant 认证医疗助理, medical assistant program 医疗助理培训, clinical medical assistant 临床医疗助理
[联] nurse 护士, physician 医生, clinic 诊所, patient 患者, healthcare worker 医护人员, receptionist 前台
[记] 可数：a medical assistant。缩写 CMA/MA。不同于 physician assistant（PA，更高权限）

**phlebotomist**
英/美 /fləˈbɒtəmɪst/
n. 采血员
[义] A person trained to draw blood for tests, donations, or research. 受训为患者抽血用于检验、献血或研究的人（ hospital phlebotomist 医院采血员）。
[例] ① The phlebotomist labeled tubes carefully after drawing blood. 采血员抽血后仔细贴标签。 ② Phlebotomists calm nervous patients during venipuncture. 采血员在静脉穿刺时安抚紧张患者。
[搭] hospital phlebotomist 医院采血员, certified phlebotomist 认证采血员, phlebotomist training 采血培训
[联] nurse 护士, lab 实验室, patient 患者, hospital 医院, healthcare worker 医护人员, technician 技术员
[记] 可数：a phlebotomist。phlebotomy 名词（静脉抽血术）

**doula**
英/美 /ˈduːlə/
n. 导乐；陪产员
[义] A person trained to support a woman emotionally and physically during childbirth. 在分娩过程中提供情感与身体支持的人（ birth doula 分娩导乐， postpartum doula 产后导乐）。与 midwife（医疗接生）分工不同。
[例] ① Their doula stayed through labor and coached breathing. 导乐陪产全程指导呼吸。 ② Doulas do not deliver babies or give medical advice. 导乐不接生、不提供医疗建议。
[搭] birth doula 分娩导乐, postpartum doula 产后导乐, hire a doula 请导乐, doula support 导乐支持
[联] midwife 助产士, nurse 护士, caregiver 照护者, birth 分娩, patient 患者, support 支持
[记] 可数：a doula。源自希腊语「女仆/侍女」。非 licensed medical role

**funeral director**
英/美 /ˈfjuːnərəl dɪˌrektə(r)/
n. 殡葬师
[义] A person whose business is arranging funerals and preparing bodies for burial or cremation. 安排葬礼、为安葬或火化做准备的人（ licensed funeral director 持证殡葬师）。亦说 mortician。
[例] ① The funeral director met with the family to plan the service. 殡葬师与家属商量仪式安排。 ② Funeral directors handle paperwork and logistics. 殡葬师处理文书与后勤。
[搭] licensed funeral director 持证殡葬师, funeral director services 殡葬服务, local funeral director 本地殡葬师
[联] caregiver 照护者, family 家庭, ceremony 典礼, bereavement 丧亲, community 社区, service 服务
[记] 可数：a funeral director。funeral home 殡仪馆。embalmer 为 embalming 专工

**diplomat**
英/美 /ˈdɪpləmæt/
n. 外交官
[义] A person who represents their country abroad and handles international relations. 代表国家从事对外关系的人（ career diplomat 职业外交官， UN diplomat 联合国外交官）。
[例] ① The diplomat negotiated a trade agreement in Geneva. 外交官在日内瓦谈判贸易协定。 ② Diplomats work at embassies and consulates. 外交官在使馆与领馆工作。
[搭] career diplomat 职业外交官, senior diplomat 高级外交官, diplomat passport 外交护照, UN diplomat 联合国外交官
[联] ambassador 大使, government 政府, negotiation 谈判, international 国际的, policy 政策, travel 旅行
[记] 可数：a diplomat。diplomatic 形容词。diplomacy 名词（外交）

**ambassador**
英/美 /æmˈbæsədə(r)/
n. 大使
[义] A diplomat of the highest rank sent to represent their country in another nation. 派往他国代表国家的最高级别外交官（ US ambassador 美国大使， brand ambassador 品牌大使——营销义）。
[例] ① The ambassador presented credentials to the host government. 大使向东道国政府递交国书。 ② She served as ambassador to Japan for four years. 她担任驻日大使四年。
[搭] US ambassador 美国大使, ambassador to 驻……大使, brand ambassador 品牌大使（营销）, ambassador program 大使项目
[联] diplomat 外交官, embassy 大使馆, government 政府, international 国际的, representative 代表, negotiation 谈判
[记] 可数：an ambassador。brand ambassador 为营销职位——另一义

**civil servant**
英/美 /ˈsɪvl ˈsɜːvənt/
n. 公务员
[义] A person who works for the government in a non-military role. 在政府从事非军事工作的职员（ senior civil servant 高级公务员）。英式常用；美式 public servant / government employee。
[例] ① Civil servants must follow strict ethics rules. 公务员须遵守严格伦理规范。 ② She spent thirty years as a civil servant in education policy. 她在教育政策领域做公务员三十年。
[搭] senior civil servant 高级公务员, career civil servant 职业公务员, civil servant exam 公务员考试
[联] government 政府, public sector 公共部门, policy 政策, employee 雇员, bureaucrat 官僚, official 官员
[记] 可数：a civil servant。the civil service 名词（公务员体系）

**politician**
英/美 /ˌpɒləˈtɪʃn/
n. 政治家；政客
[义] A person whose job is politics, especially as an elected official. 以政治为职业的人，尤指民选官员（ local politician 地方政客， career politician 职业政治家）。
[例] ① The politician promised better employment programs. 这位政客承诺更好的就业项目。 ② Politicians debated minimum wage on live TV. 政治家在电视直播辩论最低工资。
[搭] local politician 地方政客, career politician 职业政治家, elected politician 民选政客
[联] mayor 市长, government 政府, policy 政策, election 选举, public 公众, diplomat 外交官
[记] 可数：a politician。politics 名词；political 形容词

**mayor**
英/美 /meə(r)/
n. 市长
[义] The elected head of a city or town government. 城市或城镇民选行政首长（ deputy mayor 副市长， mayor's office 市长办公室）。
[例] ① The mayor announced funding for public transit workers. 市长宣布为公交职工提供资金。 ② Mayors often appoint department heads. 市长常任命部门负责人。
[搭] deputy mayor 副市长, mayor's office 市长办公室, elected mayor 民选市长, city mayor 市长
[联] politician 政治家, government 政府, city 城市, public 公众, civil servant 公务员, election 选举
[记] 可数：a mayor。mayoral 形容词（mayoral election 市长选举）

**curator**
英/美 /kjʊəˈreɪtə(r)/
n. 策展人
[义] A person who manages a museum collection and organizes exhibitions. 管理馆藏、策划展览的人（ museum curator 博物馆策展人， art curator 艺术策展）。
[例] ① The curator selected works for the spring show. 策展人为春季展选作品。 ② Curators research provenance and conservation needs. 策展人研究来源与保护需求。
[搭] museum curator 博物馆策展人, art curator 艺术策展, chief curator 首席策展, curator exhibition 策展展览
[联] museum 博物馆, artist 艺术家, historian 历史学家, archivist 档案员, culture 文化, exhibition 展览
[记] 可数：a curator。curate 动词；curation 名词

**archivist**
英/美 /ˈɑːkɪvɪst/
n. 档案管理员
[义] A person who collects, organizes, and preserves historical records and documents. 收集、整理、保存历史档案的人（ university archivist 大学档案员）。
[例] ① The archivist digitized letters from the nineteenth century. 档案员把十九世纪信件数字化。 ② Archivists help researchers access restricted collections. 档案管理员帮研究者调阅限制馆藏。
[搭] university archivist 大学档案员, digital archivist 数字档案员, archivist collection 档案馆藏
[联] librarian 图书管理员, historian 历史学家, curator 策展人, records 记录, museum 博物馆, research 研究
[记] 可数：an archivist。archive 名词/动词

**historian**
英/美 /hɪˈstɔːriən/
n. 历史学家
[义] A person who studies and writes about the past. 研究并书写历史的人（ academic historian 学术历史学家， public historian 公共历史学家）。
[例] ① The historian published a book on labor unions. 这位历史学家出版了劳动工会史。 ② Historians rely on primary sources and archives. 历史学家依赖一手资料与档案。
[搭] academic historian 学术历史学家, labor historian 劳工史学家, public historian 公共历史学家
[联] archivist 档案员, professor 教授, researcher 研究员, writer 作家, university 大学, museum 博物馆
[记] 可数：a historian。history 名词；historic/historical 形容词

**archaeologist**
英/美 /ˌɑːkiˈɒlədʒɪst/
n. 考古学家
[义] A person who studies human history through excavation and artifacts. 通过发掘与文物研究人类历史的人（ field archaeologist 田野考古学家）。
[例] ① Archaeologists mapped the ancient settlement last summer. 去年夏天考古学家测绘了古聚落。 ② The archaeologist cataloged pottery shards at the site. 考古学家在遗址登记陶片。
[搭] field archaeologist 田野考古, marine archaeologist 水下考古, archaeologist excavation 考古发掘
[联] historian 历史学家, scientist 科学家, researcher 研究员, museum 博物馆, university 大学, site 遗址
[记] 可数：an archaeologist。archaeology 名词。美式 archaeology；英式 archaeology/archaeology

**geologist**
英/美 /dʒiˈɒlədʒɪst/
n. 地质学家
[义] A person who studies rocks, earth processes, and natural resources. 研究岩石、地球过程与自然资源的人（ petroleum geologist 石油地质学家）。
[例] ① Geologists assessed earthquake risk for the new factory. 地质学家评估了新工厂的地震风险。 ② A geologist consulted on the mining project. 地质学家为采矿项目提供咨询。
[搭] petroleum geologist 石油地质学家, field geologist 野外地质师, engineering geologist 工程地质师
[联] scientist 科学家, engineer 工程师, miner 矿工, environment 环境, research 研究, oil 石油
[记] 可数：a geologist。geology 名词；geological 形容词

**chemist**
英/美 /ˈkemɪst/
n. 化学家；药剂师（英式）
[义] A person who studies chemistry or works in chemical research. 研究化学或从事化学研究的人（research chemist 研发化学家）。英式 chemist 亦指「药店/药剂师」——地域义不同。
[例] ① The chemist developed a safer solvent for the factory. 化学家为工厂开发了更安全的溶剂。 ② Chemists in the lab ran purity tests all week. 实验室化学家整周做纯度测试。
[搭] research chemist 研发化学家, analytical chemist 分析化学家, industrial chemist 工业化学家
[联] scientist 科学家, lab 实验室, engineer 工程师, pharmacist 药剂师, research 研究, technician 技术员
[记] 可数：a chemist。chemistry 名词。英式 chemist's = pharmacy

**researcher**
英/美 /rɪˈsɑːtʃə(r)/
n. 研究员
[义] A person who conducts systematic research in academia, industry, or government. 在学界、产业或政府从事系统研究的人（senior researcher 高级研究员）。；此处强调职业身份。
[例] ① Researchers published findings on remote work productivity. 研究员发表了远程办公生产力研究。 ② She works as a researcher at a pharmaceutical company. 她在制药公司当研究员。
[搭] senior researcher 高级研究员, market researcher 市场研究员, academic researcher 学术研究员, lead researcher 首席研究员
[联] scientist 科学家, professor 教授, lab 实验室, data 数据, university 大学, analyst 分析师
；本卷强调职业称谓。research 不可数

**illustrator**
英/美 /ˈɪləstreɪtə(r)/
n. 插画师
[义] A person who draws pictures for books, magazines, or products. 为书籍、杂志或产品绘制插图的人（ children's book illustrator 童书插画师）。
[例] ① The illustrator delivered cover art before the print deadline. 插画师在印刷截止前交了封面。 ② Freelance illustrators often work with publishers and ad agencies. 自由插画师常与出版社、广告公司合作。
[搭] book illustrator 书籍插画师, freelance illustrator 自由插画师, medical illustrator 医学插画师
[联] artist 艺术家, designer 设计师, writer 作家, publisher 出版人, creative 创意的, freelance 自由职业
[记] 可数：an illustrator。illustrate 动词；illustration 名词

**animator**
英/美 /ˈænɪmeɪtə(r)/
n. 动画师
[义] A person who creates animated films, videos, or games. 制作动画电影、视频或游戏的人（ 3D animator 三维动画师， character animator 角色动画师）。
[例] ① Animators rigged characters for the new series. 动画师为新系列做了角色绑定。 ② She works as a 3D animator at a game studio. 她在游戏工作室当 3D 动画师。
[搭] 3D animator 三维动画师, character animator 角色动画师, freelance animator 自由动画师
[联] artist 艺术家, designer 设计师, film 电影, creative 创意的, developer 开发者, freelance 自由职业
[记] 可数：an animator。animation 名词；animate 动词

**videographer**
英/美 /ˈvɪdiəʊɡrəfə(r)/
n. 摄像师
[义] A person who records video for events, marketing, or film. 为活动、营销或影视录制视频的人（ wedding videographer 婚礼摄像， freelance videographer 自由摄像）。
[例] ① The videographer shot b-roll for the company profile. 摄像师为公司简介拍了素材。 ② Videographers edit footage for social media campaigns. 摄像师为社交媒体 campaign 剪辑素材。
[搭] wedding videographer 婚礼摄像, freelance videographer 自由摄像, corporate videographer 企业摄像
[联] photographer 摄影师, filmmaker 电影人, editor 编辑, creative 创意的, freelance 自由职业, media 媒体
[记] 可数：a videographer。video + grapher

**buyer**
英/美 /ˈbaɪə(r)/
n. 采购员；买手
[义] A person whose job is selecting and purchasing goods for a company or store. 为公司或商店选购、采购商品的人（ retail buyer 零售买手， procurement buyer 采购员）。
[例] ① The buyer negotiated lower prices with vendors. 采购员与供应商谈低了价格。 ② Fashion buyers attend trade shows each season. 时尚买手每季参加订货会。
[搭] retail buyer 零售买手, senior buyer 高级采购, procurement buyer 采购员, buyer negotiation 采购谈判
[联] vendor 供应商, merchandiser 陈列师, store 商店, retail 零售, company 公司, negotiate 谈判
[记] 可数：a buyer。buying 名词（采购职能）。buyer vs. purchasing manager：层级不同

**merchandiser**
英/美 /ˈmɜːtʃəndaɪzə(r)/
n. 陈列师；商品规划员
[义] A person who plans product displays and stock levels in retail. 规划零售陈列与库存水平的人（visual merchandiser 视觉陈列师）。
[例] ① The merchandiser reset shelves for the holiday display. 陈列师为节日陈列重置货架。 ② Merchandisers analyze sales data to plan orders. 陈列师分析销售数据以规划订货。
[搭] visual merchandiser 视觉陈列师, retail merchandiser 零售陈列师, merchandiser plan 陈列计划

[记] 可数：a merchandiser。merchandise 名词/动词（商品；推销）

**procurement officer**
英/美 /prəˈtʃʊəmənt ˌɒfɪsə(r)/
n. 采购官
[义] A person who manages an organization's purchasing of goods and services. 管理机构物资与服务采购的人（ chief procurement officer 首席采购官， CPO）。
[例] ① The procurement officer ran a competitive bid for IT services. 采购官为 IT 服务进行招标。 ② Procurement officers enforce supplier compliance rules. 采购官执行供应商合规规定。
[搭] chief procurement officer 首席采购官, procurement officer role 采购官岗位, public procurement officer 公共采购官

**customer service representative**
英/美 /ˈkʌstəmə ˈsɜːvɪs reprɪˈzentətɪv/
n. 客服代表
[义] A person who helps customers by phone, chat, or email with questions and problems. 通过电话、聊天或邮件帮客户解决问题的人（ customer service rep 客服， CSR 缩写）。
[例] ① Customer service representatives handled refund requests all day. 客服代表整天处理退款请求。 ② She started as a CSR before moving to sales. 她先做客服再转销售。

[记] 可数：a customer service representative。缩写 CSR

**call center agent**
英/美 /ˈkɔːl ˌsentə ˌeɪdʒənt/
n. 呼叫中心坐席
[义] A person who handles incoming or outgoing phone calls for a company. 为公司接打客户电话的人（inbound call center agent 呼入坐席，outbound telemarketer 外呼——另一义）。
[例] ① Call center agents follow scripts for billing inquiries. 呼叫中心坐席按脚本处理账单咨询。 ② The company hired bilingual call center agents. 公司雇了双语坐席。

[记] 可数：a call center agent。call center 呼叫中心

**telemarketer**
英/美 /ˈtelimɑːkɪtə(r)/
n. 电话营销员
[义] A person who sells products or services by calling people by phone. 通过电话向他人推销产品或服务的人（ outbound telemarketer 外呼营销）。常含打扰意味；行业受严格法规限制。
[例] ① Telemarketers must honor do-not-call lists. 电话营销员须遵守拒接名单。 ② He worked briefly as a telemarketer between jobs. 两份工作间隙他短暂做过电销。

**auctioneer**
英/美 /ˌɔːkʃəˈnɪə(r)/
n. 拍卖师
[义] A person who conducts auctions by calling out bids. 主持拍卖、喊价的人（ licensed auctioneer 持证拍卖师）。
[例] ① The auctioneer sold the painting in under two minutes. 拍卖师两分钟内拍出画作。 ② Auctioneers need quick speech and crowd control skills. 拍卖师须语速快、控场能力强。
[搭] licensed auctioneer 持证拍卖师, auctioneer chant 拍卖师快速喊价, estate auctioneer 遗产拍卖师
[联] broker 经纪人, salesperson 销售员, bid 出价, sale 销售, client 客户, commission 佣金

**appraiser**
英/美 /əˈpreɪzə(r)/
n. 估价师
[义] A person who estimates the value of property, jewelry, or assets. 估算房产、珠宝或资产价值的人（ real estate appraiser 房产估价师， insurance appraiser 保险估价师）。
[例] ① The appraiser valued the home before the mortgage closed. 房贷结案前估价师评估了房屋。 ② Appraisers must remain independent from brokers. 估价师须独立于经纪人。
[搭] real estate appraiser 房产估价师, independent appraiser 独立估价师, appraiser report 估价报告
[联] real estate agent 房产经纪, broker 经纪人, property 房产, insurance 保险, finance 金融, client 客户

**home inspector**
英/美 /həʊm ɪnˈspektə(r)/
n. 房屋检验员
[义] A person who examines a house's condition before a sale. 在交易前检查房屋状况的人（ licensed home inspector 持证验房师）。
[例] ① The home inspector noted roof wear in the report. 验房师在报告中写了屋顶磨损。 ② Buyers often hire a home inspector after an offer. 买方出价后常雇房屋检验员。
[搭] licensed home inspector 持证验房师, home inspection report 验房报告, hire a home inspector 请验房师
[联] real estate agent 房产经纪, contractor 承包商, property 房产, buyer 买方, building 建筑, safety 安全
[记] 可数：a home inspector。home inspection 名词

**exterminator**
英/美 /ɪkˈstɜːmɪneɪtə(r)/
n. 灭虫员；除害员
[义] A person whose job is killing insects and pests in buildings. 在建筑物内杀灭虫害的人（ pest exterminator 害虫防治员）。亦说 pest control technician。
[例] ① The exterminator treated the office for ants. 除害员给办公室做了蚂蚁防治。 ② Exterminators use chemicals requiring safety training. 灭虫员使用须安全培训的药剂。
[搭] pest exterminator 害虫防治员, exterminator service 除害服务, licensed exterminator 持证除害员
[联] technician 技术员, maintenance 维护, building 建筑, safety 安全, worker 工人, tradesperson 技工
[记] 可数：an exterminator。exterminate 动词。pest control 为更正式行业说法

**proprietor**
英/美 /prəˈpraɪətə(r)/
n. 业主；店主
[义] A person who owns a business, especially a small shop or hotel. 拥有企业的人，尤指小店铺或旅馆（ small business proprietor 小生意业主， sole proprietor 个体户）。
[例] ① The proprietor opened the café at six every morning. 店主每天早上六点开店。 ② As proprietor, she handled hiring and payroll herself. 作为业主，她亲自管招聘和工资。
[搭] small business proprietor 小企业主, sole proprietor 个体户, shop proprietor 店主, hotel proprietor 旅馆老板

**franchisee**
英/美 /ˌfræntʃaɪˈziː/
n. 加盟商
[义] A person or company that operates a business under a franchise brand. 在特许经营品牌下经营业务的人或公司（ fast-food franchisee 快餐加盟商）。
[例] ① The franchisee opened three locations under the same brand. 加盟商在同一品牌下开了三家店。 ② Franchisees pay fees and follow corporate standards. 加盟商缴费并遵守总部标准。

**shopkeeper**
英/美 /ˈʃɒpkiːpə(r)/
n. 店主；小商贩
[义] A person who owns and runs a small shop. 经营小店铺的人（ local shopkeeper 本地店主， family shopkeeper 家庭小店）。
[例] ① The shopkeeper knew regular customers by name. 店主叫得出老顾客名字。 ② Shopkeepers order stock and manage cashiers. 店主进货、管理收银员。

英/美 /ˈkɒntent kriˈeɪtə(r)/
n. 内容创作者
[义] A person who produces videos, posts, or other media for online audiences, often for income. 为网络受众制作视频、帖子等媒体并常以此谋生的人（ full-time content creator 全职创作者）。
[例] ① Content creators monetize through ads and sponsorships. 内容创作者靠广告与赞助变现。 ② She became a content creator after leaving her marketing job. 离开营销工作后她成了内容创作者。

**influencer**
英/美 /ˈɪnfluənsə(r)/
n. 网红；影响者
[义] A person who affects others' buying choices through social media. 通过社交媒体影响他人购买决策的人（ brand influencer 品牌网红， micro-influencer 微型网红）。
[例] ① The brand paid influencers to review the product. 品牌付费请网红测评产品。 ② Influencers disclose sponsored posts by law in many countries. 多国法律要求网红标注赞助内容。

adj. 自雇的
[义] Working for yourself rather than for an employer. 为自己工作而非受雇于他人（ self-employed contractor 自雇承包商， self-employed consultant 自雇顾问）。
[例] ① She is self-employed as a graphic designer. 她以平面设计师身份自雇。 ② Self-employed workers handle their own taxes. 自雇劳动者自行报税。

英/美 /ˌɒkjuˈpeɪʃənl ˈhæzəd/

[义] A danger or problem that is normal in a particular job. 特定工作中常见的危险或问题（ occupational hazard of nursing 护理职业危害）。
[例] ① Back strain is an occupational hazard for warehouse workers. 背伤是仓库工人的职业危害。 ② Burnout is treated as an occupational hazard in caregiving roles. 照护岗位中倦怠被视为职业危害。

[例] ① She accepted an entry-level position at the bank. 她接受了银行的入门级岗位。 ② Entry-level roles often include training programs. 入门级职位常含培训项目。

[例] ① She became the first woman in the C-suite at the firm. 她成为该公司高管层首位女性。 ② C-suite executives set strategy and culture. 高管层定战略与文化。

英/美 /ˌsuː ˈʃef/

[义] The second-in-command in a professional kitchen, under the head or executive chef. 专业厨房中仅次于主厨的负责人（executive sous chef 行政副厨）。法语借词，餐饮职级常用。
[例] ① The sous chef ran the kitchen when the chef was off duty. 主厨休息时由副厨掌灶。 ② She was promoted from line cook to sous chef. 她从灶台厨师升为二厨。

英/美 /ˈlaɪn kʊk/

[义] A cook who prepares food on a particular station in a restaurant kitchen. 在餐厅厨房特定工位上备菜的厨师（grill line cook 烧烤台厨师， fry line cook 油炸台厨师）。
[例] ① Line cooks work fast during the dinner rush. 晚餐高峰灶台厨师节奏极快。 ② He started as a line cook before culinary school. 上厨艺学校前他做过后厨一线厨。

**prep cook**
英/美 /ˈprep kʊk/
n. 备菜厨师
[义] A kitchen worker who washes, chops, and prepares ingredients before cooking. 在烹饪前清洗、切配、预处理食材的厨房员工（morning prep cook 早班备菜）。
[例] ① Prep cooks arrived at five to chop vegetables for lunch. 备菜厨五点到岗为午餐切菜。 ② The prep cook labeled containers for the line cooks. 备菜厨为灶台厨师贴好容器标签。

[义] A chef who specializes in desserts, bread, and baked goods. 专做甜点、面包与烘焙品的厨师（executive pastry chef 糕点行政主厨）。
[例] ① The pastry chef plated the chocolate mousse for service. 甜点师为出餐摆盘巧克力慕斯。 ② Pastry chefs often train separately from savory chefs. 糕点师常与热菜厨师分轨受训。

**dishwasher**
英/美 /ˈdɪʃwɒʃə(r)/

[例] ① The dishwasher kept the kitchen running through the rush. 洗碗工让厨房在高峰中运转不滞。 ② Many chefs started as dishwashers. 不少主厨从洗碗起步。
[搭] restaurant dishwasher 餐厅洗碗工, dishwasher shift 洗碗班, kitchen dishwasher 后厨洗碗

**busser**
英/美 /ˈbʌsə(r)/
n. 餐厅收台员
[义] A restaurant worker who clears tables and resets them for new customers. 收拾餐桌、为新客人摆台的服务员（also busboy/busboy 旧称）。美式餐饮常用。
[例] ① Bussers cleared plates while servers took orders. 收台员收盘子，服务员接单。 ② He worked as a busser through college. 大学期间他做过收台。

**host**

v. 主持；做东
[义] n. A restaurant employee who greets guests and seats them at tables. 迎候客人并安排座位的餐厅员工（restaurant host 餐厅领位， host stand 领位台）。v. 主持——多义。
[例] ① The host quoted a forty-minute wait at peak hour. 高峰时领位员告知要等四十分钟。 ② Hosts manage reservations and the waiting list. 领位负责预订与等位名单。

**caterer**
英/美 /ˈkeɪtərə(r)/
n. 外烩承办商；宴会餐饮商
[义] A person or company that provides food for events, weddings, or offices. 为活动、婚礼或办公室供餐的人或公司（ wedding caterer 婚礼外烩， corporate caterer 企业餐饮）。
[例] ① The caterer delivered lunch for two hundred employees. 外烩商为两百名员工送午餐。 ② Caterers coordinate menus, staff, and equipment on site. 宴会餐饮商在现场协调菜单、人员与设备。
[搭] wedding caterer 婚礼外烩, corporate caterer 企业餐饮, caterer service 外烩服务, hire a caterer 请外烩
[联] chef 厨师, cook 厨师, event planner 活动策划, hospitality 餐饮服务业, client 客户, food 食物
[记] 可数：a caterer。cater 动词；catering 名词（外烩业）

**reporter**
英/美 /rɪˈpɔːtə(r)/
n. 记者；通讯员
[义] A person who gathers and writes news stories for media outlets. 为媒体采集并撰写新闻的人（court reporter 法庭记者——另一义为速录员， beat reporter 条线记者）。journalist 更宽；reporter 强调一线采写。
[例] ① The reporter filed a story from the union picket line. 记者从工会纠察线发稿。 ② Reporters race to meet deadlines for the morning edition. 记者抢在晨版截稿前交稿。
[搭] beat reporter 条线记者, news reporter 新闻记者, field reporter 现场记者, investigative reporter 调查记者
[联] journalist 新闻工作者, editor 编辑, media 媒体, interview 采访, news 新闻, correspondent 通讯员
[记] 可数：a reporter。report 动词/名词。court reporter 在美国常指「法庭速录员」

**correspondent**
英/美 /ˌkɒrəˈspɒndənt/
n. 驻外记者；通讯员
[义] A journalist who reports from a particular place or on a specialist topic. 在特定地区或专业领域发稿的记者（foreign correspondent 驻外记者， war correspondent 战地记者）。
[例] ① The foreign correspondent covered the election from abroad. 驻外记者在海外报道选举。 ② Correspondents often work with local fixers and translators. 通讯员常与本地的线人、译者合作。
[搭] foreign correspondent 驻外记者, war correspondent 战地记者, bureau correspondent 分社记者, correspondent report 通讯报道
[联] journalist 新闻工作者, reporter 记者, diplomat 外交官, media 媒体, news 新闻, translator 译者
[记] 可数：a correspondent。correspond 动词（通信；符合）

**news anchor**
英/美 /ˈnjuːz ˌæŋkə(r)/
n. 新闻主播
[义] A person who presents the main news program on television or radio. 在电视或电台主持主新闻节目的人（ evening news anchor 晚间新闻主播， co-anchor 联合主播）。
[例] ① The news anchor opened with the market layoff story. 新闻主播以市场裁员消息开场。 ② Anchors read from teleprompters but must ad-lib during breaking news. 主播看提词器，突发新闻须即兴。
[搭] evening news anchor 晚间新闻主播, co-anchor 联合主播, news anchor desk 主播台
[联] journalist 新闻工作者, reporter 记者, broadcaster 播音员, media 媒体, TV 电视, audience 观众
[记] 可数：a news anchor。anchor 动词本义「抛锚；主持」

**editor in chief**
英/美 /ˌedɪtə(r) ɪn ˈtʃiːf/
n. 总编辑
[义] The highest-ranking editor at a publication, responsible for overall editorial direction. 出版物中职级最高的编辑，负责整体采编方向（ magazine editor in chief 杂志总编辑）。
[例] ① The editor in chief approved the cover story. 总编辑批准了封面报道。 ② Editors in chief answer to publishers and boards. 总编辑向出版人与董事会负责。
[搭] magazine editor in chief 杂志总编辑, editor in chief role 总编辑职位, acting editor in chief 代理总编辑
[联] editor 编辑, publisher 出版人, journalist 记者, media 媒体, writer 作家, company 公司
[记] 可数：an editor in chief。亦说 editor-in-chief（连字符）

**meteorologist**
英/美 /ˌmiːtiəˈrɒlədʒɪst/
n. 气象学家；天气预报员
[义] A scientist who studies weather, or a person who forecasts it for broadcast. 研究天气的科学家，或为媒体做预报的人（ TV meteorologist 电视气象播报员）。
[例] ① The meteorologist warned of severe storms overnight. 气象学家预警夜间强风暴。 ② Meteorologists use radar and models to track hurricanes. 天气预报员用雷达与模型追踪飓风。
[搭] TV meteorologist 电视气象播报员, chief meteorologist 首席气象师, meteorologist forecast 气象预报
[联] scientist 科学家, news anchor 新闻主播, weather 天气, broadcast 广播, reporter 记者, environment 环境
[记] 可数：a meteorologist。meteorology 名词；美式 weathercaster 口语

**producer**
英/美 /prəˈdjuːsə(r)/
n. 制片人；制作人
[义] A person who manages the making of a film, TV show, or podcast. 管理电影、电视节目或播客制作的人（ executive producer 执行制片人， line producer 制片主任）。亦指「生产者」——另一义。
[例] ① The producer secured funding for the documentary. 制片人为纪录片筹到资金。 ② Producers hire directors, crew, and talent. 制作人雇用导演、剧组与艺人。
[搭] executive producer 执行制片人, line producer 制片主任, film producer 电影制片人, TV producer 电视制作人
[联] director 导演, actor 演员, media 媒体, film 电影, budget 预算, creative 创意的
[记] 可数：a producer。produce 动词；production 名词

**cinematographer**
英/美 /ˌsɪnəməˈtɒɡrəfə(r)/
n. 摄影指导；电影摄影师
[义] A person who directs camera and lighting work on a film or video shoot. 指导电影或视频拍摄中摄影与灯光的人（ director of photography 摄影指导， DP 缩写）。
[例] ① The cinematographer chose lenses for the night scenes. 摄影指导为夜景选镜头。 ② Cinematographers work closely with directors on visual style. 电影摄影师与导演紧密定视觉风格。
[搭] director of photography 摄影指导, cinematographer reel 摄影作品集, award-winning cinematographer 获奖摄影指导
[联] director 导演, filmmaker 电影人, photographer 摄影师, film 电影, camera 摄影机, creative 创意的
[记] 可数：a cinematographer。DP = director of photography

**screenwriter**
英/美 /ˈskriːnraɪtə(r)/
n. 编剧
[义] A person who writes scripts for films or television. 撰写电影或电视剧本的人（ freelance screenwriter 自由编剧， staff screenwriter 专职编剧）。
[例] ① The screenwriter revised the third act before shooting. 编剧开拍前改写了第三幕。 ② Screenwriters often sell specs or work on assignment. 编剧常卖原创剧本或接约写作。
[搭] freelance screenwriter 自由编剧, screenwriter credit 编剧署名, Hollywood screenwriter 好莱坞编剧
[联] writer 作家, director 导演, producer 制片人, film 电影, actor 演员, creative 创意的
[记] 可数：a screenwriter。screenplay 名词（剧本）

**physician assistant**
英/美 /fɪˈzɪʃn əˈsɪstənt/
n. 医师助理（PA）
[义] A licensed clinician who practices medicine under a physician's supervision. 在医师监督下行医的持证临床人员（ certified physician assistant 认证 PA）。
[例] ① The physician assistant saw patients in the rural clinic. 医师助理在乡村诊所看诊。 ② PAs can prescribe medication in many states. 许多州 PA 可开药。
[搭] certified physician assistant 认证 PA, physician assistant program PA 培训项目, rural physician assistant 乡村 PA
[联] healthcare worker 医护工作者, nurse 护士, clinic 诊所, patient 病人, hospital 医院, license 执照
[记] 可数：a physician assistant。缩写 PA。与 medical assistant 不同（偏行政/辅助）

**veterinary technician**
英/美 /ˈvetnri tekˈnɪʃn/
n. 兽医助理；兽医技师
[义] A person trained to assist veterinarians with exams, surgery, and lab work. 协助兽医做检查、手术与化验的受训人员（ licensed veterinary technician 持证兽医技师， LVT）。
[例] ① The veterinary technician took the dog's vital signs before surgery. 兽医技师术前测了狗的生命体征。 ② Vet techs often run the clinic lab during shifts. 兽医助理常在轮班时操作诊所化验室。
[搭] licensed veterinary technician 持证兽医技师, vet tech training 兽医助理培训, veterinary technician exam 兽医技师考试
[联] veterinarian 兽医, nurse 护士, animal 动物, clinic 诊所, technician 技术员, healthcare worker 医护工作者
[记] 可数：a veterinary technician。口语 vet tech。veterinarian 为执业兽医

**respiratory therapist**
英/美 /rɪˈspɪrətri ˈθerəpɪst/
n. 呼吸治疗师
[义] A healthcare worker who treats patients with breathing problems. 治疗呼吸问题患者的医疗人员（ registered respiratory therapist 注册呼吸治疗师， RRT）。
[例] ① The respiratory therapist adjusted the ventilator settings. 呼吸治疗师调整了呼吸机参数。 ② Respiratory therapists work in ICUs and emergency rooms. 呼吸治疗师在 ICU 与急诊工作。
[搭] registered respiratory therapist 注册呼吸治疗师, respiratory therapist license 呼吸治疗执照, ICU respiratory therapist ICU 呼吸治疗师
[联] healthcare worker 医护工作者, nurse 护士, hospital 医院, patient 病人, therapist 治疗师, technician 技术员
[记] 可数：a respiratory therapist。缩写 RT 或 RRT

**home health aide**
英/美 /ˌhəʊm helθ eɪd/
n. 居家护理员
[义] A worker who helps elderly or disabled people with daily care at home. 在居家环境协助老人或残障者日常照护的护理员（ certified home health aide 认证居家护理员）。
[例] ① The home health aide assisted with bathing and meals. 居家护理员协助洗浴与用餐。 ② Home health aides often work through agencies on shift schedules. 居家护理员常经机构轮班上门。
[搭] certified home health aide 认证居家护理员, home health aide training 居家护理培训, home health aide agency 居家护理机构
[联] care worker 护理员, nurse 护士, caregiver 照护者, patient 病人, elderly 老年人, healthcare worker 医护工作者
[记] 可数：a home health aide。HHA 缩写。与 nursing assistant 岗位重叠但偏居家

**lab technician**
英/美 /ˈlæb tekˈnɪʃn/
n. 实验室技术员
[义] A person who performs tests and maintains equipment in a laboratory. 在实验室做检测、维护设备的技术员（ medical lab technician 医学化验员， research lab technician 科研实验员）。
[例] ① The lab technician processed blood samples overnight. 化验员连夜处理血样。 ② Lab technicians follow strict safety protocols. 实验室技术员遵守严格安全规程。
[搭] medical lab technician 医学化验员, research lab technician 科研实验员, lab technician certification 化验员认证
[联] scientist 科学家, researcher 研究员, healthcare worker 医护工作者, hospital 医院, technician 技术员, phlebotomist 抽血员
[记] 可数：a lab technician。lab tech 口语。clinical laboratory scientist 为更高阶称谓

**radiologic technologist**
英/美 /ˌreɪdiəˈlɒdʒɪkl teknˈɒlədʒɪst/
n. 放射科技师
[义] A healthcare worker who performs X-rays, CT scans, and other imaging procedures. 操作 X 光、CT 等影像检查的医疗技师（ registered radiologic technologist 注册放射技师， RT）。
[例] ① The radiologic technologist positioned the patient for the scan. 放射科技师为扫描摆好病人体位。 ② RTs shield staff and patients from excess radiation. 放射技师保护医护与患者免受过量辐射。
[搭] registered radiologic technologist 注册放射技师, radiologic technologist license 放射技师执照, hospital radiologic technologist 医院放射技师
[联] healthcare worker 医护工作者, nurse 护士, hospital 医院, technician 技术员, patient 病人, physician 医师
[记] 可数：a radiologic technologist。缩写 RT。technologist 比 technician 强调更高认证

**physiotherapist**
英/美 /ˌfɪziəʊˈθerəpɪst/

[义] A person trained to treat injury and illness through exercise and physical methods; British equivalent of physical therapist. 以运动与物理方法治疗伤病的受训人员；英式说法，美式多用 physical therapist（ chartered physiotherapist 特许物理治疗师）。
[例] ① The physiotherapist designed a rehab plan after knee surgery. 物理治疗师在膝术后制定康复计划。 ② In the UK, physiotherapists work in NHS hospitals and clinics. 英国物理治疗师在 NHS 医院与诊所工作。
[搭] chartered physiotherapist 特许物理治疗师, sports physiotherapist 运动物理治疗师, physiotherapist clinic 理疗诊所
[联] physical therapist 物理治疗师, healthcare worker 医护工作者, athlete 运动员, hospital 医院, injury 受伤, rehabilitation 康复
[记] 可数：a physiotherapist。physiotherapy 名词。本卷 physical therapist 条为美式同职

英/美 /ˈeə ˌtræfɪk kənˈtrəʊlə(r)/
n. 空中交通管制员
[义] A person who directs aircraft movements in the air and on the ground to prevent collisions. 指挥空中与地面飞机移动、防止相撞的人（ FAA air traffic controller 美国联邦航管员）。高压力、高责任职业。
[例] ① Air traffic controllers cleared the runway for landing. 空管放行跑道供降落。 ② Controllers work rotating shifts in tower and radar rooms. 管制员在塔台与雷达室轮班工作。
[搭] air traffic controller training 空管培训, certified air traffic controller 持证空管, tower controller 塔台管制员
[联] pilot 飞行员, airport 机场, aviation 航空, dispatcher 调度员, safety 安全, shift 轮班
[记] 可数：an air traffic controller。ATC 缩写。controller 此处非「管理者」

**customs officer**
英/美 /ˈkʌstəmz ˌɒfɪsə(r)/
n. 海关官员
[义] A government employee who inspects goods and people entering or leaving a country. 检查出入境货物与人员的政府工作人员（ border customs officer 口岸海关， airport customs officer 机场海关）。
[例] ① The customs officer asked about items in their luggage. 海关官员询问行李内物品。 ② Customs officers seize undeclared commercial goods. 海关查获未申报的商业货物。

**immigration officer**

[义] An official who checks passports and decides who may enter a country. 检查护照并决定谁可入境的官员（ airport immigration officer 机场移民官）。
[例] ① The immigration officer stamped her passport at arrival. 移民官在入境时盖了护照章。 ② Immigration officers interview travelers about visa status. 移民官就签证状态询问旅客。
[搭] airport immigration officer 机场移民官, immigration officer interview 移民官问询, border immigration officer 边境移民官

**park ranger**
英/美 /ˈpɑːk ˌreɪndʒə(r)/
n. 公园管理员；护林员
[义] A person employed to protect and manage a national or state park. 受雇保护、管理国立或州立公园的人员（ national park ranger 国家公园护林员， forest ranger 森林护林员）。
[例] ① The park ranger warned hikers about the bear activity. 护林员提醒徒步者注意熊活动。 ② Park rangers lead tours and enforce fire rules. 公园管理员带讲解并执行防火规定。
[搭] national park ranger 国家公园护林员, park ranger station 护林站, park ranger uniform 护林员制服

**shop steward**
英/美 /ˈʃɒp ˌstjuːəd/
n. 车间工会代表
[义] A worker elected to represent colleagues in dealings with management and the union. 经选举代表同事与管理层、工会交涉的工人（ union shop steward 工会车间代表）。
[例] ① The shop steward filed a grievance over overtime rules. 车间代表就加班规定提出申诉。 ② Shop stewards attend union meetings on workers' behalf. 工会代表代表工人出席工会会议。
[搭] union shop steward 工会车间代表, shop steward election 车间代表选举, shop steward role 车间代表职责

**quality inspector**
英/美 /ˈkwɒləti ɪnˈspektə(r)/
n. 质检员
[义] A worker who checks products or processes meet standards before shipment or use. 在出厂或使用前检查产品、流程是否符合标准的员工（ factory quality inspector 工厂质检员， incoming quality inspector 来料检验员）。
[例] ① The quality inspector rejected a batch with defective seals. 质检员拒收一批密封缺陷产品。 ② Quality inspectors document defects for production teams. 质检员为生产团队记录缺陷。

**truck driver**
英/美 /ˈtrʌk ˌdraɪvə(r)/
n. 卡车司机
[义] A person who drives a truck to transport goods over long or local routes. 驾驶卡车在长途或本地线路运货的人（ long-haul truck driver 长途卡车司机， CDL truck driver 持证司机）。
[例] ① The truck driver logged twelve hours on the highway. 卡车司机在高速上记录了十二小时。 ② Truck drivers face strict hours-of-service rules. 货运司机受严格工时法规约束。

**bus driver**
英/美 /ˈbʌs ˌdraɪvə(r)/
n. 公交司机；巴士司机
[义] A person who drives a bus carrying passengers on a fixed route. 在固定线路上驾驶载客巴士的人（ school bus driver 校车司机， city bus driver 公交司机）。
[例] ① The bus driver waited for a wheelchair passenger to board. 公交司机等候轮椅乘客上车。 ② School bus drivers undergo background checks each year. 校车司机每年接受背景调查。

**train conductor**
英/美 /ˈtreɪn kənˈdʌktə(r)/
n. 列车员；列车长
[义] A railway worker who checks tickets and assists passengers on a train. 在列车上查票、服务乘客的铁路员工（ Amtrak conductor 美铁列车员）。英式 conductor 亦指「乐队指挥」——语境区分。
[例] ① The train conductor announced the next stop. 列车员报下一站。 ② Conductors coordinate with engineers during delays. 延误时列车长与司机协调。

[例] ① The stevedore crew moved pallets onto the dock. 装卸工班组把托盘移上码头。 ② Stevedores coordinate with crane operators and customs. 码头装卸与起重机操作员、海关协调。

**controller**
英/美 /kənˈtrəʊlə(r)/
n. 财务总监；控制员
[义] The senior accounting executive who oversees financial reporting and internal controls. 监督财务报告与内控的高级财务高管（corporate controller 公司财务总监，financial controller 英式说法）。亦指「控制器」——另一义。
[例] ① The controller closed the books before the audit. 财务总监在审计前结账。 ② She was promoted from accountant to controller. 她从会计升为财务总监。
[搭] corporate controller 公司财务总监, financial controller 财务控制官, assistant controller 助理财务总监
[联] CFO 首席财务官, accountant 会计, finance 财务, auditor 审计师, company 公司, report 报告
[记] 可数：a controller。；controller 偏财务管控

**tax preparer**
英/美 /ˈtæks prɪˈpeərə(r)/
n. 报税员
[义] A person who helps individuals or businesses prepare and file tax returns. 帮个人或企业编制、申报税表的人（ certified tax preparer 认证报税员， seasonal tax preparer 季节性报税员）。
[例] ① The tax preparer filed their return before the deadline. 报税员在截止日前提交了申报。 ② Tax preparers busy every spring with client appointments. 每年春天报税员都排满客户预约。
[搭] certified tax preparer 认证报税员, seasonal tax preparer 季节性报税员, tax preparer service 报税服务
[联] accountant 会计, client 客户, finance 金融, IRS 美国国税局, freelance 自由职业, seasonal 季节性的
[记] 可数：a tax preparer。accountant 更宽；tax preparer 可非持证 CPA

**grant writer**
英/美 /ˈɡrɑːnt ˌraɪtə(r)/
n. 基金撰稿人
[义] A person who writes proposals to obtain grants from foundations or government agencies. 撰写方案向基金会或政府机构申请资助的人（ freelance grant writer 自由基金撰稿）。
[例] ① The grant writer secured funding for the nonprofit program. 基金撰稿人为该非营利项目争取到资金。 ② Grant writers research deadlines and eligibility rules. 基金撰稿研究截止日与资格规则。
[搭] freelance grant writer 自由基金撰稿, grant writer proposal 基金申请方案, nonprofit grant writer 非营利基金撰稿
[联] writer 作家, fundraiser 筹款人, nonprofit 非营利, funding 融资, organization 组织, proposal 方案
[记] 可数：a grant writer。grant 名词（资助金）

**fundraiser**
英/美 /ˈfʌndreɪzə(r)/
n. 筹款人；募捐活动
[义] A person whose job is raising money for a charity, school, or political campaign. 为慈善机构、学校或政治竞选募集资金的人（political fundraiser 政治筹款人）。亦指「募捐活动」——另一义。
[例] ① The fundraiser organized a gala for the hospital charity. 筹款人为医院慈善组织了一场 gala。 ② Fundraisers build donor networks over years. 募捐人员多年经营捐赠者网络。
[搭] political fundraiser 政治筹款人, nonprofit fundraiser 非营利筹款, fundraiser event 募捐活动
[联] grant writer 基金撰稿人, charity 慈善, donor 捐赠者, event planner 活动策划, politician 政治家, organization 组织
[记] 可数：a fundraiser。fundraise 动词；fundraising 名词

**lobbyist**
英/美 /ˈlɒbiɪst/
n. 说客；院外活动家
[义] A person paid to influence politicians and government officials on behalf of clients. 受雇代表客户影响政客与政府官员的人（ corporate lobbyist 企业说客， registered lobbyist 注册说客）。
[例] ① Lobbyists met with legislators before the vote. 说客在投票前会见议员。 ② She worked as a lobbyist for a trade association. 她为行业协会做说客。
[搭] corporate lobbyist 企业说客, registered lobbyist 注册说客, lobbyist disclosure 说客披露
[联] politician 政治家, government 政府, lawyer 律师, company 公司, policy 政策, advocate 倡导者
[记] 可数：a lobbyist。lobby 动词/名词（游说；大厅）

**DJ**
英/美 /ˌdiː ˈdʒeɪ/
n. DJ；唱片骑师
v. 打碟
[义] n. A person who plays recorded music for an audience, especially at clubs or events. 为听众播放录制音乐的人，尤指俱乐部或活动现场（wedding DJ 婚礼 DJ，club DJ 夜店 DJ）。v. 打碟主持。
[例] ① The DJ kept the dance floor full until midnight. DJ 让舞池热到午夜。 ② She DJs weddings on weekends as a side hustle. 她周末做婚礼打碟当副业。
[搭] wedding DJ 婚礼 DJ, club DJ 夜店 DJ, radio DJ 电台 DJ, DJ set DJ 演出
[联] musician 音乐家, event planner 活动策划, entertainment 娱乐, freelance 自由职业, gig economy 零工经济, audience 观众
[记] 可数：a DJ。disc jockey 全称。DJ 亦可作动词

**choreographer**
英/美 /ˌkɒriˈɒɡrəfə(r)/
n. 编舞
[义] A person who designs dance movements for performances or videos. 为演出或视频设计舞蹈动作的人（ freelance choreographer 自由编舞， Broadway choreographer 百老汇编舞）。
[例] ① The choreographer staged a new number for the tour. 编舞为巡演排了新段。 ② Choreographers work with dancers and directors. 编舞与舞者、导演合作。
[搭] freelance choreographer 自由编舞, Broadway choreographer 百老汇编舞, choreographer rehearsal 编舞排练
[联] dancer 舞者, artist 艺术家, director 导演, performance 表演, creative 创意的, musician 音乐家
[记] 可数：a choreographer。choreography 名词

**voice actor**
英/美 /ˈvɔɪs ˌæktə(r)/
n. 配音演员
[义] A performer who provides voices for animated characters, games, or commercials. 为动画角色、游戏或广告配音的表演者（ freelance voice actor 自由配音， anime voice actor 动漫配音）。
[例] ① The voice actor recorded lines for the video game. 配音演员为游戏录台词。 ② Voice actors often work from home studios. 配音常在家录音室工作。
[搭] freelance voice actor 自由配音, voice actor audition 配音试音, anime voice actor 动漫配音
[联] actor 演员, artist 艺术家, freelance 自由职业, film 电影, creative 创意的, studio 工作室
[记] 可数：a voice actor。voice-over 名词（画外音）

**casting director**
英/美 /ˈkɑːstɪŋ dɪˈrektə(r)/
n. 选角导演
[义] A person who selects actors for film, TV, or theater productions. 为影视或戏剧作品挑选演员的人（film casting director 电影选角）。
[例] ① The casting director held auditions for the lead role. 选角导演为主角办试镜。 ② Casting directors work with directors and producers. 选角与导演、制片人合作。
[搭] film casting director 电影选角, casting director audition 选角试镜, casting call 选角通告
[联] director 导演, actor 演员, producer 制片人, talent agent 艺人经纪人, film 电影, audition 试镜
[记] 可数：a casting director。casting 名词

**ironworker**
英/美 /ˈaɪənwɜːkə(r)/
n. 铁工；钢结构工
[义] A construction worker who installs structural steel and reinforcing bars. 安装建筑钢结构与钢筋的工人（ structural ironworker 结构铁工， reinforcing ironworker 钢筋工）。
[例] ① Ironworkers bolted steel beams thirty stories up. 铁工在三十层高处栓钢梁。 ② Ironworkers belong to skilled trade unions. 钢结构工多属熟练行业工会。
[搭] structural ironworker 结构铁工, union ironworker 工会铁工, ironworker apprentice 铁工学徒
[联] construction worker 建筑工人, welder 焊工, crane operator 起重机操作员, union 工会, blue-collar 蓝领的, craft 手艺
[记] 可数：an ironworker。iron 指钢铁结构，非「熨斗」

**plant manager**
英/美 /ˈplɑːnt ˌmænɪdʒə(r)/
n. 厂长
[义] The person in charge of daily operations at a factory or production plant. 负责工厂或生产厂日常运营的人（ manufacturing plant manager 制造厂长）。
[例] ① The plant manager cut overtime after the safety audit. 安全审计后厂长削减了加班。 ② Plant managers report to operations managers or VPs. 厂长向运营经理或副总裁汇报。
[搭] manufacturing plant manager 制造厂长, plant manager role 厂长职责, plant manager safety 厂长安全责任
[联] factory worker 工厂工人, operations manager 运营经理, supervisor 主管, production 生产, company 公司, manager 经理
[记] 可数：a plant manager。plant 此处指「工厂」，非「植物」

**surveyor**
英/美 /səˈveɪə(r)/
n. 测量师；勘测员
[义] A person who measures land, buildings, or construction sites for legal or engineering purposes. 为法律或工程目的测量土地、建筑或工地的人（ land surveyor 土地测量师， quantity surveyor 工料测量师——英式）。
[例] ① The surveyor marked property boundaries before construction. 测量师在施工前标定地界。 ② Surveyors submit reports for mortgage and zoning applications. 勘测员为按揭与 zoning 申请提交报告。
[搭] land surveyor 土地测量师, licensed surveyor 持证测量师, construction surveyor 施工测量师
[联] engineer 工程师, architect 建筑师, construction worker 建筑工人, property 房产, map 地图, license 执照
[记] 可数：a surveyor。survey 名词/动词

**game warden**
英/美 /ˈɡeɪm ˌwɔːdn/
n. 渔猎监督官
[义] A law enforcement officer who enforces hunting and fishing regulations. 执法狩猎与渔业法规的官员（state game warden 州渔猎官）。与 park ranger 职能重叠。
[例] ① The game warden checked licenses at the lake. 渔猎官在湖边查许可证。 ② Game wardens investigate poaching reports. 渔猎监督调查偷猎举报。
[搭] state game warden 州渔猎官, game warden patrol 渔猎巡逻, game warden license 渔猎官执法
[联] park ranger 护林员, hunter 猎人, wildlife 野生动物, officer 官员, law 法律, outdoor 户外的
[记] 可数：a game warden。game 此处指「猎物」

**brand ambassador**
英/美 /ˈbrænd æmˈbæsədə(r)/
n. 品牌大使
[义] A person hired to represent a brand positively in public or on social media. 受雇在公众或社交媒体上正面代表品牌的人（ social media brand ambassador 社交媒体品牌大使）。
[例] ① The brand ambassador posted about the new product launch. 品牌大使发了新品上市内容。 ② Brand ambassadors often receive free products and commission. 品牌大使常获免费产品与佣金。
[搭] social media brand ambassador 社交媒体品牌大使, brand ambassador program 品牌大使计划, campus brand ambassador 校园品牌大使
[联] influencer 网红, marketer 营销人员, brand 品牌, social media 社交媒体, commission 佣金, client 客户
[记] 可数：a brand ambassador。ambassador 本义「大使」

**help desk technician**
英/美 /ˈhelp desk tekˈnɪʃn/
n. help desk 技术员
[义] A person who provides technical support to employees or customers by phone, chat, or ticket. 通过电话、聊天或工单为员工或客户提供技术支持的人（ IT help desk technician IT 服务台技术员）。
[例] ① The help desk technician reset her password in minutes. 服务台技术员几分钟内重置了她的密码。 ② Help desk techs escalate complex issues to engineers. Help desk 将复杂问题升级给工程师。
[搭] IT help desk technician IT 服务台技术员, help desk technician ticket 服务台工单, remote help desk 远程服务台
[联] technician 技术员, IT 信息技术, employee 雇员, customer 客户, software 软件, support 支持
[记] 可数：a help desk technician。亦说 IT support specialist

**magistrate**
英/美 /ˈmædʒɪstreɪt/
n. 治安法官；地方法官
[义] A civil officer who handles minor legal cases and preliminary hearings. 处理轻微案件与预审的民事法官（ magistrate judge 治安法官——美式联邦用法）。
[例] ① The magistrate set bail at five thousand dollars. 治安法官将保释金定为五千美元。 ② Magistrates hear small claims and traffic cases. 地方法官审理小额与交通案件。
[搭] magistrate judge 治安法官, magistrate court 治安法庭, presiding magistrate 主审治安法官
[联] judge 法官, lawyer 律师, court 法庭, bailiff 法警, government 政府, trial 审判
[记] 可数：a magistrate。英式 magistrate 无律师背景亦可担任

**private**
英/美 /ˈpraɪvət/
n. 列兵
adj. 私人的
[义] n. The lowest rank of soldier in the army. 陆军最低士兵军衔（private first class 一等兵——美军）。adj. 私人的——常见形容词义。
[例] ① The private reported to the sergeant at morning roll call. 列兵晨点名向中士报到。 ② He enlisted as a private and left as a captain. 他以列兵入伍，以上尉退伍。
[搭] army private 陆军列兵, private first class 一等兵, promoted from private 从列兵晋升
[联] soldier 士兵, corporal 下士, sergeant 中士, army 军队, rank 职级, officer 军官, private 列兵 vs. private investigator 私家侦探：同形异义；军队语境中 private 几乎总指军衔
[记] 可数：a private。缩写 PVT。private investigator 为另一词条

**corporal**
英/美 /ˈkɔːpərəl/
n. 下士
[义] A non-commissioned officer rank below sergeant in the army or marines. 陆军或海军陆战队中低于中士的士官军衔（lance corporal 准下士——英式）。
[例] ① The corporal led a squad of eight privates. 下士带领八名列兵的小队。 ② She was promoted to corporal after two years of service. 服役两年后她晋升为下士。
[搭] lance corporal 准下士, army corporal 陆军下士, corporal rank 下士军衔, squad corporal 班长下士
[联] sergeant 中士, private 列兵, NCO 士官, soldier 士兵, army 军队, rank 职级
[记] 可数：a corporal。corporal 亦指「肉体的」——另一义；军队语境靠搭配区分

**sergeant**
英/美 /ˈsɑːdʒənt/
n. 中士；警佐
[义] A non-commissioned officer rank above corporal in the army or police. 陆军中高于下士的士官军衔；警察系统中的警佐（staff sergeant 上士，sergeant major 军士长）。
[例] ① The sergeant inspected the barracks before inspection. 中士检阅前检查了营房。 ② Sergeants often serve as squad or platoon leaders. 中士常任班或排长。
[搭] staff sergeant 上士, sergeant major 军士长, army sergeant 陆军中士, police sergeant 警佐
[联] corporal 下士, lieutenant 尉官, NCO 士官, soldier 士兵, police officer 警察, rank 职级
[记] 可数：a sergeant。缩写 Sgt. 警察与军队均用此衔

**lieutenant**
英/美 /lefˈtenənt/
n. 尉官；中尉
[义] A commissioned officer rank below captain. 低于上尉的尉级军官（first lieutenant 中尉，second lieutenant 少尉——美军）。海军 lieutenant 相当于陆军 captain 层级，但英美军衔体系并不一致。
[例] ① The lieutenant briefed the platoon before deployment. 中尉部署前向排里作了简报。 ② She earned her commission as a second lieutenant. 她获少尉委任。
[搭] first lieutenant 中尉, second lieutenant 少尉, lieutenant colonel 中校, police lieutenant 警督
[联] captain 上尉, major 少校, officer 军官, soldier 士兵, army 军队, rank 职级
[记] 可数：a lieutenant。缩写 Lt. 英式海军 lieutenant 军衔高于陆军 lieutenant

**captain**
英/美 /ˈkæptɪn/
n. 上尉；舰长；队长
[义] A commissioned officer rank above lieutenant; also the person in command of a ship, aircraft, or team. 高于尉官的军官军衔；亦指舰长、机长或队长（army captain 陆军上尉，sea captain 船长）。
[例] ① The captain signed the orders before the unit moved out. 上尉在部队出动前签署了命令。 ② The airline captain announced a smooth landing. 航司机长宣布即将平稳着陆。
[搭] army captain 陆军上尉, sea captain 船长, team captain 队长, captain's rank 上尉军衔
[联] lieutenant 尉官, major 少校, officer 军官, pilot 飞行员, soldier 士兵, rank 职级, captain 上尉/舰长 vs. CEO/总监语境中的「一把手」：军队、航运、运动队均常用 captain 指负责人
[记] 可数：a captain。缩写 Capt. army captain 在美军 O-3 级

**major**
英/美 /ˈmeɪdʒə(r)/
n. 少校
adj. 主要的
[义] n. A commissioned officer rank above captain and below lieutenant colonel. 高于上尉、低于中校的军官军衔（major general 少将——将级）。adj. 主要的——日常高频义。
[例] ① The major commanded a battalion of six hundred soldiers. 少校指挥六百人的营。 ② She was promoted to major after fifteen years of service. 服役十五年后她晋升为少校。
[搭] army major 陆军少校, major general 少将, battalion major 营级少校, promoted to major 晋升为少校
[联] colonel 上校, captain 上尉, officer 军官, soldier 士兵, army 军队, rank 职级
[记] 可数：a major。缩写 Maj. 大学 major「专业」为另一义

**colonel**
英/美 /ˈkɜːnl/
n. 上校
[义] A high-ranking commissioned officer below general, often commanding a regiment. 低于将军的高级军官，常指挥团级单位（lieutenant colonel 中校，colonel in chief 荣誉上校）。
[例] ① The colonel addressed the regiment at the ceremony. 上校在典礼上对全团讲话。 ② Colonels often serve on division or brigade staffs. 上校常在师、旅参谋部任职。
[搭] lieutenant colonel 中校, army colonel 陆军上校, colonel rank 上校军衔, retired colonel 退役上校
[联] general 将军, major 少校, officer 军官, soldier 士兵, army 军队, rank 职级
[记] 可数：a colonel。缩写 Col. 发音 /ˈkɜːnl/，r 不发音

**general**
英/美 /ˈdʒenrəl/
n. 将军
adj. 一般的
[义] n. A senior army officer of the highest rank, such as brigadier or four-star general. 陆军最高阶军官之一，如准将或四星上将（major general 少将，brigadier general 准将）。adj. 一般的——日常义。
[例] ① The general outlined strategy before the joint exercise. 将军在联合演习前阐述战略。 ② Generals report to civilian defense leadership. 将官向文官国防领导汇报。
[搭] major general 少将, brigadier general 准将, four-star general 四星上将, retired general 退役将军
[联] colonel 上校, admiral 海军上将, officer 军官, soldier 士兵, army 军队, commander 指挥官, general 将军 vs. general manager 总经理：同形异义；军事语境中 the general 几乎总指将官
[记] 可数：a general。缩写 Gen. general officer 将官统称

**admiral**
英/美 /ˈædmərəl/
n. 海军上将；海军将官
[义] A senior naval officer of flag rank. 海军将级高级军官（rear admiral 海军少将，vice admiral 海军中将，Admiral of the Fleet 海军元帅——英式）。
[例] ① The admiral reviewed the fleet before deployment. 海军上将部署前检阅舰队。 ② Admirals command carrier groups or naval districts. 海军将官指挥航母战斗群或海区。
[搭] rear admiral 海军少将, vice admiral 海军中将, fleet admiral 海军五星上将, admiral rank 海军将衔
[联] commander 指挥官, sailor 水兵, navy 海军, officer 军官, captain 舰长, rank 职级
[记] 可数：an admiral。源自阿拉伯语 amir-al-bahr。fleet admiral 为战时最高阶

**commander**
英/美 /kəˈmɑːndə(r)/
n. 指挥官；中校（海军）
[义] An officer in charge of a unit or operation; a naval rank between lieutenant commander and captain. 负责部队或行动的军官；海军中校至船长之间的军衔（platoon commander 排长，incident commander 现场指挥）。
[例] ① The commander briefed crews before the rescue mission. 指挥官救援任务前向机组简报。 ② She rose to commander in the Coast Guard. 她在海岸警卫队升至中校。

[例] ① The warrant officer maintained the helicopter fleet. 准尉维护直升机队。 ② Warrant officers often have decades of field experience. 准尉常有数十年野战经验。

[例] ① The ensign stood watch on the bridge at midnight. 海军少尉午夜在舰桥值更。 ② She received her commission as an ensign after graduation. 毕业后她获海军少尉委任。
