英/美 /ˈsaʊnd ˌendʒɪˈnɪə(r)/
n. 音响师；录音师
[义] A person who records, mixes, or designs sound for film, TV, music, or live events. 为影视、音乐或现场活动录音、混音或设计声音的人（production sound engineer 同期录音师，re-recording mixer 终混师）。
[例] ① The sound engineer balanced dialogue and background noise in the mix. 音响师在混音中平衡对白与背景噪声。 ② Sound engineers wear headphones on set all day. 录音师整天在片场戴耳机。
[搭] production sound engineer 同期录音师, live sound engineer 现场音响师, sound engineer mixer 混音音响师
[联] boom operator 挑杆录音员, musician 音乐家, film 电影, crew 剧组, music 音乐, producer 制片人
[记] 可数：a sound engineer。亦说 sound mixer（混音师）。sound designer 偏后期设计

**production designer**
英/美 /prəˈdʌkʃn dɪˈzaɪnə(r)/
n. 美术指导
[义] The head of the art department who designs the overall visual look of sets and locations. 艺术部门负责人，设计布景与场景的整体视觉（film production designer 电影艺术指导）。
[例] ① The production designer built a 1940s street on the backlot. 艺术指导在外景场地搭了一条四十年代街道。 ② Production designers collaborate with the director and cinematographer. 生产设计与导演、摄影指导合作。
[搭] film production designer 电影艺术指导, production designer credit 艺术指导署名, award-winning production designer 获奖艺术指导
[联] set decorator 布景师, prop master 道具师, director 导演, film 电影, designer 设计师, creative 创意的
[记] 可数：a production designer。奥斯卡设有最佳艺术指导奖

**set decorator**
英/美 /ˈset ˌdekəreɪtə(r)/
n. 布景师
[义] A person who selects and arranges furniture, props, and décor for film or TV sets. 为影视布景选购、摆放家具、道具与装饰的人（lead set decorator 首席布景师）。
[例] ① The set decorator aged the living room for the period drama. 布景师把客厅做旧以符合年代剧。 ② Set decorators work under the production designer. 布景师在艺术指导领导下工作。
[搭] lead set decorator 首席布景师, set decorator budget 布景预算, film set decorator 电影布景师
[联] production designer 艺术指导, prop master 道具师, costume designer 服装设计师, film 电影, set 布景, designer 设计师
[记] 可数：a set decorator。set decoration 名词。与 interior decorator（家装）不同

**prop master**
英/美 /ˈprɒp ˌmɑːstə(r)/
n. 道具师
[义] The person responsible for acquiring, organizing, and maintaining props on a production. 负责采购、管理、维护片场道具的人（head prop master 道具组长，props 缩写）。
[例] ① The prop master tracked every gun and letter in the scene. 道具师追踪场景中每把枪与每封信。 ② Prop masters prepare hero props for close-up shots. 道具师为特写镜头准备「英雄道具」。
[搭] head prop master 道具组长, prop master inventory 道具清单, film prop master 电影道具师
[联] set decorator 布景师, actor 演员, director 导演, film 电影, crew 剧组, stagehand 舞台技工
[记] 可数：a prop master。prop = property（道具）。props department 道具组

**costume designer**
英/美 /ˈkɒstjuːm dɪˈzaɪnə(r)/
n. 服装设计师
[义] A person who designs or selects clothing for characters in film, TV, or theater. 为影视或戏剧角色设计、挑选服装的人（period costume designer 年代戏服装设计师）。
[例] ① The costume designer fitted the cast in Victorian dresses. 服装设计师给演员试维多利亚式裙装。 ② Costume designers research history and collaborate with makeup artists. 服装设计师考据历史并与化妆师合作。
[搭] period costume designer 年代戏服装师, film costume designer 电影服装设计师, costume designer fitting 服装试装
[联] makeup artist 化妆师, wardrobe supervisor 服装主管, actor 演员, designer 设计师, film 电影, theater 戏剧
[记] 可数：a costume designer。costume 名词（戏服）。fashion designer 偏时装产业

**wardrobe supervisor**
英/美 /ˈwɔːdrəʊb ˈsuːpəvaɪzə(r)/
n. 服装主管
[义] A crew member who maintains costumes on set and manages changes during filming. 在片场维护戏服、管理拍摄中换装的工作人员（film wardrobe supervisor 电影服装主管）。
[例] ① The wardrobe supervisor steamed costumes between takes. 服装主管在镜头间隙熨戏服。 ② Wardrobe supervisors label each actor's changes. 服装主管为每位演员的换装贴标签。
[搭] film wardrobe supervisor 电影服装主管, wardrobe supervisor set 片场服装主管, wardrobe truck 服装车
[联] costume designer 服装设计师, makeup artist 化妆师, actor 演员, stylist 造型师, film 电影, crew 剧组
[记] 可数：a wardrobe supervisor。wardrobe 名词（衣柜；戏服部门）

**stunt performer**
英/美 /ˈstʌnt pəˌfɔːmə(r)/
n. 特技演员
[义] A trained performer who carries out dangerous actions in place of actors. 受训替演员完成危险动作的专业人员（ stunt double 特技替身， stunt coordinator 特技统筹——相关职）。
[例] ① The stunt performer jumped from the roof onto a pad. 特技演员从屋顶跳到垫子上。 ② Stunt performers rehearse falls and fights for days. 特技演员排练摔打与打斗数日。
[搭] stunt double 特技替身, professional stunt performer 职业特技演员, stunt performer insurance 特技保险
[联] actor 演员, director 导演, film 电影, safety 安全, performance 表演, crew 剧组
[记] 可数：a stunt performer。stuntman/stuntwoman 旧称；现多用 gender-neutral performer

**extra**
英/美 /ˈekstrə/
n. 群众演员
adj. 额外的
[义] n. A background performer in a film or TV scene without speaking lines. 影视场景中无台词的背景演员（background extra 背景群众演员， crowd extra 人群演员）。adj. 额外的——日常义。
[例] ① Two hundred extras filled the stadium scene. 两百名群众演员填满体育场镜头。 ② She worked as an extra before landing speaking roles. 接到有台词角色前她做过群演。
[搭] background extra 背景群演, crowd extra 人群演员, extra casting 群演选角, work as an extra 当群演
[联] actor 演员, casting director 选角导演, film 电影, crowd 人群, performance 表演, crew 剧组
[记] 可数：an extra。background actor 更正式。featured extra 有短暂特写

**script supervisor**
英/美 /ˈskrɪpt ˌsuːpəvaɪzə(r)/
n. 场记
[义] A crew member who notes continuity details and logs each take during filming. 记录连贯性细节、登记每条镜头的剧组人员（ script supervisor notes 场记单， continuity supervisor 英式说法）。
[例] ① The script supervisor flagged a mismatched coffee cup between takes. 场记标出镜头间咖啡杯不一致。 ② Script supervisors help the editor with lined scripts. 场记用划线剧本协助剪辑。
[搭] script supervisor notes 场记单, script supervisor report 场记报告, film script supervisor 电影场记
[联] director 导演, film editor 电影剪辑师, actor 演员, film 电影, script 剧本, crew 剧组
[记] 可数：a script supervisor。亦说 continuity supervisor。场记单为剪辑关键文档

**stage manager**
英/美 /ˈsteɪdʒ ˌmænɪdʒə(r)/
n. 舞台监督
[义] The person who coordinates rehearsals and runs live theater performances backstage. 协调排练、在后台掌控现场戏剧演出的人（ assistant stage manager 助理舞台监督， ASM）。
[例] ① The stage manager called cues from the booth opening night. 首演夜舞台监督在控制间发提示。 ② Stage managers track props, actors, and timing every show. 舞台监督每场盯道具、演员与节奏。
[搭] assistant stage manager 助理舞台监督, stage manager cue 舞台监督 cue, theater stage manager 剧场舞台监督
[联] stagehand 舞台技工, actor 演员, director 导演, theater 戏剧, crew 剧组, performance 表演
[记] 可数：a stage manager。缩写 SM。戏剧现场「第三导演」

**stagehand**
英/美 /ˈsteɪdhænd/
n. 舞台技工
[义] A worker who moves scenery, lights, and equipment during theater or live events. 在戏剧或现场活动中搬运布景、灯光与设备的工作人员（ union stagehand 工会舞台工， theater stagehand 剧场技工）。
[例] ① Stagehands shifted the set during the fifteen-minute intermission. 舞台技工在十五分钟幕间搬景。 ② Stagehands work nights and weekends during the run. 演出季舞台工夜间周末上班。
[搭] union stagehand 工会舞台工, theater stagehand 剧场技工, stagehand crew 舞台班组
[联] stage manager 舞台监督, grip 场务, lighting technician 灯光技术员, theater 戏剧, crew 剧组, worker 工人
[记] 可数：a stagehand。stage + hand。影视 grip 职能类似

**lighting technician**
英/美 /ˈlaɪtɪŋ tekˈnɪʃn/
n. 灯光技术员
[义] A crew member who sets up and operates lights for theater, film, or concerts. 为戏剧、电影或演唱会架设、操作灯光的技术员（ theater lighting technician 剧场灯光师， touring lighting tech 巡演灯光）。
[例] ① The lighting technician programmed cues for the rock tour. 灯光技术员为摇滚巡演编灯光 cue。 ② Lighting technicians climb rigs under safety harnesses. 灯光师系安全吊带攀装置。
[搭] theater lighting technician 剧场灯光师, touring lighting tech 巡演灯光, lighting technician crew 灯光班组
[联] gaffer 灯光师, stagehand 舞台技工, grip 场务, technician 技术员, theater 戏剧, film 电影
[记] 可数：a lighting technician。lighting tech 口语。electrics department 电影灯光组

**visual effects artist**
英/美 /ˈvɪʒuəl ɪˈfekts ˌɑːtɪst/
n. 视觉特效师
[义] A digital artist who creates or composes visual effects for film, TV, or games. 为影视或游戏制作、合成视觉特效的数字艺术家（ VFX artist 缩写， senior VFX artist 资深特效师）。
[例] ① Visual effects artists replaced the green screen with a cityscape. 视觉特效师把绿幕换成城市天际线。 ② The VFX team worked months after principal photography. 特效团队在主拍摄结束后又工作数月。
[搭] senior VFX artist 资深特效师, visual effects studio 特效工作室, VFX supervisor 特效总监
[联] animator 动画师, film editor 电影剪辑师, designer 设计师, film 电影, creative 创意的, producer 制片人
[记] 可数：a visual effects artist。VFX = visual effects。SFX 常指现场物理特效

**film editor**
英/美 /ˈfɪlm ˌedɪtə(r)/
n. 电影剪辑师
[义] A person who selects and assembles footage into a finished film or episode. 挑选、组接素材制成成片或剧集的人（ assistant film editor 剪辑助理， picture editor 画面剪辑师）。
[例] ① The film editor cut the chase scene to under two minutes. 电影剪辑师把追逐戏剪到两分钟以内。 ② Film editors work with directors in the editing suite. 剪辑师与导演在剪辑室合作。
[搭] assistant film editor 剪辑助理, film editor cut 剪辑版, award-winning film editor 获奖剪辑师
[联] editor 编辑, director 导演, producer 制片人, script supervisor 场记, film 电影, post-production 后期
[记] 可数：a film editor。editor 条偏文字编辑；film editor 专指影像剪辑

**bureaucrat**
英/美 /ˈbjʊərəkræt/
n. 官僚；公务员（含贬义）
[义] An official in a government organization, often with a negative sense of rigid rule-following. 政府机构官员，常含僵化、繁文缛节贬义（ career bureaucrat 职业官僚， senior bureaucrat 高级官僚）。civil servant 更中性。
[例] ① Critics blamed bureaucrats for slow permit approvals. 批评者把许可审批慢归咎于官僚。 ② She spent twenty years as a bureaucrat in the trade ministry. 她在贸易部当公务员二十年。
[搭] career bureaucrat 职业官僚, senior bureaucrat 高级官僚, faceless bureaucrat 冷冰冰的官僚
[联] civil servant 公务员, government 政府, official 官员, policy 政策, politician 政治家, public sector 公共部门
[记] 可数：a bureaucrat。bureaucracy 名词；bureaucratic 形容词。贬义语境常见

**public servant**
英/美 /ˈpʌblɪk ˈsɜːvənt/
n. 公仆；公务人员
[义] A person employed by government to serve the public, often with a positive connotation. 受雇于政府、为公众服务的人，常含正面「公仆」意味（ dedicated public servant 尽职公仆）。美式常用；英式多 civil servant。
[例] ① She entered public service after law school. 法学院毕业后她进入公职。 ② Public servants must disclose conflicts of interest. 公务人员须披露利益冲突。
[搭] dedicated public servant 尽职公仆, career public servant 职业公务人员, public servant ethics 公务员伦理
[联] civil servant 公务员, government 政府, politician 政治家, policy 政策, employee 雇员, community 社区
[记] 可数：a public servant。public service 名词（公职；公共服务）

**policy analyst**
英/美 /ˈpɒləsi ˈænəlɪst/
n. 政策分析师
[义] A specialist who researches issues and drafts recommendations for government or think tanks. 研究议题、为政府或智库起草政策建议的专家（ senior policy analyst 高级政策分析师， health policy analyst 卫生政策分析师）。
[例] ① The policy analyst briefed the minister on housing reform. 政策分析师向部长简报住房改革。 ② Policy analysts review data before legislation is drafted. 政策分析师在立法起草前审阅数据。
[搭] senior policy analyst 高级政策分析师, health policy analyst 卫生政策分析师, policy analyst report 政策分析报告
[联] civil servant 公务员, politician 政治家, government 政府, researcher 研究员, analyst 分析师, policy 政策
[记] 可数：a policy analyst。think tank 智库常雇此职

**legislative aide**
英/美 /ˈledʒɪslətɪv eɪd/
n. 立法助理
[义] A staff member who supports elected legislators with research, correspondence, and scheduling. 协助民选议员做研究、文书与日程的工作人员（ congressional aide 国会助理， legislative assistant 同义）。
[例] ① The legislative aide drafted talking points for the senator. 立法助理为参议员起草谈话要点。 ② Legislative aides often work long hours during session. 会期立法助理常长时间工作。
[搭] congressional aide 国会助理, legislative aide job 立法助理岗位, senior legislative aide 资深立法助理
[联] senator 参议员, representative 众议员, politician 政治家, government 政府, policy 政策, assistant 助理
[记] 可数：a legislative aide。亦说 legislative assistant（LA）

**commissioner**
英/美 /kəˈmɪʃənə(r)/
n. 专员；委员；局长
[义] A senior official in charge of a government department or regulatory body. 负责政府部门或监管机构的高级官员（police commissioner 警察局长，election commissioner 选举专员，EU commissioner 欧盟委员）。
[例] ① The police commissioner addressed the union after the protest. 警察局长在抗议后向工会讲话。 ② Commissioners head agencies and set enforcement priorities. 专员领导机构并设定执法重点。
[搭] police commissioner 警察局长, election commissioner 选举专员, city commissioner 市委员, EU commissioner 欧盟委员
[联] civil servant 公务员, government 政府, official 官员, mayor 市长, agency 机构, bureaucrat 官僚
[记] 可数：a commissioner。sports commissioner 为联盟总裁——另一常见义

**minister**
英/美 /ˈmɪnɪstə(r)/
n. 部长；大臣
[义] A senior member of a government in charge of a department. 负责政府部门的高级官员（ prime minister 首相， foreign minister 外交部长， cabinet minister 内阁部长）。亦指「牧师」——另一义。
[例] ① The finance minister presented the budget to parliament. 财政部长向议会提交预算。 ② Ministers are appointed by the prime minister or president. 大臣由首相或总统任命。
[搭] prime minister 首相, foreign minister 外交部长, cabinet minister 内阁部长, government minister 政府部长
[联] civil servant 公务员, politician 政治家, government 政府, policy 政策, diplomat 外交官, ambassador 大使
[记] 可数：a minister。the Minister of … 英式头衔格式。

**governor**
英/美 /ˈɡʌvənə(r)/
n. 州长；总督
[义] The elected head of a US state or the appointed leader of a territory or central bank. 美国州民选首长，或领地、央行的任命首长（ state governor 州长， lieutenant governor 副州长）。
[例] ① The governor declared a state of emergency after the floods. 州长洪水后宣布紧急状态。 ② Governors appoint boards and sign state laws. 州长任命委员会并签署州法律。
[搭] state governor 州长, lieutenant governor 副州长, elected governor 民选州长, governor's office 州长办公室
[联] politician 政治家, mayor 市长, senator 参议员, government 政府, election 选举, representative 众议员
[记] 可数：a governor。英式 governor 亦指监狱长、学校理事——另一义

**senator**
英/美 /ˈsenətə(r)/
n. 参议员
[义] An elected member of a senate, especially the US Senate. 参议院民选议员，尤指美国参议员（ US senator 美国参议员， senior senator 资深参议员）。
[例] ① The senator introduced a bill on minimum wage. 参议员提交了最低工资法案。 ② Senators serve six-year terms in the US Senate. 美国参议员任期六年。
[搭] US senator 美国参议员, senior senator 资深参议员, senator election 参议员选举, senator office 参议员办公室
[联] representative 众议员, politician 政治家, legislator 立法者, government 政府, congress 国会, election 选举
[记] 可数：a senator。senate 名词（参议院）；senatorial 形容词

**representative**
英/美 /ˌreprɪˈzentətɪv/
n. 众议员；代表
adj. 代表性的
[义] n. An elected member of a lower house of legislature, especially the US House of Representatives. 议会下院民选议员，尤指美国众议员（ congressional representative 国会众议员）。亦指「代表」—— sales representative 等。
[例] ① The representative held a town hall on healthcare. 众议员召开医疗主题市民见面会。 ② Representatives serve two-year terms in the US House. 美国众议员任期两年。
[搭] congressional representative 国会众议员, House representative 众议院议员, elected representative 民选代表
[联] senator 参议员, politician 政治家, legislator 立法者, government 政府, election 选举, constituent 选民, representative 众议员 vs. customer service representative 客服：政治语境中 the representative 常指国会议员
[记] 可数：a representative。亦说 congressman/congresswoman。customer service representative 为另一词条

**councillor**
英/美 /ˈkaʊnsələ(r)/
n. 议员；市政委员
[义] An elected member of a city or county council. 市或县议会民选委员（ city councillor 市议员， county councillor 县议员）。美式拼写 councilor。
[例] ① The councillor voted against the zoning change. 议员投票反对分区调整。 ② Councillors debate local budgets and services. 市政委员辩论地方预算与服务。
[搭] city councillor 市议员, county councillor 县议员, elected councillor 民选议员, councillor meeting 议会会议
[联] mayor 市长, politician 政治家, government 政府, election 选举, local 地方的, civil servant 公务员
[记] 可数：a councillor。council 名词（议会；委员会）。councillor vs. counsellor（顾问）：拼写不同

**chief of staff**
英/美 /ˈtʃiːf əv ˈstɑːf/
n. 幕僚长；办公厅主任
[义] The senior aide who manages the office and schedule of a senior executive or elected official. 管理高官或民选官员办公室与日程的高级幕僚（ White House chief of staff 白宫幕僚长， military chief of staff 参谋长——军职）。
[例] ① The chief of staff controlled access to the mayor. 幕僚长把关谁能见市长。 ② Chiefs of staff coordinate policy teams and messaging. 办公厅主任协调政策团队与宣传口径。
[搭] White House chief of staff 白宫幕僚长, mayor's chief of staff 市长幕僚长, military chief of staff 参谋长
[联] politician 政治家, mayor 市长, assistant 助理, government 政府, executive 高管, civil servant 公务员
[记] 可数：a chief of staff。军队参谋长为另一体系，见 commander

**press secretary**
英/美 /ˈpres ˌsekətri/
n. 新闻秘书
[义] A spokesperson who handles media inquiries for a government official or organization. 为政府官员或机构应对媒体询问的发言人（ White House press secretary 白宫新闻秘书）。
[例] ① The press secretary read a statement at the daily briefing. 新闻秘书在每日简报会上宣读声明。 ② Press secretaries prepare talking points before interviews. 新闻秘书在采访前准备谈话要点。
[搭] White House press secretary 白宫新闻秘书, press secretary briefing 新闻秘书简报, government press secretary 政府新闻秘书
[联] spokesperson 发言人, journalist 记者, politician 政治家, media 媒体, government 政府, publicist 公关
[记] 可数：a press secretary。press briefing 新闻发布会

**spokesperson**
英/美 /ˈspəʊkspɜːsn/
n. 发言人
[义] A person chosen to speak officially for an organization or government. 被指定代表机构或政府正式发言的人（ government spokesperson 政府发言人， police spokesperson 警方发言人）。
[例] ① A spokesperson confirmed the layoffs after market close. 发言人收盘后确认了裁员。 ② Spokespersons stick to approved messages during crises. 危机中发言人坚持统一口径。
[搭] government spokesperson 政府发言人, official spokesperson 官方发言人, police spokesperson 警方发言人
[联] press secretary 新闻秘书, publicist 公关, journalist 记者, media 媒体, company 公司, government 政府
[记] 可数：a spokesperson。亦说 spokesman/spokeswoman；spokesperson 性别中性

**ombudsman**
英/美 /ˈɒmbʊdzmən/
n. 监察专员；申诉专员
[义] An official who investigates complaints against government agencies or companies. 调查对政府机构或企业投诉的官员（ consumer ombudsman 消费者申诉专员， parliamentary ombudsman 议会监察专员）。复数 ombudsmen。
[例] ① The ombudsman reviewed the citizen's complaint about delays. 监察专员审查了市民对延误的投诉。 ② Ombudsmen issue public reports without taking sides in court. 申诉专员发布中立报告，不介入诉讼。
[搭] parliamentary ombudsman 议会监察专员, consumer ombudsman 消费者申诉专员, ombudsman office 监察专员办公室
[联] civil servant 公务员, government 政府, official 官员, complaint 投诉, public 公众, regulator 监管者
[记] 可数：an ombudsman。源自瑞典语。ombudsperson 为性别中性变体

**foreign service officer**
英/美 /ˈfɒrən ˈsɜːvɪs ˌɒfɪsə(r)/
n. 外交官员
[义] A career diplomat employed by a country's foreign ministry. 外交部雇用的职业外交官（ US Foreign Service officer 美国外交人员， FSO 缩写）。
[例] ① The foreign service officer rotated to a new embassy post. 外交官员轮调至新使馆岗位。 ② FSOs pass rigorous exams and language training. 外交人员须通过严格考试与语言培训。
[搭] US Foreign Service officer 美国外交人员, foreign service exam 外交考试, career foreign service officer 职业外交官
[联] diplomat 外交官, ambassador 大使, consul 领事, government 政府, embassy 大使馆, civil servant 公务员
[记] 可数：a foreign service officer。缩写 FSO。the Foreign Service 外交人员体系（美国）

**consul**
英/美 /ˈkɒnsəl/
n. 领事
[义] A government official stationed abroad to assist citizens and promote trade. 派驻海外、协助本国公民并促进贸易的官员（ consul general 总领事， honorary consul 名誉领事）。
[例] ① The consul helped citizens renew passports during the crisis. 领事在危机中帮公民换护照。 ② Consuls work in consulates under ambassadors in capital cities. 领事在首都领馆工作，隶属大使。
[搭] consul general 总领事, honorary consul 名誉领事, consul office 领事馆, local consul 驻地领事
[联] diplomat 外交官, ambassador 大使, embassy 大使馆, government 政府, visa 签证, trade 贸易
[记] 可数：a consul。consulate 名词（领事馆）。consul 古罗马执政官为历史义

**attaché**
英/美 /əˈtæʃeɪ/
n. 使馆专员；武官
[义] A person assigned to an embassy with a specialist role, such as military or cultural affairs. 派驻使馆、负责军事或文化等专责事务的人员（ military attaché 武官， cultural attaché 文化专员）。法语借词，保留 accent。
[例] ① The military attaché attended the defense briefing. 武官出席国防简报会。 ② Attachés coordinate exchanges between host and home governments. 使馆专员协调东道国与本国政府交流。
[搭] military attaché 武官, cultural attaché 文化专员, commercial attaché 商务专员, embassy attaché 使馆专员
[联] diplomat 外交官, ambassador 大使, consul 领事, embassy 大使馆, officer 军官, government 政府
[记] 可数：an attaché。法语「附属、专员」。commercial attaché 促贸易

**town clerk**
英/美 /ˈtaʊn klɑːk/
n. 镇书记官
[义] An elected or appointed official who keeps municipal records and runs local elections. 保管市政档案、组织地方选举的官员（ city clerk 市书记官——较大城市）。clerk 条偏零售/文职。
[例] ① The town clerk certified the election results. 镇书记官认证了选举结果。 ② Town clerks issue marriage licenses and meeting minutes. 镇书记签发结婚证并整理会议纪要。
[搭] city clerk 市书记官, town clerk office 镇书记办公室, elected town clerk 民选镇书记
[联] mayor 市长, councillor 议员, election official 选举官员, civil servant 公务员, government 政府, clerk 职员
[记] 可数：a town clerk。新英格兰镇治理核心职位（美国）

**election official**
英/美 /ɪˈlekʃn əˈfɪʃl/
n. 选举官员
[义] A person who administers voting, counts ballots, or enforces election rules. 管理投票、计票或执行选举规则的人员（ local election official 地方选举官， poll worker 投票站工作人员）。
[例] ① Election officials trained poll workers before early voting. 提前投票前选举官员培训了投票站人员。 ② Election officials must remain nonpartisan during duty. 选举官执勤时须保持无党派立场。
[搭] local election official 地方选举官, election official training 选举官员培训, certified election official 认证选举官
[联] government 政府, mayor 市长, civil servant 公务员, vote 投票, public 公众, commissioner 专员
[记] 可数：an election official。poll worker 为投票日兼职岗位

**permanent secretary**
英/美 /ˈpɜːmənənt ˈsekrətri/
n. 常务秘书（英）
[义] The senior civil servant who heads a UK government department under the political minister. 英国政府部门中、在政治部长领导下的最高职业公务员（ Home Office permanent secretary 内政部常务秘书）。
[例] ① The permanent secretary briefed the new minister on department operations. 常务秘书向新部长简报部门运作。 ② Permanent secretaries stay in post when ministers change. 部长更替时常务秘书通常留任。
[搭] Home Office permanent secretary 内政部常务秘书, permanent secretary role 常务秘书职责, senior permanent secretary 高级常务秘书
[联] civil servant 公务员, minister 部长, bureaucrat 官僚, government 政府, official 官员, policy 政策
[记] 可数：a permanent secretary。英式文官体系核心职位；无美式完全对应

**inspector general**
英/美 /ɪnˈspektə ˈdʒenrəl/
n. 监察长
[义] An official who audits government agencies for waste, fraud, and abuse. 审计政府机构浪费、欺诈与滥权的官员（ IG 缩写， Inspector General of the Army 陆军监察长）。
[例] ① The inspector general launched an inquiry into contract fraud. 监察长对合同欺诈启动调查。 ② IG reports go to Congress and the public. 监察长报告提交国会并公开。
[搭] Inspector General report 监察长报告, army inspector general 陆军监察长, IG investigation 监察调查
[联] civil servant 公务员, auditor 审计师, government 政府, official 官员, fraud 欺诈, bureaucrat 官僚
[记] 可数：an inspector general。缩写 IG。联邦与军队均设此职

**undersecretary**
英/美 /ˈʌndəˌsekrətri/
n. 副部长；次长
[义] A senior civil servant or political appointee who assists a cabinet secretary or minister. 协助内阁部长工作的高级文官或政治任命官员（ Undersecretary of State 副国务卿， deputy minister 同义说法）。
[例] ① The undersecretary led the trade delegation in Geneva. 次长率贸易代表团赴日内瓦。 ② Undersecretaries oversee daily operations in large ministries. 副部长掌管大部日常运作。
[搭] Undersecretary of State 副国务卿, deputy undersecretary 助理次长, undersecretary role 副部长职责
[联] minister 部长, civil servant 公务员, diplomat 外交官, government 政府, official 官员, bureaucrat 官僚
[记] 可数：an undersecretary。美式 cabinet 体系常用。英式 permanent undersecretary 为文官高职

**collective bargaining**
英/美 /kəˌlektɪv ˈbɑːɡɪnɪŋ/
n. 集体谈判
[义] Negotiation between employers and a union representing workers over wages and conditions. 雇主与代表工人的工会就工资与劳动条件进行的谈判（collective bargaining agreement 集体谈判协议，right to collective bargaining 集体谈判权）。不可数。
[例] ① The union entered collective bargaining with management. 工会与管理层进入集体谈判。 ② Collective bargaining secured better overtime pay. 集体谈判争取到更好加班费。
[搭] collective bargaining agreement 集体谈判协议, right to collective bargaining 集体谈判权, collective bargaining rights 集体谈判权利, enter collective bargaining 进入集体谈判
[联] union 工会, strike 罢工, employer 雇主, employee 雇员, wage 工资, contract 合同, labor 劳动
[记] 不可数。bargain collectively 动词短语。CBA = collective bargaining agreement

**picketing**
英/美 /ˈpɪkɪtɪŋ/
n. 罢工纠察；示威纠察
[义] The act of standing outside a workplace to protest, often during a strike. 在工作场所外抗议，常伴随罢工（join the picket line 加入纠察线，picket line 纠察线）。不可数。
[例] ① Picketing continued for a third week outside the factory. 工厂外纠察进入第三周。 ② Police kept traffic moving past the picketing workers. 警方维持车辆通过纠察工人。
[搭] join the picket line 加入纠察线, picketing workers 纠察工人, legal picketing 合法纠察, ban on picketing 禁止纠察
[联] strike 罢工, union 工会, protest 抗议, worker 工人, employer 雇主, labor 劳动
[记] 不可数。picket 动词/名词；picket line 纠察线

**unionize**
英/美 /ˈjuːniənaɪz/
v. 组建工会；加入工会
[义] To form or join a labor union. 组建或加入工会（unionize a workplace 在工作场所组建工会，efforts to unionize 组建工会努力）。及物/不及物。
[形] unionize / unionized / unionized / unionizing
[例] ① Workers voted to unionize the warehouse. 工人投票决定仓库组建工会。 ② Tech companies have resisted efforts to unionize. 科技公司抵制组建工会努力。
[搭] unionize a workplace 在工作场所组建工会, efforts to unionize 组建工会努力, unionize workers 组织工人加入工会, right to unionize 组建工会权
[联] union 工会, collective bargaining 集体谈判, strike 罢工, employee 雇员, labor 劳动, organize 组织
[记] 及物/不及物。unionized 形容词（已工会化的）；unionization 名词

**grievance**
英/美 /ˈɡriːvəns/
n. 申诉；不满
[义] A formal complaint by an employee about treatment at work, or a general feeling of unfairness. 员工对工作待遇的正式投诉，或普遍的不公平感（file a grievance 提出申诉，grievance procedure 申诉程序）。可数。
[例] ① She filed a grievance over unpaid overtime. 她就未付加班费提出申诉。 ② The union handled member grievances through arbitration. 工会通过仲裁处理会员申诉。
[搭] file a grievance 提出申诉, grievance procedure 申诉程序, workplace grievance 职场申诉, air grievances 表达不满
[联] complaint 投诉, union 工会, employee 雇员, employer 雇主, arbitration 仲裁, unfair 不公平的
[记] 可数：a grievance。air one's grievances 固定短语（表达不满）

**sexual harassment**
英/美 /ˌsekʃuəl ˈhærəsmənt/
n. 性骚扰
[义] Unwelcome sexual behavior or comments at work or school that create a hostile environment. 工作场所或学校中不受欢迎的性行为或言论，造成敌对环境（report sexual harassment 举报性骚扰，sexual harassment training 性骚扰培训）。不可数。
[例] ① The company updated policies on sexual harassment. 公司更新性骚扰政策。 ② Victims of sexual harassment often fear retaliation. 性骚扰受害者常担心报复。
[搭] report sexual harassment 举报性骚扰, sexual harassment training 性骚扰培训, sexual harassment claim 性骚扰指控, workplace sexual harassment 职场性骚扰
[联] harassment 骚扰, workplace 职场, employee 雇员, complaint 投诉, law 法律, discrimination 歧视
[记] 不可数。harass 动词；harasser 骚扰者

**sick leave**
英/美 /sɪk liːv/
n. 病假
[义] Paid or unpaid time off from work when you are ill. 因病不上班的有薪或无薪假期（take sick leave 请病假，paid sick leave 带薪病假）。不可数。
[例] ① She used three days of sick leave for the flu. 她因流感请了三天病假。 ② The law mandates paid sick leave for employees. 法律要求雇主提供带薪病假。
[搭] take sick leave 请病假, paid sick leave 带薪病假, sick leave policy 病假政策, on sick leave 休病假
[联] leave 假期, vacation 休假, employee 雇员, illness 疾病, benefit 福利, PTO 带薪假
[记] 不可数。on sick leave 短语。与 personal leave、vacation 区分

**non-disclosure agreement**
英/美 /ˌnɒn dɪsˈkləʊʒə əˈɡriːmənt/
n. 保密协议
[义] A legal contract that prevents someone from sharing confidential information. 禁止分享机密信息的法律合同（sign an NDA 签署保密协议，NDA violation 违反保密协议）。可数。缩写 NDA。
[例] ① New hires must sign a non-disclosure agreement. 新员工须签保密协议。 ② The startup used an NDA before sharing product details. 创业公司在分享产品细节前要求签 NDA。
[搭] sign an NDA 签署保密协议, NDA violation 违反保密协议, non-disclosure agreement clause 保密条款, mutual NDA 双向保密协议
[联] contract 合同, confidential 机密的, employer 雇主, employee 雇员, legal 法律的, trade secret 商业秘密
[记] 可数：an NDA / a non-disclosure agreement。NDA 极常用缩写

**gig worker**
英/美 /ɡɪɡ ˈwɜːkə(r)/
n. 零工；平台零工
[义] A person who does short-term or freelance jobs, often through apps like ride-hailing or delivery platforms. 从事短期或自由职业工作的人，常通过打车、外卖等平台（gig economy worker 零工经济从业者，gig worker rights 零工权益）。可数。
[例] ① Gig workers lack traditional employee benefits. 零工缺少传统雇员福利。 ② Cities debated how to protect gig workers. 各城市争论如何保护零工。
[搭] gig economy worker 零工经济从业者, gig worker rights 零工权益, gig worker classification 零工身份认定, independent gig worker 独立零工
[联] freelance 自由职业, contractor 承包商, app 应用, delivery 配送, economy 经济, employment 就业
[记] 可数：a gig worker。gig economy 零工经济；gig 原义「单场演出」
